Conclusion: 该工作填补了视频生成中物体运动保真度评估的空白，提供了数据集与诊断评估方法，为运动缺陷的定位与理解提供有效基础。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in video generation models, they still exhibit obvious motion deficiencies, often manifested as incorrect object motion. However, most existing video quality evaluations focus on aesthetic quality or text-video alignment. To address this gap, we study object-centric motion fidelity assessment, evaluating target objects along object consistency, motion continuity, and physical plausibility. To achieve this, we first introduce VidMotion, a diagnostic dataset of 6,879 videos with designated moving objects and fine-grained annotations including dimension-wise scores and failure causes. We further propose MotionInsight, a diagnostic evaluator that shifts assessment from implicit RGB-frame observation to explicit motion-space diagnosis. By constructing motion-aware representations, MotionInsight makes subtle motion deficiencies more observable. We also introduce motion-specific rewards during GRPO to transform observed motion into a diagnostic assessment. Experiments demonstrate that MotionInsight provides an effective basis for diagnosing object motion deficiencies, producing human-aligned scores along three dimensions and grounded explanations.

</details>


### [102] [UniBuild: Unified Building Mapping From Multi-Source Optical Remote Sensing Imagery With Detail Decoding and Geometry Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37031)
*Wei Huang,Chenying Liu,Yilei Shi,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniBuild 统一建筑物提取框架，通过多数据集训练、HR-DPT 细节保持解码器与几何感知正则化，改善跨域泛化、边界锐度与相邻建筑分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学遥感影像建筑物提取方法往往依赖单一数据集，跨域泛化差；同时细节恢复不足、几何约束弱，导致边界模糊、形状不规则及相邻建筑粘连。

Method: 1) 构建多源异构 RGB 光学数据集上的统一多数据集训练方案，学习跨传感器、跨分辨率的可迁移建筑物表征；2) 设计 HR-DPT 细节保持解码器，融合高层语义与高分辨率空间特征；3) 引入几何感知正则化，包括基于结构张量的方向感知损失以保证边界方向一致性，以及鞍点感知损失以抑制低分辨率下狭窄建筑间隙中的虚假激活。

Result: 在 10 个公开高分辨率数据集和 2 个自采低分辨率数据集上训练与评估，UniBuild 在建筑物区域精度、边界锐度与相邻建筑分离方面稳定提升，并对未见域具有良好泛化能力，支持最高 10 m 分辨率的 RGB 光学遥感建筑物提取；预测掩膜经简单多边形化可转为 GIS 兼容建筑轮廓。

Conclusion: UniBuild 为多源 RGB 光学遥感影像提供了统一、可泛化的建筑物提取方案，兼顾细节恢复与几何规整，并具备实际 GIS 应用潜力；模型与推理代码已开源。

Abstract: Building extraction from optical remote sensing (RS) imagery is fundamental to urban mapping, yet existing methods are often dataset-specific and generalize poorly to unseen domains. Their practical use is also limited by insufficient detail recovery and weak geometric regularization, leading to blurred boundaries, irregular shapes, and merged adjacent buildings. To address these issues, we propose UniBuild, a unified building extraction framework for multi-source RGB optical RS imagery. First, a unified multi-dataset training scheme is constructed over heterogeneous RGB optical datasets to learn transferable building representations across sensors and resolutions. Second, a novel detail-preserving HR-DPT decoder is designed to integrate high-level semantic features with high-resolution spatial features, enhancing building detail recovery. Third, geometry-aware regularization is introduced through a structure-tensor-based direction-aware loss for boundary direction consistency and a saddle-aware loss for suppressing false activations in narrow inter-building gaps under low-resolution conditions. We train and evaluate UniBuild on multi-source RGB optical datasets, including 10 public high-resolution datasets and two self-collected low-resolution datasets. Experiments show that UniBuild consistently improves building-region accuracy, boundary sharpness, and adjacent-building separation across diverse datasets. It also generalizes well to unseen domains and supports practical building extraction from RGB optical RS imagery up to 10\,m resolution. The predicted masks can be further converted into GIS-compatible building footprints through simple polygonization. The trained model and inference code are released at https://github.com/zhu-xlab/UniBuild.

</details>


### [103] [GleanVID: Complementary Token Selection for Efficient Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37042)
*Shuo Yang,Changbai Li,Rui Tang,Xinyu Zhao,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GleanVID：一种免训练的视频大模型推理加速框架，将视频token选择视为渐进式证据累积过程，联合考虑帧间新颖性、局部代表性与子空间互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大模型理解能力强，但视觉token数量庞大导致推理开销高。现有token压缩方法多依赖与选择无关的评分，忽视跨帧互补性，导致跨帧保留冗余证据。

Method: 提出GleanVID免训练推理加速框架：先根据时间新颖性在帧间分配全局token预算，再联合考虑局部代表性与子空间互补性进行token选择，从而保留更丰富、更少冗余的视觉证据。

Result: 在多种VideoLLM和基准上持续达到SOTA。仅用25%视觉token时，保留Qwen3-VL原始性能的98.6%，prefill延迟降低44.7%；在LLaVA-OV-7B上25%保留率甚至略超原始模型。

Conclusion: 将视频token选择建模为渐进式证据累积过程，可有效兼顾信息性与互补性，在有限token预算下实现高效且高性能的视频理解推理。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) have achieved strong video understanding capabilities but incur substantial inference overhead due to the large number of visual tokens. Existing VideoLLM token compression methods largely rely on selection-independent scoring, overlooking cross-frame complementarity and consequently retaining redundant evidence across frames. Instead, we view video token selection as a progressive evidence accumulation process. It aims to retain visual evidence that is individually informative and collectively complementary under a limited token budget. Building on this insight, we introduce GleanVID, a training-free inference acceleration framework for VideoLLMs. Specifically, GleanVID first allocates the global token budget across frames according to temporal novelty and then selects tokens by jointly considering local representativeness and subspace complementarity, thereby preserving richer and less redundant visual evidence. Extensive experiments across diverse VideoLLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that GleanVID consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, with only 25% of visual tokens, GleanVID preserves 98.6% of Qwen3-VL's original performance while reducing its prefill latency by 44.7%. On LLaVA-OV-7B, GleanVID at a 25% retention ratio even slightly surpasses the original model.

</details>


### [104] [Speed in the Blind Spot: An Interpretability Analysis of Dynamic Perception in VLMs for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37046)
*Katharina Winter,Stefan Englmeier,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文以速度理解作为诊断任务，系统评估了视觉语言模型（VLM）从视觉输入中恢复动态物理状态的能力，发现模型存在"内部可解码但难以言语化"的关键短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型日益应用于自动驾驶系统，但其从视觉输入中恢复动态物理状态的能力尚未得到充分表征。作者希望揭示这类模型在速度理解上的真实能力边界，而非仅凭规划输出的合理性来判断其可靠性。

Method: 在nuScenes数据集上，围绕三类任务（周围车辆速度、当前自车速度、短期未来自车速度预测）设计受控诊断实验。评估开放权重通用VLM、PhysicalAI VLM以及面向驾驶的Alpamayo-1.5视觉-语言-动作模型，采用多种输入输出形式。方法结合言语评估、时间扰动、反事实自车速度提示与隐藏表示的线性探针，并在非优化QLoRA下进行任务特定适配。

Result: 三类任务呈现不同失败模式：周围车辆速度以智能体特定形式被弱编码；当前自车速度常可内部访问但言语化较差，连续探针MAE为4.7-5.8 km/h，而言语输出MAE达10.2-16.8 km/h。多帧相比单帧言语收益不一致，帧序几乎未被利用。QLoRA任务适配可改善任务相关潜在速度表示与言语读出，但连续周围车辆速度估计仍弱，且多数未来速度增益在帧打乱后仍存留，表明时间基础有限。Alpamayo-1.5在周围车辆与未来自车速度上潜在表示更强，但当前自车速度可解码性相当，探针与言语差距仍显著。

Conclusion: 驾驶专业化可增强运动表示，但不能保证在场景状态与自车状态上都有更强编码，也不能保证可靠读出。研究结果表明，看似合理的规划输出并不必然意味着模型能可靠恢复或具备对底层动态状态的时间基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are increasingly used in autonomous-driving systems, yet their ability to recover dynamic physical state from visual input remains insufficiently characterized. We study velocity understanding as a controlled diagnostic across three tasks: surrounding-agent speed, current ego speed, and short-horizon future ego-speed proposal. On nuScenes, we evaluate open-weight general-purpose and PhysicalAI VLMs, together with the driving-oriented Alpamayo-1.5 Vision-Language-Action model, using multiple input and output formulations. We combine verbal evaluation with temporal perturbations, counterfactual ego-speed hints and linear probes of hidden representations. The tasks exhibit distinct failure modes. Surrounding-agent speed is weakly encoded in an agent-specific form, whereas current ego speed is often internally accessible but poorly verbalized: continuous probes achieve 4.7-5.8 km/h MAE compared with 10.2-16.8 km/h MAE for verbal outputs. Multiple frames provide inconsistent verbal gains to single frame inputs, and frame order is rarely exploited. Under non-optimized QLoRA, task-specific adaptation improves both task-relevant latent speed representations and verbal readout, but continuous surrounding-agent speed estimation remains weak, while most future-speed gains survive frame shuffling, indicating limited temporal grounding. Driving specialized Alpamayo-1.5 shows stronger latent representations for surrounding-agent and future ego speed, while current ego-speed decodability is comparable and substantial probe-verbal gaps remain. Thus, driving specialization can strengthen motion representations but does not guarantee stronger encoding across both scene and ego states or reliable readout. The results show that plausible planning outputs do not necessarily imply reliable recovery or temporal grounding of the underlying dynamic state.

</details>


### [105] [NHO: A Neural Hamiltonian Operator for Anchor-based Region Localization and Dense Correspondance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37048)
*Jing Li,Yawei Luo,Xiangze Meng,Ying Li,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 NHO 方法，通过神经哈密顿算子学习实现稀疏锚点下的非刚性部分到完整形状对应，将区域定位与稠密对应统一到局部化特征空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏锚点下的部分到完整形状匹配存在空间歧义：既要确定部分形状对应完整表面的哪个区域，又要恢复稠密对应关系。稀疏锚点信息不足以同时解决这两个问题，需要引入内在几何信息来消歧。

Method: 将稀疏锚点与部分形状的内在几何结合，学习一个神经哈密顿算子，其局部化特征空间同时编码区域支撑与内在坐标。哈密顿势能参数化为内在神经场，并用锚点证据、谱约束和几何约束联合优化。针对稀疏锚点带来的空间歧义，引入算子估计与对应恢复之间的相互细化：每轮中当前特征空间提供谱坐标并限制匹配到其诱导支撑区域，可靠对应反过来更新势能。迭代后聚合特征函数能量给出最终定位，恢复的映射用于初始化稠密对应细化。

Result: 在区域定位与稠密对应两个任务上均取得有竞争力的精度，并对均匀缩放和旋转具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将部分形状定位与稠密对应统一在由神经哈密顿算子定义的局部化特征空间中，是一种可行且有效的方案，相互细化机制能缓解稀疏锚点的空间歧义。

Abstract: Non-rigid partial-to-full shape correspondence from sparse anchors requires identifying the corresponding region on the full surface and recovering dense correspondences between the partial shape and that region. We present NHO, which combines sparse anchors with the intrinsic geometry of the partial shape to learn a neural Hamiltonian operator whose localized eigenspace encodes both the region support and intrinsic coordinates for dense correspondence. NHO parameterizes the Hamiltonian potential as an intrinsic neural field and optimizes it using anchor evidence together with spectral and geometric constraints. To resolve the spatial ambiguity left by sparse anchors, we introduce reciprocal refinement between operator estimation and correspondence recovery. At each round, the current eigenspace provides spectral coordinates and restricts matching to its induced support, while geometrically reliable correspondences provide additional evidence for updating the potential. After refinement, aggregated eigenfunction energy yields the final localization, and the recovered map initializes dense correspondence refinement. Experiments demonstrate competitive accuracy on both tasks and robustness to uniform scaling and rotation.

</details>


### [106] [OmniRoute: Mapping Temporal Semantic Evidence to Audio-Visual Token Budgets for Efficient Omnimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37052)
*Yuchen Deng,Zidang Cai,Feidiao Yang,Yufei Wang,Jie Wang,Hai-Tao Zheng,Yuxing Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的OmniRoute两阶段压缩框架，通过对齐音视频语义相关性的时间变化来降低Omni-LLM长音视频token序列的预填充开销，在四个基准上取得效率与性能的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni-LLM将音视频流编码为时间交错的token序列进行多模态推理，但长音视频token序列带来巨大的预填充成本。现有压缩方法虽有进展，却常忽略音视频语义相关性随时间变化的特性以及局部连续性。

Method: OmniRoute为免训练的两阶段框架。第一阶段TEGB（时间证据引导预算分配）依据语义相关性与局部内容变化，推导分块的模态偏好与初始主导模态预算；第二阶段BCSC（预算约束语义压缩）先压缩主导模态，再依据实际保留比例校准跟随模态的保留目标。视频侧结合时空分组与查询引导选择；音频侧基于编码器注意力与查询相关性选token，并在视觉引导下将残余token合并为上下文锚点。

Result: 在四个代表性基准上的实验表明，相比竞争性基线，方法在推理效率与性能之间取得更好的权衡。代码与接口将开源以促进后续研究。

Conclusion: 利用音视频语义相关性的时间变化与局部连续性进行免训练的两阶段预算分配与语义压缩，可有效降低Omni-LLM的预填充开销并保持性能。

Abstract: Omnimodal large language models (Omni-LLMs) encode audio and visual streams into temporally interleaved token sequences for multimodal reasoning. However, processing long audio-visual token sequences incurs substantial prefill costs. Existing compression methods have made progress, but often overlook temporal changes in audio-visual semantic relevance. Motivated by temporal variation and local continuity, we propose OmniRoute, a training-free, two-stage compression framework. First, Temporal Evidence-Guided Budgeting (TEGB) derives chunk-wise modality preferences and initial leading-modality budgets from semantic relevance and local content variation. Second, Budget-Constrained Semantic Compression (BCSC) compresses the leading modality and then calibrates the follower's retention target using the actual retained fraction. For video, it combines spatiotemporal grouping with query-guided selection; for audio, it selects tokens based on encoder attention and query relevance, then merges residual tokens into context anchors under visual guidance. Experiments on four representative benchmarks demonstrate a better trade-off between inference efficiency and performance than competitive baselines. The code and interface will be released to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [107] [Spatial-OPSD: Self-Improving Spatial Reasoning via Label-Free Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37055)
*Zhenyu Liu,Zhangquan Chen,Keyi Chen,Mingze Sun,Xiang An,Haodong Jing,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial-OPSD，一种无需标签的自我改进框架，利用感知/重建工具提供的空间先验，通过特权教师模型对学生进行 token 级密集监督，并采用轮次递归训练实现多轮自我提升，在五个空间推理基准上取得开源领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在具身与空间场景中需准确理解深度、视角与三维关系，但现有空间推理能力提升方法普遍依赖真实答案标签、基于答案的奖励或其他任务特定监督，成本高且难以扩展，因此需要一种无需标注的自我改进方案。

Method: 提出 Spatial-OPSD 无标签自我改进框架：训练时特权教师接收自动可得的空间先验（深度、重建的 3D 关系、相机几何等），学生仅见原始视觉-语言输入；在学生自采样轨迹上，教师提供密集 token 级监督，使学生内化空间知识，且推理时无需真实答案或特权信息。进一步采用轮次递归训练：每轮内教师冻结以提供稳定目标，改进后的学生作为下一轮教师与学生的初始化，由空间先验重新建立信息不对称，从而支持多轮重复自我改进并避免教师快速漂移。

Result: 在四个 VLM 系列上，单轮 Spatial-OPSD 即可稳定提升五基准平均分；三轮训练将强空间专精模型推向开源前沿，在开源模型中取得最高平均分，并在五个空间推理基准中的三个上取得最优结果。

Conclusion: Spatial-OPSD 表明，无需标签、仅借助自动空间先验的特权教师监督与轮次递归训练，可有效且可重复地提升 VLM 的空间推理能力，具备良好的可扩展性与实用价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly operate in embodied and spatially grounded settings, where accurate understanding of depth, viewpoint, and three-dimensional relations is essential. However, improving spatial reasoning typically relies on ground-truth answers, answer-derived rewards, or other forms of task-specific supervision. We introduce Spatial-OPSD, a label-free self-improvement framework that instead exploits spatial structure naturally available from perception and reconstruction tools. During training, a privileged teacher receives automatically obtainable spatial priors, such as depth, reconstructed 3D relations, and camera geometry, while the student observes only the original visual-language input. On trajectories sampled by the student itself, the teacher provides dense token-level supervision, allowing the student to internalize spatial knowledge without ground-truth answer labels or privileged information at inference time.
  To extend this supervision beyond a single round, we adopt a round-wise recursive training scheme: the teacher remains frozen within each round to provide a stable learning target, and the improved student initializes both teacher and student in the next round, where privileged spatial priors re-establish an informative teacher--student asymmetry. This enables repeated self-improvement while avoiding a rapidly moving teacher during optimization. Across four VLM families, a single round of Spatial-OPSD consistently improves the five-benchmark average, while three rounds further push a strong spatially specialized model to the open-source frontier, achieving the highest average among the open models and the best results on three of five spatial reasoning benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/vermouth599/Spatial-OPSD.

</details>


### [108] [Context without Commitment: Robust Dense Correspondence under Non-Rigid Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37071)
*Yuzhen He,Sara Homscheid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CoCo-Reg，用区域块丰富稠密点特征但不限制最终点级搜索，在非刚性点云配准中大幅降低对应误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非刚性点云配准需为形变源表面每个点找到目标点。点级匹配保留完整目标云，但局部几何相似时对应关系模糊；区域或由粗到细方法提供更广空间上下文，却可能因区域匹配错误在稠密匹配前排除正确对应。

Method: CoCo-Reg 构建最远点采样的区域块，在源与目标内部及之间交换几何信息，用经身份校正的点重叠度监督块相似性，并将区域信息投影回稠密点特征；最终配准阶段仍对完整目标云打分后再进行全局点级候选选择。

Result: 在 ModelNet10 的 726 个留出对象、九个形变水平上，两个学习型基线平均对应误差为 0.1993 和 0.1921，CoCo-Reg 为 0.0547，相对点级基线降低 72.6%；在 92.3% 的配对测试对象上误差更低，误差大于 0.1 的点比例均值从 47.3% 降至 17.3%；Chamfer 距离与 HD95 同向下降，且在各形变水平上均保持更低。

Conclusion: 结果支持在稠密非刚性对应中使用区域上下文，同时不对最终搜索施加硬性块级限制。由于评估每种方法仅用一个检查点，所报告增益刻画的是完整系统而非单个组件的独立因果贡献；代码将公开。

Abstract: Non-rigid point-cloud registration aims to find the corresponding target point for each point on a deforming source surface. Point-level matching keeps the full target cloud available, but correspondence becomes ambiguous when different regions have similar local geometry. Regional or coarse-to-fine methods provide broader spatial context, but an incorrect regional match can exclude the correct correspondence before dense matching. We propose CoCo-Reg, which uses regional patches to enrich dense point features without allowing patch predictions to restrict the final point-level search. CoCo-Reg constructs farthest-point-sampled patches, exchanges geometric information within and between source and target, supervises patch similarity using identity-corrected point overlap, and projects the resulting regional information back to dense point features. The final registration stage still scores the full target cloud before global point-level candidate selection. On 726 held-out ModelNet10 objects across nine deformation levels, two established learning-based baselines obtain mean correspondence errors of 0.1993 and 0.1921, whereas CoCo-Reg obtains 0.0547. Relative to its point-level baseline, this is a 72.6\% reduction. CoCo-Reg achieves lower correspondence error on 92.3\% of paired test objects and reduces the mean fraction of points with error above 0.1 from 47.3\% to 17.3\%. Chamfer distance and HD95 decrease in the same direction, and CoCo-Reg remains lower across all tested deformation levels. These results support using regional context for dense non-rigid correspondence without imposing a hard patch-level restriction on the final search. Because evaluation uses one checkpoint per method, the reported gains characterize the complete systems rather than the isolated causal contribution of an individual component. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [109] [LDM-is-AE: Latent Diffusion Model is an Auto-Encoder for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37080)
*Zhengqiang Zhang,Lingchen Sun,Rongyuan Wu,Qiaosi Yi,Xiangtao Kong,Chaodong Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LDM-is-AE，一种端到端单阶段LDM训练框架，无需单独训练tokenizer，让扩散模型自身充当自编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LDM采用两阶段流水线：先预训练自编码器定义隐空间，再在其上训练扩散模型。这种设计引入了表示不匹配，因为隐空间是为重建而非适应去噪动态而优化的。

Method: 作者发现LDM本身就是一种自编码器，其在每个去噪步实际执行隐空间→特征→隐空间的变换，可视为内部解码-编码过程。据此将DiT主干拆分为互为对偶的DiT-E（编码）与DiT-D（解码），并在所有时间步对中间特征施加图像空间监督，建立显式的隐空间→图像→隐空间路径；在零噪声时间步还执行图像→隐空间→图像映射，对应自编码过程。

Result: LDM-is-AE以端到端方式联合学习隐表示与去噪动态，形成适配生成过程的扩散原生隐空间。在256x256和512x512类条件图像生成任务上分别取得FID 1.80和1.90，生成性能极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过消除两阶段设计中的表示不匹配，LDM-is-AE在无需独立tokenizer的情况下实现了有竞争力的生成效果，为扩散原生隐空间学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) typically adopt a two-stage pipeline: an auto-encoder (AE) is first pre-trained to define a latent space, then a diffusion model is trained to perform denoising within it. Such a two-stage design introduces a representation mismatch, as the latent space is optimized for reconstruction rather than adapting the denoising dynamics. We reveal that the LDM itself is an AE, and consequently present LDM-is-AE, an end-to-end one-stage LDM training framework that eliminates the need for a separately trained tokenizer. Our key observation is that the LDM backbone actually performs a latent-to-feature-to-latent transformation at each denoising step, which can be interpreted as an internal decoding--encoding process. Leveraging this structure, we split the DiT backbone into two reciprocal components, DiT-E (i.e., DiT Encoding) and DiT-D (i.e., DiT Decoding), and impose image-space supervision on the intermediate features across all timesteps. Our model encourages the internal representation to align with the image domain throughout denoising, thereby establishing an explicit latent-to-image-to-latent path. At the zero-noise timestep, our model further performs an image-to-latent-to-image mapping, corresponding to an auto-encoding process. As a result, LDM-is-AE jointly learns latent representations and denoising dynamics in an end-to-end manner, yielding a diffusion-native latent space tailored to the generation process. Experiments demonstrate that LDM-is-AE exhibits highly competitive generation performance, achieving an FID of 1.80 and 1.90 on 256x256 and 512x512 class-conditional image generation, respectively.

</details>


### [110] [Real2Gym: Building Gyms from Videos, Bringing Skills to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37089)
*Kerui Ren,Yingxiang Xu,Kaiwen Song,Linning Xu,Bo Dai,Mulin Yu,Tao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Real2Gym 是一个 Real2Sim2Real 智能体框架，将人类与机器人演示视频转化为可交互的仿真环境，并把仿真中学到的技能迁移到真实机器人上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界视频富含操作演示，但将其转化为可复用机器人技能需要视觉对齐的环境、可执行的物理交互以及从经验中学习的机制，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 框架包含 Real2Sim 模块：重建可编辑场景、对齐物体与相机、通过原生物理执行验证演示或重定向动作、生成带可行动性检查的任务条件变体；智能体在环境中生成操作阶段的可执行代码、观察结果，并将成功与失败经验蒸馏为可复用任务流程、物体相对运动和恢复策略；通过统一的感知与控制接口指导仿真及真实机器人执行，动作依据当前观测自适应调整，且不更新模型权重。

Result: 在仿真环境重建中实现高保真，成功率比 GPT-6 Astra Direct Mode 高出 16.7%，同时策略执行 token 减少约 74.9%；在真实 Franka 机器人上四项任务的物理执行成功率高出 33.3%。

Conclusion: Real2Gym 有效打通了从真实演示到仿真环境再到真实机器人的技能迁移路径，在仿真与真实执行中均显著优于对比方法，并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Real-world videos provide rich demonstrations of manipulation, but turning them into reusable robot skills requires visually aligned environments, executable physical interactions, and mechanisms for learning from experience. We introduce Real2Gym, an agentic Real2Sim2Real framework that turns human and robot demonstrations into interactive simulation gyms and brings skills acquired in simulation to physical robots. The Real2Sim module reconstructs editable scenes, aligns objects and cameras with the input, validates demonstrated or retargeted actions through native physics execution, and generates task-conditioned variations with action-feasibility checks. Within these environments, the agent generates executable code for manipulation stages, observes their outcomes, and distills successful attempts and failures into reusable task procedures, object-relative motions, and recovery strategies. Through a shared perception-and-control interface, these skills guide subsequent execution in simulation and on real robots, with motions adapted to current observations and no updates to the underlying model weights. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Real2Gym enables high-fidelity simulation environment reconstruction, outperforming GPT-6 Astra Direct Mode by 16.7% in success rate with approximately 74.9% fewer policy-execution tokens across these environments, while exceeding it by 33.3% in physical robot execution success rate across four tasks on a real Franka robot.

</details>


### [111] [Task-Oriented Visual Feature Compression via Residual Vector Quantization for Device-Edge Multimodal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37090)
*Luning Pang,Cheng Yuan,Jiawei Shao,Mingtao Huang,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出查询引导的任务导向特征压缩Q-TOFC，用RVQ离散编码、查询相关性聚合和量化误差补偿适配器，在七个多模态基准上相比TOFC减少53.6%视觉载荷并保持相当性能，带宽受限时端到端延迟更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型多模态推理难以完全在资源受限设备上运行，设备-边缘协同推理虽有帮助，但带宽受限的上行链路传输视觉数据会带来显著延迟。现有任务导向特征压缩（TOFC）通过特征聚合与熵编码减少载荷，但连续特征编码开销仍高，且与查询无关的聚合会丢失任务相关的局部证据。

Method: 提出查询引导的任务导向特征压缩（Q-TOFC）：1）使用残差向量量化（RVQ）将每个融合特征编码为紧凑的码本索引序列，降低表示成本并允许传输更多特征；2）在特征聚合中引入查询相关性；3）使用量化误差补偿适配器缓解离散量化带来的失真。

Result: 在七个多模态基准上的实验表明，Q-TOFC相比TOFC将视觉载荷减少53.6%，同时保持相当的平均归一化任务性能；端到端延迟评估显示在带宽受限上行链路下延迟更低。

Conclusion: Q-TOFC通过查询引导的离散特征压缩与误差补偿，有效降低设备-边缘多模态推理的传输载荷与延迟，在带宽受限场景下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) support diverse visual understanding and reasoning tasks but are often impractical to run entirely on resource-constrained devices. Device-edge co-inference reduces device computation, yet transmitting visual data over bandwidth-limited uplinks can introduce substantial delay. Task-oriented feature compression (TOFC) reduces the payload through feature aggregation and entropy coding. However, continuous-feature coding remains costly, and query-agnostic aggregation may discard task-relevant local evidence. We propose query-guided task-oriented feature compression (Q-TOFC) for device-edge multimodal inference. Q-TOFC employs residual vector quantization (RVQ) to encode each merged feature as a compact sequence of codebook indices, reducing its representation cost and allowing more features to be transmitted. It further incorporates query relevance into feature aggregation and uses a quantization error compensation adapter to mitigate the distortion introduced by discrete quantization. Experiments on seven multimodal benchmarks show that Q-TOFC reduces the visual payload by 53.6% relative to TOFC while maintaining comparable average normalized task performance. End-to-end latency evaluations further demonstrate lower latency under bandwidth-constrained uplinks.

</details>


### [112] [Why MLLMs Struggle to Count: Overcoming Individuation and Aggregation Bottlenecks with ConvStack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37096)
*Liwei Che,Yihao Quan,Sen Fang,Hongyi Wang,Ranjay Krishna,Ruixiang Tang,Vladimir Pavlovic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多模态大模型细粒度视觉计数失败进行机制分析，揭示"个体化瓶颈"与"聚合崩塌"两个瓶颈，并提出轻量架构ConvStack予以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉计数任务上持续表现不佳，但其内在原因尚缺乏清晰解释，作者希望从机制层面揭示失败根源并加以改进。

Method: 通过机制分析定位两个瓶颈：一是图像分块导致的个体化瓶颈，ViT独立处理patch，难以跨边界将碎片化几何特征组合为独立对象表示；二是计数聚合过程中的表示崩塌，随数量增加，注意力压缩使表示分离度迅速下降。针对此，提出ConvStack，在视觉token空间中直接操作，通过零初始化残差连接显式聚合与注入局部空间结构。

Result: 仅通过在计数任务上微调，模型在密集物体计数及更广泛的空间理解基准上取得显著提升，同时不损害通用视觉能力。

Conclusion: 显式解决个体化瓶颈可为下游聚合提供明确几何证据，ConvStack在提升计数与空间理解能力的同时保持通用性能，验证了机制分析的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) consistently struggle with fine-grained visual counting, yet the underlying causes remain poorly understood. In this work, we present a mechanistic analysis of this failure mode, identifying two critical bottlenecks inherent to the global attention pipeline of MLLMs. First, we reveal an individuation bottleneck stemming from image patchification: because Vision Transformers process patches independently, they struggle to group fragmented geometric features across boundaries into distinct object representations. Second, we identify a collapse in the subsequent counting aggregation process, where representation separation rapidly diminishes as numerosity increases due to attention compression. Identifying and formalizing these twin bottlenecks constitutes our first major contribution. To overcome them, we propose ConvStack, a lightweight architecture that operates directly in the visual token space to explicitly aggregate and inject local spatial structures via zero-initialized residual connections. By explicitly addressing the individuation bottleneck, ConvStack provides unambiguous geometric evidence for downstream aggregation. Remarkably, by fine-tuning exclusively on counting tasks, the model achieves substantial improvements in dense object counting and broader spatial understanding benchmarks, without compromising on general visual capabilities.

</details>


### [113] [Waypoint-1.5: A Real-Time Video World Model for Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37107)
*Rajit Rajpal,Shahbuland Matiana,Liew Wei Pyn,Anmol Agarwal,Ryan Craig,Andrew Lapp,Mithun Hunsur,Sami BuGhanem,Scottie Fox,Aaron Sanders Carson Poole,Irene Park,Dave Rossi,Spencer Frazier,Louis Castricato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Waypoint 1.5 是一个面向消费级硬件的实时扩散世界模型，可在数百款游戏数据上预训练，根据键盘鼠标输入生成可玩视频，并强调延迟与吞吐的交互性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需在严格延迟与吞吐约束下响应用户密集控制，这与通用视频扩散模型的目标不同，因此需要专门的模型、数据与运行时设计。

Method: 在 10 万小时、控制对齐的多元游戏视频数据上预训练；条件于完整键盘与鼠标输入；包含两种分辨率版本以适配不同消费级硬件；并介绍数据管线、架构、训练方法与运行时系统。

Result: 模型可在消费级硬件上实时生成可玩视频，覆盖多种硬件；论文区分渲染 FPS、潜空间 FPS 与控制率，并通过延迟和吞吐评估交互性。

Conclusion: Waypoint 1.5 给出了面向交互式视频生成的实时扩散世界模型方案，并讨论了该领域特有的安全与伦理问题。

Abstract: We present Waypoint 1.5, a real-time diffusion world model for interactive video generation on consumer-grade hardware. Unlike general video diffusion models, interactive world models (iWMs) must respond to dense user controls under strict latency and throughput constraints. Waypoint 1.5 is pre-trained on 100,000 hours of diverse, control-aligned video game data across hundreds of games, and generates playable video conditioned on full keyboard and mouse input. The model includes two resolution variants that run across a wide spectrum of consumer hardware. To characterize this unique setting, we distinguish rendered FPS, latent FPS, and control rate. We describe the data pipeline, architecture, training methodology, and runtime system behind Waypoint 1.5. We evaluate interactivity through latency and throughput. Finally, we discuss the safety and ethics considerations unique to iWMs.

</details>


### [114] [NRF-GS: Neural Residual Fields for Expressive and Compact Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37115)
*Pratik Singh Bisht,Andreas Kolb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NRF-GS，用共享的神经残差场替代每个高斯的低阶球谐基，增强视图相关反射建模，在减少最多50%高斯数量的同时保持或提升渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）使用低阶球谐（SH）来建模视图相关反射，其表达能力有限，难以建模高频方向性效果。为了补偿这一不足，通常需要增加高斯数量，导致表示冗余和几何冗余。作者希望从外观建模角度解决这一问题。

Method: 提出 NRF-GS（Neural Residual Fields for Gaussian Splatting）混合表示。将每个高斯的SH基替换为一个共享的神经残差场：每个高斯仅编码紧凑的外观特征和朗伯基色，由一个轻量的全局场景级MLP基于观察方向、距离和每个高斯的特征来预测视图相关残差。该方式结合了每个高斯的漫反射表示与共享全局函数来表达高频细节，实现更强的表达能力与跨高斯的参数共享。

Result: NRF-GS 在减少最多50%高斯数量的情况下，达到了与基线相当或更好的渲染质量，并在镜面反射和高频细节上产生明显改进。

Conclusion: 准确捕捉高频方向性反射（尤其是镜面区域）可使GS表示更具表现力，从而减少对几何冗余高斯的依赖。用共享神经残差场替代逐高斯SH基是提升3DGS外观建模效率的有效途径。

Abstract: We revisit the role of appearance modeling in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and show that limited expressiveness in view-dependent reflectance is a key driver of representation redundancy. In standard 3DGS, low-order spherical harmonics (SH) are used, restricting the splats' ability to model high-frequency directional effects, which is typically compensated by increasing the number of splats. We propose \emph{NRF-GS: Neural Residual Fields for Gaussian Splatting}, a hybrid representation that replaces per-splat SH-bases with a shared neural residual field. Each Gaussian encodes a compact set of appearance features and a lambertian base color, while a lightweight \emph{global scene-level MLP} predicts view-dependent residuals conditioned on viewing direction, distance, and per-splat features. This formulation enhances directional reflectance modeling by combining diffuse per-splat reflectance representations with a shared global function for high-frequency details, enabling both higher expressiveness and parameter sharing across splats. Our key insight is that by accurately capturing high-frequency directional reflectance, especially in specular regions, the GS-representation becomes more expressive, reducing the need for geometrically redundant splats. As a result, NRF-GS achieves comparable or better rendering quality while reducing the number of Gaussians by up to 50\%, and produces visibly improved specular and high-frequency details.

</details>


### [115] [EviViT: Evidence-Adaptive Vision Transformers for Fine-Grained Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37123)
*Yaoxin Niu,Zhangquan Chen,Yang Zhang,Xiang An,Zhumei Wang,Chih-Ting Liao,Hongkun Cao,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviViT 是一种轻量级附件，让预训练视觉Transformer学会"去哪里"获取细节，通过人类视觉搜索轨迹监督生成问题条件证据密度图，引导区域重读与视觉token分配，从而在细粒度视觉感知任务中既不浪费token又不丢失上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率场景下，整图高分辨率处理会把视觉token浪费在无关内容上；而孤立裁剪又会丢失理解所选证据所需的上下文。因此需要在"全局处理"与"局部裁剪"之间找到平衡，有选择地、有上下文地获取细节。

Method: 提出EviViT：1）用人类视觉搜索轨迹监督学习一个问题条件的证据密度图，决定模型该在哪里获取细节；2）依据该密度图从原始像素进行区域重读（regional re-reading），并据此分配视觉token；3）通过稀疏的、坐标感知的桥接（coordinate-aware bridge）将区域特征连接到全局场景，使宿主模型能在上下文中解读精确证据；4）训练时冻结宿主骨干，附件可同时服务于基础模型及兼容的后训练衍生模型，无需重新拟合。

Result: 在九个宿主模型上实验，平均细粒度准确率均获得一致提升；在相同预算的对比中，EviViT 在所有测试的token上限下都优于纯全局处理，且使用的视觉token更少。

Conclusion: EviViT 以轻量、即插即用的方式解决了高分辨率细粒度视觉感知中的token效率与上下文保持矛盾，广泛适用于多种宿主模型并具备良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception enables vision-language models to distinguish subtle attributes and ground their answers in visual evidence. In high-resolution scenes, processing the whole image at greater resolution spends visual tokens on irrelevant content, while isolated crops can lose the context needed to interpret the selected evidence. We introduce EviViT, a lightweight attachment that learns where a pretrained vision transformer should acquire detail. Human visual-search traces supervise a question-conditioned evidence density, which guides regional re-reading from the original pixels and the allocation of visual tokens. A sparse, coordinate-aware bridge then connects the regional features to the global scene, allowing the host to interpret precise evidence in context. Learned with the host backbone frozen, the attachment serves both the base model and compatible post-trained descendants without refitting. Experiments across nine hosts show consistent gains in average fine-grained accuracy. Matched-budget comparisons further show that EviViT outperforms global-only processing at every tested token ceiling while using fewer visual tokens.

</details>


### [116] [Multi-Granularity Language-Guided Imitation Learning via Instruction Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37135)
*Yi-Pei Chiu,Wei-Ta Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于指令分解的多粒度语言引导方法，将整体任务描述分解为细粒度子任务级指令，提升多任务模仿学习的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导策略学习通常用整体任务描述指导整条演示轨迹。但涉及多执行阶段的操控任务中，不同子任务被赋予相同的语言描述，难以区分各阶段所需行为，影响学习。

Method: 提出基于指令分解的多粒度语言引导方法，将整体任务描述分解为更细粒度、更具体的子任务级语言指令，用作策略学习的条件。

Result: 在多任务模仿学习设定下进行评估，验证了所提方法的有效性，提升了学习效率与性能。

Conclusion: 通过指令分解提供多粒度语言引导，可有效增强多阶段操控任务的策略学习效率与表现。

Abstract: Using language instructions as conditions to guide robot policy learning has recently become an important research domain. However, existing language-guided policy learning methods typically use an overall task description to guide the entire demonstration trajectory. For manipulation tasks involving multiple execution stages, these methods assign the same language description to different subtasks, making it difficult to distinguish the behaviors required at different stages. In this work, we propose a multi-granularity language guidance method based on instruction decomposition. The proposed method decomposes an overall task description into more fine-grained, concrete subtask-level language instructions, thereby enhancing learning efficiency and improving performance. We evaluate the proposed method in the setting of multi-task imitation learning and validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [117] [TaoFlowForge: Progressive Native Mesh Generation via Cascaded Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37139)
*Xianze Fang,Qiyuan Feng,Dongfang Sun,Yan Zhang,Xiuchao Wu,Jingnan Gao,Jiangjing Lyu,Chengfei Lyu,Gang Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaoFlowForge 是一个艺术网格基础模型，将网格生成分解为顶点生成和连接性（边）预测，生成可直接用于生产、轻量且拓扑干净的 3D 内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 内容生成技术难以产出轻量、可编辑、拓扑干净且可直接用于生产的艺术内容，这制约了设计师、3D 打印和游戏等行业的工作效率。

Method: 方法包含三部分：一是将网格生成分解为顶点生成与连接性（边）预测；顶点生成采用由粗到细的两阶段流程并引入多种有效损失函数；连接性预测提出简单有效的顶点间连接亲和度估计方法，并额外预测逐顶点法线以确定面的正确朝向。此外构建了融合手工 3D 资产与公开高质量拓扑数据集的大规模数据集，并通过精心设计的数据筛选流程保留高质量拓扑数据用于训练。

Result: 模型在合并数据集上训练，并在分布外手工 3D 资产集与公开数据集上测试。在图像条件生成任务中，TaoFlowForge 优于自回归方法，在开源网格拓扑生成器中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: TaoFlowForge 能生成可直接用于生产、轻量可编辑且拓扑干净的网格，性能领先同类开源方法，作者将公开全部代码、权重及部分测试数据集。

Abstract: 3D content generation technology has significantly advanced the work of designers, as well as the 3D printing and gaming industries. However, it remains difficult to produce lightweight, editable, and topologically clean artistic content that is directly production-ready. To achieve this, we present TaoFlowForge, an artistic mesh foundation model that generates production-ready meshes. Specifically, TaoFlowForge decomposes the mesh generation process into vertices generation and their connectivity prediction, i.e., edges. We formulate vertices generation as a two-stage coarse-to-fine process and incorporate several effective loss functions to further enhance its performance. In the connectivity prediction stage, we propose a simple yet effective method for estimating the connectivity affinity between vertices and additionally predict per-vertex normals, which determines the correct orientation of faces. Besides, we construct a large-scale dataset combining hand-crafted 3D assets with public high-quality topology datasets. Based on this, a carefully designed data curation pipeline is employed to filter the raw dataset, retaining only high-quality topology data for model training. Our model is trained on the combined dataset and tested on both out-of-distribution hand-crafted set of 3D assets and public datasets. Under image-conditioned generation, TaoFlowForge outperforms autoregressive methods and achieves state-of-the-art results among open-source mesh topology generators. We will release all the code and weights together with a portion of our test dataset.

</details>


### [118] [MSTypography: Multi-character Semantic Typography via Balancing Word Legibility and Object Recognizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37141)
*Xinye Yang,Xinding Zhu,Kai Fang,Xinyi Ren,Mengjian Li,Bin Cao,Jiazhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向多字符场景的语义排版框架，通过全局到局部的变形策略，在保持单词可读性的同时提升物体可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字排版方法主要针对单字符场景，扩展到多字符单词时存在可读性约束不足、局部变形不充分的问题，因为多字符间的复杂结构在排版过程中难以保留。作者希望同时平衡单词可读性与物体可识别性。

Method: 提出全局到局部的排版框架：全局层面进行掩码驱动的轮廓近似，局部层面进行语义引导的细化，中间加入剔除步骤提升效率。为保持可读性，设计了结构损失（显式碰撞约束与隐式Jacobian奇异值约束）以及字符级OCR约束；为增强可识别性，利用扩散先验的语义引导，使字形朝目标概念变形同时保持结构完整性。

Result: 在五种代表性语言（英语、中文、日语、韩语、阿拉伯语）上的评估表明，该方法优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个有效平衡单词可读性与物体可识别性的多字符语义排版方法，代码将开源。

Abstract: Semantic typography is a design technique where the visual representation of a word conveys its semantic meaning, while maintaining its legibility. Existing digital typography methods mainly focus on single-character scenarios. They suffer from a lack of legibility constraints and insufficient local deformation when extended to multi-character words, as the intricate structures among multiple characters are hardly preserved during the typography process. In this paper, we propose a global-to-local typography framework for multi-character scenarios. It performs mask-driven silhouette approximation at the global level, while semantic-guided refinement at the local level, with a culling step in between to improve efficiency. To preserve word legibility, we designed structural losses (including explicit collision constraints and implicit Jacobian singular value constraints) and an OCR constraint for character-level readability. To enhance the object recognizability, we leverage semantic guidance with diffusion priors, which drives the character glyph toward the target concept while preserving its structural integrity. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first multi-character semantic typography method that effectively balances word legibility and object recognizability. Evaluations on five representative languages (English, Chinese, Japanese, Korean, Arabic) demonstrate superiority over SOTA methods. Codes will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [119] [Improved Distributional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37147)
*Tommaso Martorella,Alexandre Galashov,Felix Krause,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Valentin De Bortoli,Arthur Gretton,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对分布扩散模型（DDMs）的两大扩展瓶颈——多粒子训练开销大、评分规则超参数全局固定——提出将粒子扩展推迟到Transformer后层，并引入基于动力学机制的时间相关评分规则调度，从而在ImageNet-256²上实现高效的少步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布扩散模型用分布式去噪器（通过评分规则目标训练）替代标准均值预测去噪器，学习 p(x1|xt) 的随机逼近。然而将 DDM 扩展到现代图像生成场景面临两个障碍：(i) 多粒子训练带来的开销随粒子数增长；(ii) DDM 使用全局固定的评分规则超参数，导致只能在单一权衡下适应不同采样预算。作者希望缓解这两个限制，使 DDM 训练在实用规模下可行。

Method: 作者提出两项改进：一是将粒子扩展推迟到Transformer的后层进行，以降低多粒子训练开销；二是引入时间相关的评分规则调度（time-dependent scoring rule schedules），其设计受 Biroli2024 的动力学机制启发，以缓解固定超参数的单一权衡问题。这些改进与基于 DiT 的潜空间（latent）设置相结合，形成完整的训练方案。

Result: 在类条件 ImageNet-256² 上取得：4步采样 FID 为 4.48，50步采样 FID 为 2.38；使用 DiT-XL/2，单阶段从零训练单个模型，无需教师模型、自蒸馏或 JVPs。该随机少步生成器在采样预算从 4 增加到 50 NFE 时 FID 不退化，且同一方案可迁移到文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 通过将粒子扩展推迟到后层并采用时间相关的评分规则调度，结合 DiT 潜空间设置，DDM 训练在大规模图像生成上变得实用，能够在单一模型、单阶段训练下实现高质量且采样预算鲁棒的少步生成，并可推广至文本到图像任务。代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Distributional Diffusion Models (DDMs) replace the standard mean-prediction denoiser with a \emph{distributional} denoiser trained via a scoring rule objective, learning a stochastic approximation to $p(x_1 \mid x_t)$ rather than its conditional mean. However, scaling DDMs to modern image-generation settings faces two obstacles: (i) multi-particle training incurs overhead that scales with the number of particles, (ii) DDMs use globally fixed scoring rule hyperparameters, forcing a single trade-off across sampling budgets. We mitigate these limitations by deferring particle expansion to late transformer layers, and the hyperparameter trade-off by introducing time-dependent scoring rule schedules informed by the dynamical regimes of~\citet{Biroli2024}. Combined with a DiT-based latent setup, these changes make DDM training practical on class-conditional ImageNet-$256^2$, achieving 4.48 FID at 4 steps and 2.38 at 50 steps with DiT-XL/2, from a single model trained from scratch in one stage, without a teacher, self-distillation or JVPs. The result is a stochastic few-step generator whose FID does not degrade as the sampling budget grows from 4 to 50 NFE, and the same recipe transfers to text-to-image generation. Code and pre-trained models available at https://github.com/CompVis/iDDM.

</details>


### [120] [End-to-End Self-Supervised RGB-T Tracking without Modality Misleading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37162)
*Shenglan Li,Rui Yao,Kunyang Sun,Hong Jia,Yong Zhou,Javen Qinfeng Shi,Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ESMTrack：全端到端自监督RGB-T跟踪框架，仅需首帧标注，通过双三元组损失和三支路模态解耦实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督RGB-T跟踪依赖昂贵的模态对齐边界框标注；多数自监督方法采用两阶段伪标签范式，训练对伪标签质量敏感且无法端到端联合优化。

Method: 提出ESMTrack，无需离线伪标签生成或密集帧级框标注，仅用初始帧标注。采用两个互补目标：带标注初始帧上的grounding三元组损失，以及无标注搜索帧上的跨帧时序三元组损失（前向-后向一致性筛选可靠样本）。为缓解模态主导偏差，设计三支路架构（融合支路+RGB/热红外单模态支路），用APCE度量融合与单模态支路响应差异量化模态贡献，进而引导训练时的模态解耦机制，抑制主导模态捷径并自适应加权跨模态对比学习。

Result: 在五个RGB-T跟踪基准上取得有竞争力的SOTA性能，具备强跨数据集泛化能力和实时推理速度。

Conclusion: ESMTrack证明了无需伪标签的端到端自监督RGB-T跟踪的可行性，兼顾性能、泛化与效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: RGB-T object tracking leverages the complementary characteristics of visible and thermal infrared modalities to improve robustness under adverse conditions. Existing supervised methods typically rely on costly modality-aligned bounding box annotations, while most self-supervised approaches follow a two-stage pseudo-labeling paradigm, making tracker training sensitive to pseudo-label quality and preventing joint end-to-end optimization. In this paper, we propose ESMTrack, a fully end-to-end self-supervised RGB-T tracking framework without offline pseudo-label generation or dense frame-level bounding box annotations. Given only the standard initial-frame annotation used in visual tracking, ESMTrack learns discriminative and temporally consistent representations through two complementary objectives: a grounding triplet loss on annotated initial frames and a cross-frame temporal triplet loss on unlabeled search frames, with reliable samples selected by forward-backward consistency. To address modality dominance bias, ESMTrack employs a three-branch architecture consisting of a fusion branch and two unimodal branches for RGB and thermal inputs. We quantify modality contributions using the Average Peak-to-Correlation Energy by measuring response discrepancies between the fusion and unimodal branches. The resulting reliability estimates guide a training-time modality decoupling mechanism that suppresses dominant-modality shortcuts and adaptively weights cross-modal contrastive learning for task-level alignment. Extensive experiments on five RGB-T tracking benchmarks show that ESMTrack achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance, strong cross-dataset generalization, and real-time inference speed. The source code is available at https://github.com/LiShenglana/ESMTrack.

</details>


### [121] [Sparse cubical complexes for efficient topology-preservation in image data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37177)
*Alexander H. Berger,Marco Fontana,Daniel Rueckert,Johannes C. Paetzold,Laurin Lux,Ulrich Bauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出稀疏立方过滤（sparse cubical filtrations）作为持久同调（PH）计算的新基础，可将后续计算成本降低最高100倍，同时保持与稠密方法相近的优化信号，并在六类数据集上提升拓扑精度最高80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调（PH）在图像分割等领域能有效提取并保留拓扑信息，具有跨维度、跨领域、跨目标结构的通用性。但其运行时成本高昂，导致许多实际应用不可行。作者认为这一成本主要源于处理了对下游应用（如优化目标）而言并不重要的信息。

Method: 作者提出以稀疏立方过滤（sparse cubical filtrations）替代原有PH计算基础，仅处理对下游应用重要的信息，从而降低后续计算成本。并将该方法作为优化目标，在真实训练场景（如大补丁尺寸的3D数据）中评估其有效性，与稠密对应方法进行对比。

Result: 在真实数据集上计算成本降低最高达100倍；与稠密方法的优化信号高度一致；在六类多样化数据集上，拓扑精度提升最高达80%，同时保持像素级和区域级精度。

Conclusion: 稀疏立方过滤能大幅降低PH计算成本，使PH-based优化目标在以往难以运行的训练场景中（如大补丁尺寸3D数据）变得可行，并在保持像素与区域精度的同时显著提升拓扑准确性。

Abstract: Persistent homology (PH) is a frequently used tool for extracting and preserving topological information from image data, particularly in image segmentation, where preservation of topological structures is important. However, despite its general applicability across dimensionality, domains, and target structures, the runtime cost of PH-based methods often makes their practical use infeasible. In this work, we argue that this runtime cost is largely driven by processing information that is unimportant for downstream application (e.g. as optimization objective). We propose sparse cubical filtrations as an alternative foundation for PH computation, reducing subsequent computational costs by factors of up to 100 on real datasets. We show close agreement with the optimization signal of the dense counterpart and empirically evaluate our solution's effectiveness as an optimization objective in realistic training regimes where other PH-based objectives can practically not operate (i.e., 3D data with large patch sizes). We show how our solution improves topological accuracy by up to 80\% across six diverse datasets while maintaining pixel- and region-based accuracy.

</details>


### [122] [InsightMap: Structured Spatial Modeling for Embodied Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37187)
*Hongpei Zheng,Hujun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsightMap利用俯视地图作为显式空间记忆和动作条件预测目标，通过共享多模态主干联合学习导航动作预测与动作后地图生成，在多个导航与空间任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导导航需要将局部观测连接到持久空间参考，并学习动作如何改变该表示。

Method: 将历史视图链接到带标注地图位置；使用共享多模态主干联合学习导航动作预测和动作后地图生成；地图预测提供辅助监督，导航推理从观测到的空间上下文解码动作；构建对齐的RGB-D数据管线，为导航、视觉问答、情境推理和3D定位提供统一接口。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE验证未见划分上SR分别为56.9%和54.9%；加入地图预测监督使R2R-CE SR提升4.3、SPL提升3.2个百分点；ScanQA CIDEr 103.7，SQA3D精确匹配60.1%，ScanRefer 0.5 IoU定位精度53.1%；在Unitree Go2的走廊、实验室和办公环境中优于NaVid和NaVILA。

Conclusion: 以俯视地图作为空间记忆与预测目标，可有效提升语言引导导航及多种空间任务表现。

Abstract: Language-guided navigation requires connecting partial observations to a persistent spatial reference and learning how actions change that representation. We introduce InsightMap, a framework that uses top-down maps as both explicit spatial memory and action-conditioned prediction targets. Historical views are linked to labeled map locations, and a shared multimodal backbone jointly learns navigation action prediction and post-action map generation. Map prediction provides auxiliary training supervision, while navigation inference decodes actions from the observed spatial context. An aligned RGB-D data pipeline supports a common interface for navigation, visual question answering, situated reasoning, and 3D grounding. On the validation-unseen splits of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, InsightMap achieves success rates (SR) of 56.9% and 54.9%, respectively. Adding map-prediction supervision improves R2R-CE SR by 4.3 and success weighted by path length (SPL) by 3.2 percentage points. On static spatial tasks, InsightMap achieves 103.7 CIDEr on ScanQA, 60.1% exact-match accuracy on SQA3D, and 53.1% grounding accuracy at 0.5 IoU on ScanRefer with detected object proposals. On Unitree Go2, it outperforms NaVid and NaVILA in hallway, lab, and office environments.

</details>


### [123] [HaPRL: Human-Anchored Process Reinforcement Learning for Visual Search Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37190)
*Zhangquan Chen,Yaoxin Niu,Xiang An,Mingze Sun,Zhumei Wang,Chih-Ting Liao,Hongkun Cao,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HaPRL框架，首次用人类搜索行为对多轮视觉搜索智能体的搜索过程进行强化，克服仅奖励最终答案导致的错误路径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮视觉搜索智能体通过迭代决定"看哪里"来回答高分辨率图像问题，但强化学习只奖励最终答案，搜索过程缺乏监督，导致"推理错误却结果正确"的故障路径频繁出现，造成训练低效、沿错误路径扩展。

Method: 构建标注平台，收集1K+带细粒度行为信号的人工标注数据；训练时设计评分器（judge），以任务自适应权重对每次rollout打分，并以人类标注者实际搜索同一图像的提炼轨迹为锚点。

Result: HaPRL持续优于基于结果的强化学习；早期阶段的过程监督使后续基于结果的扩展提升达6.7倍。

Conclusion: 将模型行为与人类过程标注信号对齐十分重要，为基座模型训练提供了新见解。

Abstract: Multi-turn visual search agents answer questions about high-resolution images by iteratively deciding where to look. Reinforcement learning for these agents rewards only the final answer, leaving the search process unsupervised. Consequently, faulty routes in which the reasoning process is erroneous yet the final result is correct arise frequently, which in turn leads to ineffective training, i.e., scaling along the wrong paths. In this paper, we introduce HaPRL, the first framework to reinforce the search process with human search behavior. We first build an annotation platform and collect 1K+ human-annotated data with fine-grained behavioral signals. During training, a carefully designed judge scores each rollout with task-adaptive weights, anchored on the distilled trace of how a human annotator actually searched the same image. Extensive experiments show that HaPRL consistently outperforms outcome-based RL, and early-stage process supervision yields 6.7x more improvement in subsequent outcome-based scaling. Our results also demonstrate the importance of aligning model behavior with human process annotation signals, which offer new insight into the training of foundation models.

</details>


### [124] [Exploring In-Context Learning for Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37195)
*Eric Ayllon,Abel Gandia,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索利用预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）的上下文学习（ICL）构建无需参数更新的手写文本识别（HTR）流程，并在跨域场景下验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTR研究多依赖专用模型，需要大量标注样本才能达到满意性能，成本高且难以泛化到域偏移场景。作者希望探索免微调、可跨域迁移的通用方案。

Method: 使用预训练VLM进行上下文学习，构建不更新模型参数的转录流程；在多个数据集与模型上评估，并设计受控的同域（ID）与更贴近实际的跨域（CD）场景，其中上下文示例来自与查询图像不同的数据集合。

Result: ID与CD场景呈现相同规律：随着上下文规模增大，误差范围向平均性能收窄，即更大的上下文规模以牺牲最优采样（oracle-best）性能换取更低的期望误差；不同上下文采样方式效果差异明显。

Conclusion: 无需任何参数更新，该方法在存在域偏移时具有与传统HTR竞争的潜力；部分上下文采样策略优于其他策略，未来应投入更多精力寻找理想的采样方法。

Abstract: Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) systems have become an indispensable tool for the digitization of historical documents. Not only do they cut down time and cost, but they also allow democratizing access and processing of their contents by generating their transcripts. However, literature in HTR currently focuses mostly on specialized models that require large amounts of annotated samples to achieve satisfactory performance.
  We explore the use of In-Context Learning with pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to create a transcription pipeline without updating the model's parameters. We then evaluate this pipeline across multiple collections and models, and demonstrate that general-purpose VLMs can be effectively taught how to transcribe handwritten text from images. To assess how our observations may translate to practical applications, we evaluate the performance in a Cross-Domain (CD) scenario, where context examples are drawn from a different collection than the query image.
  Results in both the controlled In-Domain (ID) scenario and the realistic CD scenario follow the same patterns. First, as context size grows, the error range is expected to narrow towards the average performance. Thus, larger context sizes sacrifice the performance of the oracle-best sampling for lower expected error rates.
  The results obtained show that, without any parameter updates, this methodology has strong potential to compete with traditional HTR in the presence of domain shift. Moreover, we show and argue that some context samplings work better than others and suggest more effort should be put into finding an ideal sampling method in future work.

</details>


### [125] [Adaptive Reward Routing: Dynamic Multi-Reward Optimization for Joint Audio-Video Diffusion via Forward-Process RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37200)
*Songlin Yang,Xiaotong Zhao,Jiacheng Zhang,Zhe Wang,Toyota Li,Eric Liu,Alan Zhao,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应奖励路由（Adaptive Reward Routing），在联合音视频扩散模型的正向过程强化学习中同时自适应调整更新位置与奖励协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励强化学习可提升联合音视频扩散模型在模态质量、跨模态语义对齐与时序同步等多目标上的表现，但其效果依赖两个随训练变化的量：奖励驱动更新应作用于何处，以及竞争性奖励应如何组合。现有方法多采用固定的路由与奖励权重，难以跟踪模型功能的演化。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(i) 跨模态影响引导路由（局部化更新）：以双向交叉注意力响应作为演化中的跨模态影响的高效代理，动态重加权token感知损失，并在跨模态层间缩放梯度，无需额外模型干预；(ii) 偏好保持的模态感知重加权（奖励协调）：将预设权重作为偏好先验，在预热后用分支特定的奖励梯度交互作残差修正。

Result: 大量实验表明，相比强RL基线，在模态质量、语义一致性和音视频同步方面均取得持续提升；消融与机制分析进一步验证了自适应更新路由与奖励协调的互补收益。

Conclusion: 通过自适应地联合调整更新位置与奖励协调，可有效缓解演化冲突、避免主导奖励压制弱但必要的目标，从而提升联合音视频扩散模型的多目标强化学习效果。

Abstract: Multi-reward guided reinforcement learning (i.e., RL) offers a promising way to improve joint audio-video diffusion models along several complementary objectives, including modality-specific quality, cross-modal semantic alignment, and temporal synchronization. Its effectiveness, however, depends on two quantities that change during training: where reward-driven updates should act, and how competing rewards should be combined. Existing methods tend to rely on fixed routing and reward weights, failing to track evolving model functions. To address these limitations, we propose Adaptive Reward Routing to jointly adapt update locations and reward coordination during forward-process RL (i.e., DiffusionNFT) of joint audio-video diffusion models. Our method consists of two components. (i) Cross-Modal Influence-Guided Routing (Localizing Updates): We use bidirectional cross-attention responses as an efficient proxy for evolving cross-modal influence, dynamically reweighting token-aware losses and scaling gradients across cross-modal layers without additional model interventions. (ii) Preference-Preserving Modality-Aware Reweighting (Coordinating Rewards): We preserve predefined weights as preference priors and use branch-specific reward-gradient interactions as residual corrections after warm-up. This resolves evolving conflicts without letting dominant rewards suppress weak but essential objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in modality quality, semantic consistency, and audio-video synchronization over strong RL baselines. Ablations and mechanism analyses further validate the complementary benefits of adaptive update routing and reward coordination.

</details>


### [126] [ResComEmb: Effective and Efficient Multimodal Embedding via Residual Homogeneity Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37225)
*Zijing Cai,Yuzhe Wang,Jingxian Zhu,Fengbin Zhu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResComEmb是一个可训练的多向量多模态嵌入框架，通过残差同质压缩与双向后交互匹配，在有限视觉token预算下实现高效且细粒度的通用多模态嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型嵌入方法存在权衡困境：要么将输入压缩为单一向量，限制细粒度表达能力；要么保留长序列视觉token向量，带来存储和交互成本过高的问题。

Method: 先在原生动态分辨率下将输入编码为有序的全局、中间和细粒度视图；经MLLM上下文化嵌入投影后，用可训练的残差同质压缩（RHC）模块在显式视觉token预算下减少粒度内冗余和跨粒度重复；再引入长度自适应双向后交互匹配机制进行查询-文档打分，对各方向最强token级匹配取平均，并基于双方有效token数加权组合两个分数。

Result: 在MMEB、ViDoRe V1和ViDoRe V2上，ResComEmb生成的通用多模态嵌入质量高于VLM2Vec-V2；在视觉文档检索中仅用ColQwen2.5完整视觉token预算的37.5%即超越后者，展现出良好的效果-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ResComEmb通过压缩与匹配机制的有效设计，缓解了表达能力与效率之间的矛盾，为通用多模态嵌入提供了一种高效且高质量的方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for universal multimodal representation learning. However, existing methods either compress each input into a single vector, limiting fine-grained expressiveness, or retain long sequences of visual-token vectors, incurring substantial storage and interaction costs. To resolve this trade-off, we propose ResComEmb, a trainable framework for effective and efficient universal multi-vector multimodal embedding. ResComEmb first encodes each input at native dynamic resolution into ordered global, intermediate, and fine-grained views. After MLLM contextualization and embedding projection, a trainable Residual Homogeneity Compression (RHC) module reduces within-granularity redundancy and cross-granularity repetition under explicit visual token budgets. Then, ResComEmb introduces a length-adaptive Bidirectional Late-Interaction Matching mechanism for robust query-document scoring, which averages the strongest token-level matches in each direction and combines the two scores using a weight based on how many valid tokens each side has. Extensive experiments on MMEB, ViDoRe V1, and ViDoRe V2 show that ResComEmb produces higher-quality universal multimodal embeddings than VLM2Vec-V2, and outperforms ColQwen2.5 in visual document retrieval using only 37.5% of its full visual token budget, demonstrating a favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [127] [AESOP: Asymmetric Human-Camera Generation with Translation-Intensity Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37229)
*Jingzhong Lin,Zhanke Wang,Heng Li,Wenxiang Liu,Zhao Zhang,Kecheng Tang,Dongdong Xiang,Changbo Wang,Di Kang,Chunchao Guo,Linchao Bao,Gaoqi He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AESOP是一个统一框架，采用独立人体路径与共享的人体条件相机生成器，可同时处理“给定动作生成相机轨迹”与“人体-相机联合生成”两个任务，并通过轨迹对学习显式平移强度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体动作定义行为，相机轨迹决定其呈现方式。相机生成与人体-相机联合生成通常被当作两个独立任务处理，但二者共享一种非对称依赖：人体动作可独立生成，相机则响应已实现的动 作。因此需要一个能统一两者、并在相机生成时保持人体输出不变的框架。此外，人体语境锚定镜头与动作的关系、相机文本描述运动方式，但平移强度仍缺乏明确指定。

Method: 提出AESOP统一框架：包含独立的人体生成路径与共享的“人体条件相机生成器”，其非对称结构同时服务两个任务并保持人体输出不变。针对平移强度未被充分指定的问题，构造轨迹对——共享相同人体动作与相机文本、仅相机平移幅度不同，用这些轨迹对学习显式的强度条件。

Result: 在PulpMotion数据集上的实验表明，两项任务均获得良好的相机分布质量与取景质量，并能有效控制相机平移强度。

Conclusion: AESOP通过非对称的人体独立、相机条件共享架构统一了两个相关任务，并借助轨迹对实现显式强度条件，验证了统一建 模与平移强度可控性的可行性。

Abstract: Human motion defines an action, while a camera trajectory determines how it is presented. Camera generation for a given human motion and joint human-camera generation are usually treated as separate tasks, although both share an asymmetric dependency: human motion can be generated independently, whereas the camera responds to the realized action. We introduce AESOP, a unified framework with an independent human pathway and a shared human-conditioned camera generator. Its asymmetric architecture serves both tasks while preserving the human output during camera generation. Although human context anchors the shot to the action and camera text describes its movement, translation intensity remains underspecified. We therefore construct trajectory pairs that differ in camera translation magnitude while sharing human motion and camera text, then use these pairs to learn an explicit intensity condition. Experiments on the PulpMotion dataset demonstrate strong camera distributional and framing quality in both tasks and effective control over camera translation intensity.

</details>


### [128] [Seeing Is Not Addressing: Auditing Linguistic Access to Frozen Visual Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37230)
*Woosang Jeon,Jiwon Yang,Soo Chung,Taehyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉可辨别性与语言可寻址性在文生图检索中的不完全重合，并提出图像侧对比的匹配视觉对齐方法来缩小这一访问差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉区分往往比语言概念化更精细。视觉-语言模型也存在类似不对称：某些区分在冻结图像几何中仍可辨别，却难以通过原生文本接口寻址。作者希望系统性研究这一可辨别性与可寻址性之间的差距。

Method: 使用 FactorAtlas 全交叉测试床，含 23,040 张图像，覆盖形状、色调、图案及干扰变化。将视觉可辨别性与语言可寻址性分离，在留出图像上比较两种读出方式；推导图像侧对比以将每个取值与其备选值区分开，实现匹配视觉对齐，并检验其是否缩小原生文本访问差距。

Result: 图像侧对比带来的增益经方向特异性与视觉缺失控制确认与相关视觉对比挂钩；增益在全局对齐后依然存在，并扩展到组合式检索和自然图像。

Conclusion: 视觉可辨别性与语言可寻址性不必一致；匹配视觉对齐能够探测并缩小由此产生的访问差距。

Abstract: Visual distinctions are often finer than those reflected in linguistic conceptualization. Vision-language models exhibit a similar asymmetry: a distinction can remain discriminable in frozen image geometry while being weakly addressable through the native text interface. We study this gap by separating visual discriminability from linguistic addressability in text-to-image retrieval. Using FactorAtlas, a fully crossed testbed of 23,040 images spanning shape, hue, pattern, and nuisance variation, we compare both readouts on held-out images of the same distinctions. We then derive image-side contrasts that separate each value from its alternatives for matched visual grounding, and test whether this reduces the native-text access gap across factors and models. Direction-specific and visual-absence controls tie these gains to the relevant visual contrast; the gains persist after global alignment and extend to compositional retrieval and natural images. Together, these results show that visual discriminability and linguistic addressability need not coincide, and that matched visual grounding can probe and reduce the resulting access gap.

</details>


### [129] [Codebook-Guided Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Structurally Heterogeneous Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37243)
*Dae Ung Jo,Jongin Lim,YoungJoon Yoo,Daeho Um*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于向量量化码本的跨模态蒸馏框架，将教师特征抽象为概念级锚点，实现结构异构特征空间间的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征级对齐方法假设教师与学生特征处于可结构对齐的表示空间，但该假设在跨模态特征结构异构、缺乏单元级对应关系时（如2D空间视觉网格与1D时序音频序列）不成立，限制了特征级对齐的适用性。

Method: 利用向量量化码本，将教师特征不论其原始结构如何都抽象为一组向量形式的码；以选取的码作为学生学习的"概念级锚点"；码的选择同时受任务相关性和学生兼容性双重引导，使学生无需直接进行单元级特征对齐即可接收可迁移的教师知识。

Result: 在多种跨模态蒸馏场景下的实验表明，该框架在分类与语义分割任务上均有效。

Conclusion: 通过概念级码本锚点替代单元级特征对齐，可实现结构异构跨模态特征空间之间有效的知识迁移。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation transfers knowledge from a teacher modality to a student modality. Existing feature-level alignment methods typically assume that teacher and student features reside in structurally alignable representation spaces. However, this assumption does not hold when cross-modal features are structurally heterogeneous and lack clear unit-level correspondence, such as 2D spatial visual grids and 1D temporal audio sequences, thereby limiting the applicability of feature-level alignment. To address this challenge, we propose a cross-modal distillation framework that enables effective knowledge transfer across structurally heterogeneous feature spaces via a vector-quantized codebook. Specifically, teacher features are abstracted into a set of vector-form codes regardless of their original feature structure, and the selected codes serve as concept-level anchors for student learning. Code selection is guided by both task relevance and student compatibility, allowing the student to receive transferable teacher knowledge without requiring direct unit-level feature alignment. Experimental results across diverse cross-modal distillation scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on classification and semantic segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [130] [V-JEPA Policy: Building Effective World-Action Models on Predictive Visual Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37250)
*Yang Zhang,Jiangyuan Zhao,Chenyou Fan,Jiayu Hu,Xiu Yuan,Chenjia Bai,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 V-JEPA Policy，直接在冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 预测性视觉潜空间上构建世界-动作模型（WAM），无需继承完整预训练视觉生成模型。实验表明该框架在 LIBERO 等基准上取得有竞争力的表现，且预测性视觉潜空间优于其他视觉基础，并可从无动作标签视频中迁移未来建模知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 多依赖大规模预训练的视频生成或图像编辑模型，需继承完整生成模型。作者质疑：仅由大规模预测预训练诱导的预测性视觉潜空间，是否足以作为有效 WAM 学习的基础，而无需继承完整生成模型。

Method: 构建 V-JEPA Policy：以冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器潜空间为基础，从零联合学习指令条件下的未来潜变量预测器和流匹配动作专家，预测器的未来信息上下文 KV 状态用于条件化动作生成。总参数 0.9B，其中 0.6B 可训练。此外，在无动作标签的 DROID 视频-指令对上预训练预测器，再适配为 WAM。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboCasa-GR1 上，与代表性 WAM 和视觉-语言-动作基线取得有竞争力的性能。相同下游框架和训练预算下，V-JEPA 潜空间比判别式、重建式和视频理解导向的替代方案更有效，尤其在分布偏移下。用无动作标签的 DROID 数据预训练预测器可显著提升下游控制和 OOD 泛化。

Conclusion: 预测性视觉潜空间可作为 WAM 学习的有效基础，既能从任务特定演示中学习，也能转移从广泛野外视频中获得的未来建模知识。代码已开源。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple future visual-state prediction with action generation. By adapting video generators or image-editing models pretrained at scale, a prominent line of recent WAMs inherits both predictive knowledge and the models in which it was learned. We ask whether a predictive visual latent space induced by large-scale predictive pretraining can instead provide a sufficient foundation for effective WAM learning without inheriting a complete pretrained visual generative model. To answer this question, we introduce V-JEPA Policy, a simple framework that builds a WAM on the latent space of a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. An instruction-conditioned future-latent predictor and a flow-matching action expert are jointly learned from scratch in a single downstream stage, with the predictor's future-informed context key--value states conditioning action generation. With 0.9B total parameters, of which 0.6B are trainable, V-JEPA Policy achieves competitive performance with representative WAM and vision-language-action baselines across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa-GR1. Comparing visual foundations under the same downstream framework and training budget identifies V-JEPA latents as more effective than the discriminative, reconstructive, and video-understanding-oriented alternatives, particularly under distribution shifts. Beyond task-specific learning, pretraining the predictor on DROID video--instruction pairs without action labels and adapting it into a WAM yields substantial gains in downstream control and out-of-distribution generalization. Together, these findings establish predictive visual latents as a foundation for effective WAM learning from task-specific demonstrations and for transferring future-modeling knowledge acquired from broader in-the-wild videos. Our code is available at https://github.com/breez3young/VJEPA-Policy.

</details>


### [131] [Collision-Aware and Observation-Aligned Object-Centric Scene Reconstruction from Point Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37260)
*Yuxuan Xie,Xuan Yu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COOL框架，以实例与背景点云为条件进行物体生成，实现碰撞感知、观测对齐的场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式物体中心场景重建方法多基于图像条件，存在尺度模糊和几何约束不足的问题，难以在保持度量对齐的同时补全部分观测并避免碰撞。

Method: COOL在物体生成模型基础上，以实例点云和背景点云作为条件：实例几何将生成锚定在场景坐标系中，背景几何提供局部上下文以实现场景一致的补全；并引入显式碰撞损失，结合联合优化与重采样在推理阶段减少碰撞。

Result: 在3D-Front和Scan2CAD数据集上，方法在场景级保真度、观测对齐和碰撞减少方面表现良好；额外实验验证了对掩码错误的鲁棒性及在真实场景复刻中的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过实例与背景点云条件化生成并显式建模碰撞，可在保持度量对齐的前提下高质量补全部分观测，提升场景重建的物理合理性与实用范围。

Abstract: Object-centric scene reconstruction requires completing partial object observations while preserving metric alignment and avoiding collisions with the surrounding. Existing generation-based methods are often image-conditioned and suffer from scale ambiguity and insufficient geometric constraints. We propose COOL, a framework for COllision-aware and Observation-aLigned reconstruction. Based on an object generation model, COOL conditions the generation on instance and background point clouds. Instance geometry anchors generation in scene coordinates, while background geometry provides local context for scene-consistent completion. We further introduce an explicit collision loss and use joint optimization and resampling to reduce collisions during inference. Experiments on 3D-Front and Scan2CAD demonstrate strong scene-level fidelity, observation alignment, and collision reduction. Moreover, additional studies validate its robustness to mask errors and its applicability to real-world scene replicas.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Attention Imbalance: Mitigating Hallucinations via Spectral Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37263)
*Siqi Lu,Suo Wei,Yongbin Zheng,Jianhang Yao,Wanying Xu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLASH框架，通过频域视角识别幻觉模式，无需训练与对比解码即可缓解LVLM幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将LVLM幻觉归因于跨模态注意力失衡，解决方案集中于重加权视觉token或抑制语言先验，但忽视了视觉信息流的频谱特征，且常依赖对比解码导致推理时间翻倍。

Method: 作者识别出两种幻觉模式——感知-语义解离与局部固着，并提出FLASH框架：利用谱涡旋分数（Spectral Vortex Score）在多注意力层中检测视觉头，并在解码过程中施加自适应谱调制以矫正视觉信息流。

Result: 实验结果表明，FLASH在性能与效率的平衡上优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: FLASH是一种免训练、免对比解码的框架，从频域角度为缓解LVLM幻觉提供了高效且有效的新思路。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve remarkable success, hallucinations remain a significant barrier to their reliable deployment. Recent studies primarily attribute these issues to cross-modal attention imbalances; most solutions therefore focus on reweighting visual tokens or suppressing language priors. However, such approaches often overlook the spectral characteristics of the visual information flow and frequently rely on Contrastive Decoding (CD), which doubles inference time. Instead of following conventional approaches, we identify two distinct hallucination patterns-Perceptual-Semantic Dissociation and Localized Fixation-and propose FLASH (Frequency-Localized Attention SHaping), a training-free and CD-free framework. FLASH utilizes a Spectral Vortex Score to detect vision heads within multi-head attention layers and applies adaptive spectral modulation to rectify the visual information flow during decoding. Empirical results demonstrate that FLASH achieves a superior balance between performance and efficiency compared to SOTA methods.

</details>


### [133] [UniAfford: Token-Routed Multitask Learning for Generalizable 2D-3D Affordance Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37264)
*Yuhao Liu,Yiming Zhong,Hanqing Wang,Shaocheng Yan,Yuhang Zhang,Wenzhou Lyu,Ziyang Ding,Wei Zhang,Xue Zhao,Jin Pan,Yuexin Ma,Xinge Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Token Router for Tasks 多任务训练范式与 UniAfford 统一框架，实现2D-3D可供性感知的统一建模与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D与3D可供性感知长期作为独立问题发展，任务定义、监督格式、数据集与评测协议各异，阻碍了跨视觉与几何空间的可迁移物体-可供性语义学习。

Method: 提出 Token Router for Tasks 范式，将上下文隐状态路由至任务专用分支，由分支特定目标直接监督，无需语言头生成预定义标记；实例化为 UniAfford，以 MLLM 为共享语义枢纽，配合模态感知 token 路由器生成图像与点云可供性查询，分别驱动 SAM 风格像素解码器与 SONATA 点解码器；并构建 UniAfford-Data 数据集，整合像素级2D标注、点级3D标注与语言指令，共享物体-可供性分类体系。

Result: 在2D与3D可供性基准上展现强零样本泛化能力，无需目标域微调；在模态隔离协议下取得分支最优性能；消融验证了token路由、2D-3D联合监督与解码器耦合的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言头诊断表明路由后的隐状态携带有效的物体-可供性语义，证明统一的多任务路由范式可有效桥接2D与3D可供性感知。

Abstract: Affordance perception aims to localize actionable regions supporting embodied interaction, yet 2D and 3D affordance grounding have evolved as separate problems, with different task definitions, supervision formats, datasets, and evaluation protocols. This fragmentation limits the learning of transferable object-affordance semantics across visual and geometric spaces. We propose Token Router for Tasks, a multitask training paradigm for MLLM-based systems that routes contextual hidden states to task-specific branches without requiring the language head to generate predefined markers. Routed states are supervised directly by branch-specific objectives, enabling dense prediction losses to shape shared MLLM representations. We instantiate this paradigm as UniAfford, a unified framework for generalizable 2D-3D affordance perception, together with UniAfford-Data, a dataset integrating pixel-level 2D annotations, point-level 3D annotations, and language instructions under a shared object-affordance taxonomy, supporting heterogeneous supervision through semantic-level 2D-3D pairing. UniAfford adopts an MLLM as a shared semantic hub and a modality-aware token router to produce image- and point-cloud-affordance queries. These queries respectively condition a SAM-style pixel decoder and a SONATA-based point decoder, enabling flexible 2D, 3D, and joint affordance inference from image-only, point-cloud-only, or paired multimodal inputs. Experiments demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization across 2D and 3D affordance benchmarks without target-specific fine-tuning, alongside state-of-the-art branch-wise performance under modality-isolated protocols. Ablations validate token routing, joint 2D-3D supervision, and decoder coupling, while language-head diagnostics show that routed latent states carry meaningful object-affordance semantics. Project page: https://4dvlab.github.io/UniAfford

</details>


### [134] [SAM Meets VLM: Parameter-Decoupled Full-Parameter Training for Unified Medical Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37283)
*Xuyang Cao,Enyou Liu,Jun Zhao,Zhuoyun Liu,Jintao Fei,Leo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出参数解耦训练框架，通过分离<SEG>提示状态与语言状态，实现医学多模态大模型统一推理与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多模态大模型需同时具备临床问答与视觉证据定位能力。现有通过<SEG> token连接VLM与SAM的方案中，全参数训练困难，因为图像级推理与像素级分割对共享表示空间的要求不同。

Method: 将<SEG>隐状态视为语义到空间的提示，鼓励其与通用语言状态可分离。分三阶段：(1)医学浅层对齐，适配视觉特征而不扰动LLM；(2)受控指令微调，塑造可分离的<SEG>提示状态，用DBI监控，并缩放进入语言主干的割梯度；(3)冻结VLM，专门优化SAM分支提升掩码精度。

Result: 在医学指代分割、grounding、视觉QA和文本QA基准上，实现强语言条件分割，同时保持有竞争力的推理能力。

Conclusion: 消融显示两阶段指令微调、梯度缩放和分割专门化均有贡献，验证参数解耦训练框架的有效性。

Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected not only to answer clinical questions, but also to localize the visual evidence behind their predictions. A common strategy connects a vision--language model (VLM) with SAM-style segmentation through a special <SEG> token, yet full-parameter training of this unified architecture is difficult because image-level reasoning and pixel-level segmentation impose different requirements on the shared representation space. To address this issue, we propose a parameter-decoupled training framework for unified medical reasoning and segmentation. The framework treats the <SEG> hidden state as a semantic-to-spatial prompt for the mask decoder and encourages it to become separable from generic language states, reducing ambiguous segmentation prompts and potential disruption to reasoning representations. It first performs medical shallow alignment to adapt visual features to clinical language without disturbing the LLM; then controlled instruction tuning shapes separable <SEG> prompt states, monitored by the Davies--Bouldin Index (DBI), while scaling segmentation gradients entering the language backbone; finally, the SAM branch is specialized with the VLM frozen to improve mask precision without altering reasoning parameters. Experiments on medical referring segmentation, grounding, visual QA, and textual QA benchmarks show that our framework achieves strong language-conditioned segmentation while preserving competitive reasoning ability. Ablations show that two-phase instruction tuning, gradient scaling, and segmentation specialization all contribute to the model.

</details>


### [135] [VISTA-Bench: Benchmarking Multilingual Image Translation with Image-Specific Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37287)
*Bo Lv,Mao Zheng,Zheng Li,Fangxu Liu,Mingrui Sun,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA-Bench，一个覆盖22种语言、10个领域的图像翻译基准，并设计了针对图像特点的评分标准评价协议，对16个主流模型进行了系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像翻译是多模态模型在多语言应用中的基础能力，需要跨语言的视觉理解与语义保持。但现有基准语言覆盖有限，且缺乏明确的图像针对性评价标准，难以全面评估该能力。

Method: 构建VISTA-Bench基准：涵盖22种语言、10个领域；结合语言与场景覆盖的采样策略，采用模型辅助、人工校验的标注方式，将相关文本归并为连贯语义单元并提供多语言参考译文；设计针对图像的评分标准，明确必要内容、语义关系与可接受译文变体，分别给出译文质量和视觉及知识依赖信息保持的得分。

Result: 对16个主流模型（12个多模态模型和4个文本输入模型）进行了广泛评测，并在语言、领域和评价维度上进行了系统分析。

Conclusion: VISTA-Bench为图像翻译能力提供了更全面、更细粒度的评估框架，有助于系统刻画多模态模型在多语言图像翻译上的表现与不足。

Abstract: Image translation is a fundamental capability of multimodal models for multilingual applications, requiring visual understanding and meaning preservation across languages. However, existing benchmarks have limited language coverage and often lack explicit image-specific evaluation criteria, making it difficult to comprehensively assess this capability. To systematically evaluate this capability, we introduce VISTA-Bench, covering 22 languages and 10 domains, and develop an image-specific rubric evaluation protocol. The benchmark combines sampling for language and scenario coverage with model-assisted, human-verified annotations that group related text into coherent semantic units and provide multilingual reference translations. The rubrics specify essential content, semantic relations, and acceptable translation variants, yielding separate output-based scores for translation quality and the preservation of visual and knowledge-dependent information. We conduct extensive evaluations of 16 mainstream models, including 12 multimodal models and four text-input models, and provide systematic analyses across languages, domains, and evaluation dimensions.

</details>


### [136] [Why Cross-Skeleton Retargeting Is Non-Identifiable: Structural Limits of Generative Motion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37297)
*Zhiyuan Li,Wenyan Yang,Pekka Marttinen,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出跨骨架动作生成存在结构性歧义：标准生成目标下源条件重定向映射不可识别，要么因非配对分布匹配产生规范不可识别性，要么因稀疏配对监督导致条件均值退化；为此提出源实例保真度（SIF）诊断，发现现有方法多停留在源盲下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨骨架动作生成希望将动作结构与运动意图从一个身体迁移到另一个身体，但训练数据无法区分两种解释：模型真的迁移了源片段，还是仅根据所请求动作复原了典型动作。作者希望说明这种歧义是结构性的，并提供可观测这一缺失证据的诊断方法。

Method: 作者从理论层面分析标准生成目标下的不可识别性：非配对分布匹配导致规范不可识别性，不同骨架的潜空间可相互变换而不改变训练证据；稀疏配对监督则导致条件均值退化，平方误差训练收敛到忽略源片段的平均目标动作。为观测缺失证据，提出源实例保真度（SIF）诊断，在固定目标骨架与动作的情况下，检验输出之间的差异是否与源片段之间的差异一致。

Result: 在动物运动数据的标准动作级测试中，成功的方法往往处于源盲下限；而能够超出该下限的方法也只保留部分关系信号，说明当前方法难以真正识别源条件映射。

Conclusion: 跨骨架重定向需要新的目标函数与评估指标，以识别其声称学习的源条件映射，而不能仅依赖动作级测试。

Abstract: Cross-skeleton motion generation trains generative models to carry action structure and motion intention from one body to another. Yet a target motion that shows the right action has two explanations that the training data cannot tell apart: the model transferred the source clip, or it recovered a typical motion for the requested action. We show that this ambiguity is structural rather than incidental: under standard generative objectives, the source-conditioned retargeting map is non-identifiable in sparse heterogeneous motion domains. Unpaired distribution matching yields gauge non-identifiability: the latent spaces of different skeletons can be transformed relative to one another without changing the training evidence, so different source-conditioned maps fit it equally well. Sparse paired supervision admits the complementary failure mode, \emph{conditional-mean degeneration}: when clips are paired only by action, squared-error training converges to an average target motion that ignores the source clip. To make the missing evidence observable, we introduce Source-Instance Fidelity (SIF), a diagnostic that tests whether outputs differ from one another the way their source clips do, with the target skeleton and action held fixed. Under this diagnostic, methods that succeed at the standard action-level test on animal motion data often sit at the source-blind floor, while the methods that rise above it retain only a partial relational signal. Retargeting therefore needs objectives and evaluations that can identify the source-conditioned map it claims to learn. Project page: https://cross-skeleton-retargeting.netlify.app/.

</details>


### [137] [Technical note on: Zero-Training Feature-Space Alignment via Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37302)
*Behraj Khan,Tahir Qasim Syed,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZFGA是一种无需训练、闭式的测试时自适应方法，通过Fisher几何对齐提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性，仅需前向传播与矩阵运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
