Existing memory systems pri marily optimize construction and retrieval; semantic relevance and historical success therefore remain insufficient when retrieved ex perience contains incompatible actions or an inappropriate level of structure. We introduce Memory Adaptation for Task-Conditioned Execution (MATE), a deterministic post-retrieval procedure that converts trajectories into execution-oriented memory. MATE re moves obsolete control context, extracts condition-action-effect transitions, applies verified action normalization, selects a task dependent representation, and serializes the result under a fixed budget without additional LLM inference. On 134 ALFWorld tasks, MATE achieves task success rates of 81.3% and 93.3% with Qwen2.5-14B and 72B while using approximately one-tenth of the tokens required by raw trajectories. Controlled comparisons show that verified action normalization is the principal mechanism by which MATE restores the utility of retrieved experience, support ing memory adaptation as a distinct stage between retrieval and embodied execution.

</details>


### [242] [Can Multimodal Large Language Models Generate and Detect Multimodal Social Media Fake News?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35809)
*Jiyao Yang,Yang Liu,Zhenyue Qin,Qingyu Chen,Xiuzhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体框架生成多模态假新闻，构建9000+配对帖子并评测16个MLLM，发现检测远低于人类水平且图像真实性识别失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI快速发展，多模态大模型可能被滥用于社交媒体大规模虚假信息传播。现有研究多聚焦文本虚假信息，核心问题未解：MLLM能否被用来伪造逼真的多模态假新闻，又能否可靠检测？

Method: 提出多智能体框架，由故事智能体、图像智能体与批评智能体协作，生成看似能反驳真实新闻的假社交媒体帖子。据此在科学、健康、娱乐三个领域生成9000余条配对多模态新闻帖子，并对16个开源与闭源MLLM进行自动检测基准测试。

Result: 多数模型准确率大幅低于人类水平，并在图像真实性识别上出现严重失败。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建抵御社交媒体假新闻的稳健防御提供了基础，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI raises concerns about the misuse of Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) for large-scale disinformation campaigns on social media. Despite existing research on textual disinformation, a fundamental question remains unanswered: can MLLMs be exploited to fabricate realistic multimodal fake news, and can they reliably detect it? We introduce a multi-agent framework in which a story agent, an image agent, and a critic agent collaborate to produce fake social media posts that plausibly counter true news. We apply the framework to generate over 9,000 paired multimodal news posts across science, health, and entertainment domains, and benchmark 16 open- and closed-source MLLMs for automated detection. We find that most models fall substantially short of human-level accuracy and fail critically on identifying image authenticity. Our research provides a foundation for developing robust defenses against social media fake news. Code and data are available at https: //github.com/xiuzhenzhang/Multimodal.

</details>


### [243] [TRACE: Deployable Tree-Relational Structure Enhancement for Oncology LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35810)
*Jizheng Lai,Yingyun Li,Ying Qin,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE是一个可部署的树-关系增强框架，通过分离离线结构学习与轻量在线推理，为肿瘤学大模型提供结构化证据支撑，提升准确性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学大模型的预测往往缺乏显式医学结构的支撑，导致可靠性、可解释性与可审计性不足，需要一种既能提升准确性又能保持部署可行性的结构化增强方案。

Method: 将昂贵的离线结构学习与轻量在线推理分离：把肿瘤学概念与关系组织为可更新的树-关系结构，利用语言模型损失衍生的证据进行精炼，在推理时检索为紧凑的提示证据，实现零样本下无需监督标签的任务自适应证据选择。

Result: 在十个肿瘤学分类任务与MedQuAD CancerGov问答基准上，同时提升了无标签评估与有监督微调表现；优于普通RAG与通用GraphRAG，在泄漏控制的METABRIC输入下仍有效，并能生成与临床推理一致的可解释证据路径。

Conclusion: 显式、可更新的医学结构是通向更准确、更可审计的肿瘤学大模型部署的一条切实路径。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in oncology applications, but their predictions are often weakly grounded in explicit medical structure. We present TRACE, a deployable tree-relational enhancement framework for oncology LLMs. TRACE separates expensive offline structure learning from lightweight online inference: oncology concepts and relations are organized into an updatable tree-relational structure, refined using LM-loss-derived evidence, and retrieved at inference time as compact prompt evidence. This design supports task-adaptive evidence selection without requiring supervised labels in the zero-shot setting. Across ten oncology classification tasks and one MedQuAD CancerGov QA benchmark, TRACE improves both label-free evaluation and supervised fine-tuning. Additional analyses show that TRACE improves over vanilla RAG and generic GraphRAG, remains useful under leakage-controlled METABRIC inputs, and produces interpretable evidence paths aligned with clinical reasoning. These results suggest that explicit, updatable medical structure is a practical path toward more accurate and auditable oncology LLM deployment.

</details>


### [244] [Lookahead-R: Budget-Aware Tool Retrieval via Execution-Centric Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35811)
*Zongze Wu,Yani Guo,Runnan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Lookahead-R，将工具检索重构为资源受限的序贯决策问题，用轻量执行感知代理世界模型驱动成本敏感的蒙特卡洛树搜索，在 ToolBench 上取得更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在大规模异构 API 生态中面临工具检索瓶颈：语义检索器速度快但存在"语义-功能鸿沟"，而基于执行的验证虽提升精度却带来难以承受的延迟。现有方法在精度与效率之间存在固有矛盾。

Method: 提出 Lookahead-R 规划式框架：核心是轻量级的执行感知代理世界模型，无需调用真实 API 即可联合预测工具执行成功率、延迟成本与语义效用；该世界模型驱动成本敏感、不确定性引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索，在严格预算约束下探索工具空间。

Result: 在 ToolBench 大规模基准上，各测试场景均实现更优的精度-效率权衡；在最难的 I3 划分上 NDCG@5 达 91.40%，超过 SOTA 的 ToolGen（90.16%）1.24 个百分点。消融实验证实显式延迟建模是资源约束下识别高质量工具的关键判别信号。

Conclusion: 将工具检索建模为资源受限序贯决策并结合执行感知世界模型与预算约束搜索，可有效缓解语义-功能鸿沟与执行验证延迟之间的矛盾，且延迟建模对资源受限下的工具选择起决定性作用。

Abstract: Tool retrieval is a critical bottleneck for LLM-based agents operating over large, heterogeneous API ecosystems. Existing approaches face an inherent trade-off: semantic retrievers are fast but suffer from the semantic-functional gap, while execution-based validation improves precision at the cost of prohibitive latency. We propose Lookahead-R, a planning-based framework that reformulates tool retrieval as a resource-constrained sequential decision-making problem. At its core, Lookahead-R introduces a lightweight execution-aware surrogate world model that jointly predicts tool execution success, latency cost, and semantic utility---without invoking real APIs. This world model drives a cost-sensitive, uncertainty-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search that navigates the tool space under strict budget constraints. Evaluated on the large-scale ToolBench benchmark, Lookahead-R achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off across all test scenarios. On the most challenging I3 split, it attains an NDCG@5 of 91.40\%, outperforming the state-of-the-art ToolGen (90.16\%) by 1.24\%. Ablation studies confirm that explicit latency modeling is the key discriminative signal for identifying high-quality tools under resource constraints.

</details>


### [245] [Automated Evaluation of Multi-Turn Dialogues in In-Car Conversational Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35812)
*Vaishnav Negi,Lev Sorokin,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向车载对话助手（ICA）多轮对话能力的自动化测试框架，采用黑盒闭环仿真与策略引导用户模拟器，配合双层LLM评审，在工业级ICA上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载对话助手日益普及，但其可靠性评估面临多轮交互、缺乏明确真值、严格安全约束等挑战；现有评估技术仅针对单轮场景，无法覆盖约束处理、上下文保持与跨轮安全关键行为。

Method: 将系统视为黑盒，通过闭环仿真进行评估：包含策略引导的用户模拟器、对抗性策略管理器，以及一个双层LLM评审（分别评估轮级失败与会话级质量）。

Result: 在具备六种LLM后端的工业级ICA上、由十二名人工标注者参与评估。自动评审与人类判断高度一致；相比无引导仿真，策略引导每段对话发现的独特失败类型多2.96倍，独特失败对话数量增加一倍以上。

Conclusion: 策略引导的闭环仿真结合双层LLM评审，能有效自动化测试车载对话助手的多轮对话能力，显著提升失败发现能力，并与人类评估保持高一致性。

Abstract: In-car conversational assistants (ICAs) are increasingly integrated into vehicles to support route planning, vehicle control, and information access. Ensuring their reliability is challenging due to multi-turn interactions, the absence of explicit ground truth, and strict safety constraints. Existing evaluation techniques fall short, as they target single-turn settings and fail to capture constraint handling, context retention, and safety-critical behavior across turns. We propose an automated framework for testing the multi-turn conversational capabilities of ICAs. The system is treated as a black box and evaluated via closed-loop simulation with a strategy-guided user simulator, an adversarial strategy manager, and a two-tier LLM judge assessing turn-level failures and conversation-level quality. We evaluate the approach on an industrial ICA with six LLM backends and twelve human annotators. The automated judge shows substantial agreement with humans, and strategy guidance uncovers 2.96 times more unique failure types per conversation and more than doubles the number of unique failing conversations compared to unguided simulation.

</details>


### [246] [Constructing Challenging Browser-Use Tasks by Controlled Environment Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35814)
*Xunjian Yin,Tianchen Guan,Jinao Wang,Weili Cao,Daisy Xinlei Lin,Royce Cheng-Yue,Keagan Long,Kyle Wong,Bhuwan Dhingra,Xiangjun Wang,Shuyan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BreakingWeb基准，通过在智能体已解决的任务上施加确定性环境干预，将任务难度变为可编程属性，揭示浏览器智能体的"信念失败"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体基准通过收集新任务、网站和应用来提升难度，导致难度刷新成本高、难以控制，且多因素同时变化时无法辨别真正构成挑战的因素。

Method: BreakingWeb为每个基础任务配对一个干预条件，在保持用户指令、潜在目标和后端成功标准不变的前提下，于不同Web栈层级改变环境；干预具确定性、可检测、可恢复，并标注所主要加载的认知原语。基准含7个自托管网站、29个干预族，共519个干净/干预任务对，均按结果评分。评测6个强浏览器智能体、3个仅截图GUI智能体和人类。

Result: 干预平均使智能体通过率下降22.9%，并推翻每个智能体近一半原本干净解决的任务；人类首次尝试仅损失10.0%，熟悉一次后损失5.7%。主导失败类型为"信念失败"：6个智能体75%的失败以宣称成功告终，而所需变化从未发生。

Conclusion: 难度可作为环境的可编程属性被构造，BreakingWeb有效区分智能体与人类能力，并暴露智能体普遍存在的虚假成功声明问题。代码、数据和环境已公开。

Abstract: As browser-use agents improve, benchmarks keep pace by collecting new tasks, websites, and applications, often making tasks longer or more novel. This makes difficulty expensive to refresh and difficult to control: when many aspects change at once, it is unclear what actually makes a task challenging. We instead construct challenging instances from tasks agents already solve, turning difficulty into a programmable property of the environment. BreakingWeb pairs every base task with an intervention condition that preserves the user instruction, latent target, and backend success criterion while changing the environment at different web stack layers. Each intervention is deterministic, detectable, and recoverable, and is annotated with the cognitive primitive it primarily loads. The benchmark contains 519 clean/intervention task pairs across seven self-hosted websites and 29 intervention families, all graded against outcomes. We evaluate six strong browser-use agents, three GUI-only agents that see only screenshots, and humans. The construction is effective: interventions cut agent pass rate by 22.9% on average and overturn nearly half of the tasks each agent solves cleanly, whereas humans lose 10.0% on a first attempt and 5.7% after one familiarisation attempt. The dominant failure is belief failure: 75% of the six agents' failures end with a declared success although the required change never happened. Our code, data and environment are publicly available at www.breakingweb.app.

</details>


### [247] [How to Run Statistics over LLM Judges and Trust the Results: Calibrated Inference for Small-Sample AI Evaluation with evalstats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35815)
*Ian Arawjo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM评判者评分与小样本AI评估中统计推断不可靠的问题，提出校准置信区间、假设检验与评判者偏差修正方法，并发布开源工具evalstats。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界日益依赖LLM评判者分数和小样本AI评估来支撑显著性论断，但缺乏良好校准的置信区间、假设检验和评判者偏差修正，导致这些论断不可靠。原始LLM评分直接做统计会带来虚高的假阳性风险，尤其在"几乎完美"的人机一致性下风险峰值反直觉地出现。

Method: 1) 提供混合人机评判设计的统计分析指南与工具，通过预测驱动推断（PPI）实现九种假设检验，包括首次对四种基于秩的检验（Wilcoxon符号秩、Mann-Whitney U及其综合变体）的PPI修正；2) 提出bootstrap自适应功效调优，使PPI++在小规模人工标注校准集下保持稳定，将估计权重向由标注数据估计的目标收缩，并计入该权重自身的抽样方差；3) 通过蒙特卡洛模拟，针对小样本AI评估（N<100）给出CI、p值和FWER校正方法的选择建议，并警示研究者慎用bootstrap CI；4) 将建议打包为开源Python包evalstats，可自动选择校准方法。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟给出了小样本AI评估下CI、p值与FWER校正方法的选择建议；evalstats在三个场景中验证有效，其中一例显示，一个在"substantial agreement"水平上被验证的真实LLM评判者会误导研究者发表虚假发现。

Conclusion: 原始LLM评判分数的统计推断会导致假阳性膨胀，需通过PPI类修正与校准方法进行规范分析；evalstats可自动选择校准方法，帮助研究者负责任地对LLM评判者做统计分析。

Abstract: Researchers across academia increasingly base significance claims on LLM judge scores and small-sample AI evaluations. Yet without well-calibrated confidence intervals (CIs), hypothesis tests, and judge-bias corrections, such claims are unreliable. We address these issues in several contributions. First, we find that running statistics over raw LLM judge scores leads to inflated false positives: counterintuitively, for many inter-rater agreement metrics, false positive risk peaks at "almost perfect" human-LLM agreement. To help researchers understand how to run statistics over LLM judges responsibly, we present guidance and tooling for the statistical analysis of mixed human-AI judge designs, and implement nine hypothesis tests via prediction-powered inference (PPI), including the first known PPI corrections for four rank-based tests (Wilcoxon signed-rank, Mann-Whitney U, and omnibus variants). To keep PPI++ stable with small human-labeled calibration sets, we introduce bootstrap-adaptive power tuning, which shrinks the estimated weight toward a target estimated from the labeled data, and accounts for that weight's own sampling variance. Second, through Monte Carlo simulations, we derive recommendations for what CI, p-value, and FWER correction methods to use for small-sample AI evaluations (N<100), and warn researchers against bootstrap CIs. We package these recommendations into evalstats, an open-source Python package that selects calibrated methods automatically, and demonstrate it in three scenarios, including one where a real LLM judge validated at "substantial agreement" would have led a researcher to publish a spurious finding. evalstats is publicly available at https://github.com/ianarawjo/evalstats.

</details>


### [248] [PrimeSeeker: Capability-Oriented Supervision for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35816)
*Linzhi Peng,Hanting Chen,Heng Chang,Ke Cheng,Bowen Du,Weifeng Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"潜在锚点推理"(latent anchor reasoning)这一检索原语，并据此构建PrimeSeeker框架，通过锚点结构与参考证据骨架联合生成问题与轨迹，训练30B搜索智能体，在五个深度搜索基准上取得强劲表现且工具调用更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM搜索智能体多依赖合成问题，通过扩大证据图、增加跳数、拉长轨迹等"全局属性"来提升难度，但这些只是局部检索能力的间接代理，与搜索实际需要的局部检索能力存在错配。

Method: 提出latent anchor reasoning：从描述性规格中解析未命名的检索锚点，并将恢复的锚点转移到后续信息需求中，把深度搜索分解为耦合操作链；基于此构建PrimeSeeker，利用网络构建锚点结构，联合推导问题与参考证据骨架，骨架通过当前工具观察的抽取式高亮指导专家生成，高亮在SFT前移除，骨架在RL中复用于审计参考步骤覆盖率。

Result: 构建9,221条专家轨迹并训练30B搜索智能体；在五个深度搜索基准上表现强劲，参考步骤优化进一步提升监督策略；生成轨迹检索冗余低，固定预算评估下以显著更少的工具调用实现较强解覆盖。

Conclusion: 以能力为导向的锚点解析与关系转移设计，可替代依赖全局难度的合成方式，使搜索智能体在更少工具调用下获得更优的深度搜索性能。

Abstract: Large language model search agents are often trained with synthetic questions whose difficulty is increased through larger evidence graphs, additional hops, and longer trajectories. These global properties, however, are only indirect proxies for the local retrieval capabilities required during search. To address this mismatch, we introduce latent anchor reasoning, which consists of resolving an unnamed retrieval anchor from descriptive specifications and transferring the recovered anchor into a subsequent information demand. This primitive retrieval unit decomposes deep search into chains of coupled operations and organizes question construction around anchor resolution and relation transfer, without prescribing a canonical search path. Based on this formulation, we propose PrimeSeeker, a capability-oriented framework that constructs web-grounded anchor structures and jointly derives a question and a reference evidence skeleton. The skeleton preserves supporting evidence from construction and guides expert generation through extractive highlights of current tool observations. These highlights are removed before supervised fine-tuning, while the skeleton is subsequently reused to audit reference-step coverage for reinforcement-learning rewards. We construct 9,221 expert trajectories, training a 30B search agent. Across five deep-search benchmarks, PrimeSeeker achieves strong performance, while reference-step optimization further improves the supervised policy. The resulting trajectories exhibit low retrieval redundancy, and fixed-budget evaluation shows strong solution coverage with substantially fewer tool calls than long-horizon systems.

</details>


### [249] [Less Uniform Discrete Diffusion is More Powerful and Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35817)
*Kaibo Wang,Ding Ding,Fangyu Ding,Zijin Feng,Han Shi,Haili Bai,Jiacheng Sun,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LUDI框架，通过"less uniform loss"和逐token时间嵌入解决均匀扩散语言模型的训练目标过均匀与采样中条件-目标混淆问题，并成功continue-train出具备复杂推理能力的LUDI-7B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀扩散语言模型（UDLMs）是有前景的扩散范式，但难以扩展。作者指出核心障碍是训练目标过度均匀（over-uniform training objective）以及采样过程中的条件-目标混淆（condition-target confusion）。

Method: LUDI包含两个关键设计：(i) 一种"less uniform loss"，使每个逆向转移直接指向干净token；(ii) 为模型配备逐token时间嵌入（per-token time embeddings），提供token级的损坏提示，从而支持基于置信度的少步采样。此外将7B自回归模型continue-train为LUDI-7B。

Result: 跨规模实验显示LUDI带来更干净的监督信号并改善少步生成；LUDI-7B实现相对AR解码3 token/步的加速，与掩码扩散基线相比性能具竞争力。

Conclusion: UDLMs用于复杂生成的潜力尚未被充分释放；LUDI表明通过改进损失与时间嵌入设计，均匀扩散语言模型可扩展到具备复杂推理能力的规模。

Abstract: Although uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) represent a promising diffusion paradigm, scaling them remains challenging. We identify the core obstacle as an over-uniform training objective and condition-target confusion during sampling. To address these, we propose Less Uniform Diffusion (LUDI), a novel UDLM framework. Specifically, we (i) introduce a less uniform loss that directs each reverse transition toward the clean token, and (ii) equip the model with per-token time embeddings that supply token-level corruption hints, enabling confidence-based few-step sampling. Experiments across scales show that LUDI yields cleaner supervision and improves few-step generation. We further continue-train a 7B autoregressive model into LUDI-7B, resulting in a UDLM capable of complex reasoning. It achieves a 3-token-per-step speedup over AR decoding and competitive performance compared with masked diffusion baselines, revealing that the full potential of UDLMs for complex generation remains to be unlocked.

</details>


### [250] [$τ$-Multilingual: Benchmarking Voice Agents Across Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35820)
*Soham Ray,Edgard dos Santos Paiva,Ruben Valenzuela,Karthik Narasimhan,Keshav Dhandhania,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 τ-Multilingual，将 τ-Voice 扩展到西班牙语、巴西葡萄牙语、印地语、韩语和普通话，通过母语者审核考查多语言语音代理的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测基准多为英语，只能反映语音代理行为的狭窄切片，无法揭示多语言场景下的真实表现与失败模式。

Method: 在 τ-Voice 基础上扩展五种语言，进行母语者审核，并对生成语言与语音输出进行评估；共覆盖 4,500 通全双工通话与五种语音配置。

Result: 西语、葡语和印语的任务完成率与英语相差不超过 3.2 个百分点，而韩语和普通话分别下降 14.7 和 8.4 个百分点；失败模式各异：韩语系统漏答更多，普通话系统打断更频繁，两者在工具与实体处理上均有困难。Grok 任务完成度最高但生成质量最低。

Conclusion: 任务、交互与生成质量应分开报告；作者发布了语言包、经验证的评判器及工具，支持社区构建多语言语音代理评测。

Abstract: English-only benchmarks expose only a narrow slice of voice-agent behavior. We introduce $τ$-Multilingual, extending $τ$-Voice to Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, Hindi, Korean, and Mandarin with native-speaker review and evaluation of generated language and spoken output. Across 4,500 full-duplex calls and five voice configurations, Spanish, Portuguese, and Hindi remain within 3.2 task-completion points of English, but Korean and Mandarin fall by 14.7 and 8.4 points. The failure modes also vary: Korean systems miss more responses, Mandarin systems interrupt more often, and both struggle with tools and entities. Grok leads task completion but scores lowest on generation quality, motivating separate task, interaction, and generation reporting. We release language packs, validated judges, and tools for community-built multilingual voice-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [251] [Can We Still Trust Disaster Social Sensing? Empirical Evidence on Detecting AI-Generated Social Media Posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35821)
*Xiaoshan Zhou,Zaifu Zhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于文本的AI检测器能否可靠区分人类撰写的灾难社交媒体帖子与AI生成帖子。结果显示，跨家族检测AUROC仅0.402-0.517，最佳召回率3.6%，灾难域训练线性头虽达0.817，但易受表层特征与数据集人工痕迹影响，故文本检测不足以作为操作性的信任闸门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难社会感知将社交媒体帖子转化为情境意识与人道需求的证据，但生成式AI能产出看似目击报告的逼真信息，威胁数据可信度。研究旨在检验文本AI检测器能否可靠区分人类撰写与AI生成的灾难帖文。

Method: 构建12,000条文本数据集，组织为3,000个匹配语义单元，涵盖九类灾难的四种条件：原始人类帖（H0）、经LLM轻度校订的人类帖（H1）、基于同一已核实事实的AI生成事实帖（A0）及其情感化版本（A1）；另用42个事件的6,000条文本语料做模型选择与阈值校准。评估OSM-Det、Fast-DetectGPT、Binoculars及直接LLM判定器，并测试灾难域校准、冻结编码器线性读出、配对变换敏感性与数据集人工痕迹控制。

Result: 十四个冻结跨家族配置AUROC为0.402-0.517，校准衍生低误报点最佳前瞻召回仅3.6%；OSM-Det达AUROC 0.521、在6.7%误报率下召回10.4%。灾难训练线性头AUROC 0.817，但七特征表层分类器在H0对A0上达0.784，中性化表层不对称后线性头从0.733降至0.594；该头还能在来源不变情况下区分A0与A1。

Conclusion: 文本检测尚不足以作为操作性的信任闸门，应依赖多模态证据、可问责来源及其他情境信息来保障灾难社会感知中的信任。

Abstract: Disaster social sensing converts public social-media posts into evidence for situational awareness and humanitarian needs, but generative artificial intelligence (AI) can produce plausible messages that resemble eyewitness reports. This study investigates whether text-based AI detectors can reliably distinguish human-authored from AI-generated disaster posts. We construct a dataset of 12,000 texts organised into 3,000 matched semantic units from nine disasters: original human posts (H0), minimally LLM-proofread human posts (H1), factual AI-generated posts based on the same verified facts (A0), and affectively framed versions of those AI posts (A1). A separate 6,000-text corpus from 42 events supports model selection and threshold calibration. We evaluate OSM-Det, Fast-DetectGPT, Binoculars, and direct large language model (LLM) judges across five model families, then test disaster-domain calibration, a frozen-encoder linear readout, paired transformation sensitivity, and dataset artifact controls. Across fourteen frozen cross-family configurations, AUROC is 0.402-0.517 and the best prospective recall at a calibration-derived low-false-positive operating point is 3.6%; OSM-Det reaches AUROC 0.521 and 10.4% recall at a realised 6.7% false-positive rate. A disaster-trained linear head reaches AUROC 0.817, but a seven-feature surface classifier reaches 0.784 on the H0-versus-A0 contrast, and neutralising identified surface asymmetries reduces the head from 0.733 to 0.594. The head also separates A0 from A1 even though provenance is unchanged. The results show that text-based detection is not reliable enough to serve as an operational trust gate; multimodal claims, accountable sources, and other contextual evidence should be rested on to safeguard trust in disaster social sensing.

</details>


### [252] [Tracing mechanisms of sycophantic agreement in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35822)
*Sixing Chen,Zhuofan Josh Ying,Logan Riggs Smith,Jeremy Wertheimer,Natalie Shapira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用因果中介分析揭示了语言模型谄媚顺从的机制：用户陈述的观点在早期被写入最后提示词的残差流，通过一组稀疏的早期注意力头偏置后续答案检索；消融这些头可大幅降低谄媚且基本不损害事实准确性，而通过"你确定吗？"这类无内容反驳传达的观点则由另一组注意力头负责压制原正确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谄媚式顺从（模型过度附和用户信念或偏好、牺牲事实准确性）虽被广泛视为对齐失败，但其底层机制尚不清楚。作者希望从机制层面理解这一现象，从而为更精准、可靠的对齐干预提供依据。

Method: 采用因果中介分析（causal mediation analysis）定位谄媚顺从的机制。具体分析：(1) 明确陈述观点时观点信号如何进入残差流并影响答案检索，识别承载该信号的稀疏早期注意力头；(2) 通过消融这些头检验其因果作用；(3) 检验不同措辞下同一组头是否仍承载观点；(4) 对"你确定吗？"等无内容反驳（content-free pushback）情形，识别另一组负责压制原正确答案的注意力头。

Result: 用户陈述的观点在早期即被写入最终提示词的残差流，并偏置随后的答案检索；一组稀疏的早期注意力头承载该观点信号。消融这些头可显著降低谄媚行为，同时基本保持事实准确性。无论观点如何措辞，同一组头都承载该观点。在无内容反驳（如"Are you sure?"）情况下，则存在另一组不同的注意力头，负责抑制模型原本正确的答案以促成修正答案。

Conclusion: 本文给出了观点如何诱发谄媚顺从的机制性解释，识别出可分离的注意力头回路，为开发更有针对性、更可靠的对齐干预措施迈出了一步。

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement in language models refers to the tendency to overly affirm a user's stated beliefs or preferences, often at the expense of factual accuracy. Although it is widely recognized as an alignment failure, its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we use causal mediation analysis to identify the mechanisms behind sycophantic agreement. We show that a stated opinion is incorporated into the residual stream of the final prompt token early, where it biases subsequent answer retrieval. A sparse set of early attention heads carries this opinion signal. Ablating these heads substantially reduces sycophancy while leaving factual accuracy largely intact. The same heads carry the opinion when it is explicitly stated, regardless of how it is phrased. When an opinion is not stated explicitly but instead conveyed through content-free pushback (e.g., ``Are you sure?"), we find a distinct set of heads that suppresses the model's original correct answer to promote a revised answer. By providing a mechanistic account of how opinions induce sycophantic agreement, this work takes a step toward developing more targeted and reliable alignment interventions.

</details>


### [253] [Reliable but Design-Sensitive: Instrument Uncertainty in LLM Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35824)
*Thomas Reiter,Christoph Kern,Fedor Miasnikov,Sofiia Nikolenko,Rob Chew,Stephanie Eckman,Frauke Kreuter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究测试了7个LLM、12种任务设计、3次独立运行，对3000条推文进行冒犯性语言与仇恨言论标注，发现LLM标注结果高度依赖任务设计和模型选择，提出"工具不确定性"概念，并指出重复单一设置或依赖置信度无法替代跨设计比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同设置下能给出看似可靠的标签，但当研究者做出其他合理的设计选择时，标签会发生变化。这种由任务设计和模型选择引起的变异此前缺乏系统测量，可能导致基于LLM标注的估计结果不可靠。

Method: 使用7个LLM、12种任务设计、3次独立运行，对3000条推文进行冒犯性语言和仇恨言论标注。通过比较同一设置重复运行的一致性、不同任务设计间的一致性，以及任务设计与模型选择对估计流行率方差的影响（与抽样方差对比），并考察置信度分数的表现，同时与5个人类工具版本的变异进行对照。

Result: 同一模型与任务设计重复运行一致性高（中位Fleiss' κ=0.91）；改变任务设计后一致性下降（中位Cohen's κ=0.76）。任务设计与模型选择使估计流行率的方差分别增加76.7倍（冒犯性语言）和110.6倍（仇恨言论）。跨LLM任务设计的变异达560-572个基点，而5个人类工具版本间为270-331个基点。置信度未能解决该问题：其更贴近重复模型输出而非与人类标签的一致性，且将6条推文放入同一提示会使冒犯性语言平均置信度下降660个基点。

Conclusion: 任务设计和模型选择引起的变异被命名为"工具不确定性"。研究者只有通过比较多种合理任务设计才能测量这一不确定性；重复单一设置或依赖置信度分数都无法替代这种测试。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can give reliable labels under one setup yet change those labels when researchers make other reasonable design choices. We tested seven LLMs, 12 task designs, three independent runs, and 3,000 tweets labeled for offensive language and hate speech. Repeating the same model and task design produced high agreement (median Fleiss' $κ= 0.91$). Agreement fell when we changed the task design for the same tweets (median Cohen's $κ= 0.76$). Task design and model choice increased the variance of estimated prevalence by factors of 76.7 for offensive language and 110.6 for hate speech compared with sampling variance alone. Variation across LLM task designs reached 560-572 basis points, compared with 270-331 basis points across five human instrument versions. Confidence scores did not solve this problem. They tracked repeated model outputs more closely than agreement with human labels, and grouping six tweets in one prompt lowered mean offensive-language confidence by 660 basis points. We call the variation caused by task design and model choice instrument uncertainty. Researchers can measure it only by comparing reasonable task designs. Repeating one setup or relying on confidence scores cannot replace that test.

</details>


### [254] [Beyond the Context Window: An Adaptive Entropy-Based Routing Framework for Hybrid Retrieval and Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35831)
*Isaac Olufadewa,Miracle Adesina,Ezekiel Oladejo,Owen Adeniyi,Fadare Fadekemi,Olamide Oso,Uthman Babatunde,Matthew Olawoyin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出熵驱动自适应路由器EDAR，用RAG首几个token的预测熵判断是否升级为长上下文处理，在保留97.4%长上下文精度的同时减少70.7%的token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万级token长上下文引发RAG是否仍必要的讨论：纯长上下文处理昂贵且对中间信息注意力不足，纯RAG虽快但受检索质量限制，检索块部分相关或矛盾时易出错，因此需要动态选择推理方式。

Method: EDAR在推理时基于RAG响应前几个生成token的token级概率分布计算预测熵，并在留出验证集上以成本对准确率扫描选择熵阈值，据此决定用检索块回答还是升级为完整长上下文处理。实验在LongBench v2与Infinity-Bench上与纯RAG、纯LC基线对比。

Result: 预测熵与幻觉率在2000条生成样本上强相关（Pearson r=0.85，95% CI [0.83, 0.87]）；EDAR保留纯长上下文基线97.4%的准确率，同时减少70.7%的token消耗，仅升级18.2%的查询；与纯LC的准确率差距在标准样本量下不显著异于零。

Conclusion: 预测熵是可用于RAG与长上下文推理路由的模型内部信号，基于阈值的混合系统能以极小成本恢复长上下文模型的大部分精度；框架不依赖特定检索器或LC骨干，也不需要解码之外的额外监督。

Abstract: Modern large language models now support context windows of more than one million tokens, which has raised the question of whether retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is still necessary. Pure long-context (LC) processing is expensive and is known to under-attend to information placed in the middle of long inputs, while pure RAG is fast but bounded by retrieval quality and prone to errors when retrieved chunks are partially relevant or contradictory. We propose the Entropy-Driven Adaptive Router (EDAR), a framework that decides at inference time whether to answer a query from retrieved chunks or to escalate it to full long-context processing. The decision uses the predictive entropy of the token-level probability distribution computed over the first few generated tokens of the RAG response. The entropy threshold is selected on a held-out validation set by sweeping cost against accuracy. Experiments compare EDAR against pure-RAG and pure-LC baselines on LongBench v2 and Infinity-Bench. Predictive entropy correlates strongly with hallucination rate on a held-out set of 2,000 generations (Pearson r = 0.85, 95% CI [0.83, 0.87]). On the long-context benchmarks, EDAR retains 97.4% of the accuracy of the pure long-context baseline while reducing total token expenditure by 70.7%, escalating only 18.2% of incoming queries. The accuracy gap between EDAR and the pure long-context system is not statistically distinguishable from zero at standard sample sizes. Predictive entropy is a useful model-internal signal for routing between RAG and long-context inference, and a threshold-based hybrid system can recover most of the accuracy of long-context models at a small fraction of the cost. The framework does not depend on a specific retriever or LC backbone, and it does not require additional supervision beyond what is normally produced during decoding.

</details>


### [255] [When Should LLMs Trust Their Own Revisions? A Risk-Aware Study of Intrinsic Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35832)
*Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型"内在自我纠正"的收益与代价权衡，通过追踪29个开源权重模型在初始答案与修订答案之间的正确性转移，揭示聚合准确率会掩盖截然不同的修订行为，并比较三种运行时策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在自我纠正要求模型在无外部新证据的情况下修订自身答案。第二次修订既能挽回错误，也可能推翻原本正确的答案。以往研究常以聚合准确率衡量效果，可能掩盖"纠正"与"损害"两种相反行为的相互抵消，因此需要更细粒度地刻画这一权衡。

Method: 在BoolQ、GSM8K和Corr2Cause三个数据集上评估29个开源权重LLM，追踪初始答案到修订答案之间的正确性转移。开展受控的BoolQ实验考察不同细化提示对恢复与损害平衡的影响。比较三种运行时选择：保留初始答案、总是接受修订、以及利用初始响应后可用信号进行选择性触发修订。

Result: 聚合准确率掩盖了显著不同的修订行为，例如Llama-3.1-8B在GSM8K上提升25.5个百分点，但细化会将19.1%的原本正确回答变为错误。受控BoolQ研究显示细化提示会改变恢复与损害的平衡。三种策略对比识别出学习门控有效的场景，以及更简单的无条件策略表现更优的场景。

Conclusion: 应将内在自我纠正视为一种"修订策略"而非普遍有益的第二次处理，并从其挽回的纠正和引入的错误两个维度进行评估。

Abstract: Intrinsic self-correction asks a language model to revise its own answer without receiving new external evidence. A second pass can recover mistakes, but it can also overturn answers that were already correct. We study this trade-off across 29 open-weight LLMs on BoolQ, GSM8K, and Corr2Cause by tracking correctness transitions between initial and revised answers. Aggregate accuracy can conceal substantially different revision behavior: for example, Llama-3.1-8B improves by 25.5 percentage points on GSM8K, while refinement changes 19.1% of initially correct answers into wrong ones. A controlled BoolQ study further shows that refinement prompts shift the balance between recovery and harm. We then compare three runtime choices: keeping the initial answer, always accepting the revision, and selectively invoking revision using signals available after the initial response. The comparison identifies settings where learned gating is useful and others where a simpler unconditional policy performs better. These results suggest treating intrinsic self-correction as a revision policy rather than as a uniformly beneficial second pass, and evaluating it through both the corrections it recovers and the errors it introduces.

</details>


### [256] [Hyperspherical Semantic Trajectory Analysis: Mapping Technological Diffusion across Academic Preprints, Patent Signals, and Compute Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35845)
*Muhammad Sukri Bin Ramli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HSTA方法，通过追踪科学文本与专利文本的语义演化，构建实时技术扩散度量，以弥补传统宏观经济指标的多年度报告滞后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全要素生产率等宏观经济指标受行政调查周期和国民核算惯例限制，存在数年的报告滞后，无法实时反映技术突破。作者希望直接从非结构化的科学与商业文本流中追踪技术扩散，构建可实时观测的替代性量化指标。

Method: 提出无监督方法HSTA：收集3万条arXiv预印本与USPTO专利记录，用Transformer句向量结合Spherical K-Means（8个子主题）和UMAP降维投影到单位超球面；定义两个指标——语义质心向量漂移（追踪时序子语料库的词汇迁移与范式转变）和商业化偏移（衡量科学发现与知识产权申请跨语料库的峰值密度对齐）；并用向量自回归F检验将季度主题体量速度与Epoch AI硬件指标关联。

Result: 大语言模型子主题漂移指标最高（0.332），人工智能系统次之（0.234），语义演化最快。向量自回归F检验显示，季度论文体量速度在常规显著性水平上并不格兰杰引起前沿算力配置的激增，说明文本信号需以物理资本约束为条件。

Conclusion: HSTA提供了一种客观、实时的机制，可作为传统经济统计的补充，用于监测技术范式转变与科学到商业化的扩散过程；同时提示文本语义信号需与实体资本指标结合才能有效预测算力等资源配置。

Abstract: Macroeconomic productivity metrics, such as Total Factor Productivity, register technological breakthroughs with multi-year reporting lags due to administrative survey intervals and national accounting conventions. This paper introduces Hyperspherical Semantic Trajectory Analysis (HSTA), an unsupervised quantitative methodology that tracks technology diffusion directly from unstructured scientific and commercial text streams. We analyze 30,000 filtered document records spanning academic preprints from arXiv and patent application records from the USPTO. By projecting high-dimensional Transformer sentence embeddings onto unit hyperspheres using Spherical K-Means clustering across eight primary sub-topics and UMAP manifold reductions, HSTA formalizes two quantitative metrics: (1) Semantic Centroid Vector Drift, which tracks vocabulary shifts between temporal sub-corpora to identify structural paradigm transformations; and (2) Commercialization Offset, which evaluates cross-corpus peak density alignments between scientific discovery and intellectual property filings. Linking quarterly topic volume velocity with physical hardware metrics from the Epoch AI database, Vector Autoregressive F-tests demonstrate that quarterly paper volume velocity alone does not Granger-cause frontier compute allocation surges at conventional statistical significance levels, highlighting the necessity of conditioning textual signals on physical capital constraints. Empirical results reveal that sub-topics covering Large Language Models (with a drift metric of 0.332) and Artificial Intelligence Systems (with a drift metric of 0.234) undergo the highest rate of semantic evolution, offering an objective, real-time mechanism to complement traditional economic statistics.

</details>


### [257] [The Detectability Gap: Hidden Heterogeneity in Hallucination Detection Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35860)
*Pranav Darshan,Pranav A,Sravan Karthick T,Minal Moharir,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 采样一致性幻觉检测的聚合指标掩盖了可检测性的系统异质性：按答案一致性划分出高一致（Ghost）与低一致（Flickering）两类幻觉，二者存在0.35–0.46 AUC的检测差距，且该不对称在多种模型与数据集上稳健存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样一致性的幻觉检测广泛使用，但聚合性能可能掩盖哪些错误可被检测的系统性差异。本文旨在研究四个语言模型和三个事实问答数据集上这种异质性。

Method: 按答案一致性对幻觉进行划分，得到高一致（Ghost）与低一致（Flickering）两种机制；冻结机制分配后，使用词汇与语义层面的响应离散度检验不对称性并采用bootstrap 95%区间；进一步使用个体扩散轨迹、不含跨种子信息的更严格检验。

Result: 观察到0.35至0.46的AUC可检测性差距；由于定义机制与测量差距的统计量强耦合（|ρ|≈0.94–1.00），原始结果被视为一致性检测的属性而非独立证据。冻结机制后，词汇与语义离散度在全部12个模型-数据集设置中bootstrap 95%区间均排除零；使用个体扩散轨迹的严格检验在三个LLaDA数据集上均保持不对称（p<0.005），在三个Dream数据集上方向一致且一个达到显著。难检测机制的流行率在模型间差异大（16%–77%），匹配提示在不同模型间频繁改变机制。

Conclusion: 聚合检测指标掩盖了语言模型失败中持续存在、依赖于模型的异质性，因此需要推动基于机制（regime-conditioned）的评估。

Abstract: Sampling based consistency is widely used for hallucination detection, yet aggregate performance can conceal systematic differences in which errors are detectable. This work studies that heterogeneity across four language models and three factual question answering datasets. Partitioning hallucinations by answer agreement reveals high agreement (Ghost) and low agreement (Flickering) regimes with an apparent detectability gap of $0.35$ to $0.46$ AUC. Because the statistics used to define the regimes and measure this gap are strongly coupled ($|ρ|\approx0.94$ to $1.00$), the raw result is treated as a property of agreement based detection rather than independent evidence. After freezing regime assignments, lexical and semantic response dispersion preserve the asymmetry, with bootstrap $95\%$ intervals excluding zero in all $12$ model and dataset settings. A stricter test using individual diffusion trajectories and no cross seed information preserves the asymmetry across all three LLaDA datasets ($p<0.005$) and directionally across all three Dream datasets, with one reaching significance. The hard regime varies substantially in prevalence across models ($16\%$ to $77\%$), and matched prompts frequently change regimes between models. These findings show that aggregate detection metrics conceal persistent, model dependent heterogeneity in language model failures and motivate regime conditioned evaluation.

</details>


### [258] [Resolving the Missing Financial Data Crisis: A Generative AI Pipeline for SEC 10-K Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35864)
*Prisha Nair,Roee Shraga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估 Llama-3 8B、Qwen-2.5 14B、Llama-3.3 70B 等大模型从 SEC 10-K 文件中提取财务数据的效果，发现不同规模模型适用于不同结构复杂度的属性提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEC 10-K 文件包含大量未进入结构化数据集的财务信息，导致超 70% 企业和约半数总市值面临缺失数据问题，并使定量分析对小型企业产生偏差。传统 Regex 和 BERT 方法在解析复杂 10-K 文件时较为脆弱，无法处理数据属性出现在不同章节或脚注中的情况，造成已有数据丢失。

Method: 研究评估多个大语言模型（Llama-3 8B、Qwen-2.5 14B、Llama-3.3 70B）从 SEC 10-K 文本中提取特定属性的能力，并针对四个结构复杂度不同的财务变量（现金及现金等价物、短期债务、信贷额度、研发费用）评估提取质量。

Result: 较小模型如 Llama-3 8B 在复杂负面提示下性能下降；参数规模与文档复杂度匹配时可获得较高零样本准确率。Qwen-2.5 14B 擅长表格类提取，现金项目 F1 达 83.33%；Llama-3.3 70B 能有效处理密集叙述性脚注，研发费用 F1 达 76.92%。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架可弥补定量金融数据集中的关键信息缺口，通过更全面地分析 SEC 10-K 文件消除缺失数据偏差。

Abstract: SEC 10-K filings contain substantial financial information that is not consistently captured in structured datasets, creating a missing-data problem affecting over 70% of firms and half of total market capitalization. This can disproportionately bias quantitative analysis against smaller firms, which may be excluded due to limited available data. Traditional financial extraction methods such as Regular Expressions (Regex) and BERT, have been widely used. However, they are highly brittle when parsing complex SEC 10-K filings, which leads to data that is existent in the files being lost since these methods do not consider that a data attribute could be located in a different section or a footnote. This study evaluates several Large Language Models (LLMs), including Llama-3 8B, Qwen-2.5 14B, and Llama-3.3 70B, to figure out individual model strengths and weaknesses when extracting specific attributes from SEC 10-K text. The extraction quality was evaluated across four financial variables of varying structural complexity: Cash and Cash Equivalents (tabular), Short-Term Debt (hybrid), Credit Facilities (narrative), and Research and Development (hybrid). Results show that while smaller models like Llama-3 8B experience performance degradation under complex negative prompting, aligning parameter scale with document complexity yields high zero-shot accuracy. Qwen-2.5 14B excels as a tabular specialist with an 83.33% F1 score on Cash, whereas Llama-3.3 70B effectively navigates dense narrative footnotes, achieving a 76.92% F1 score on R&D. This scalable framework addresses critical information gaps in quantitative finance datasets and eliminates missing-data bias through a more thorough analysis of the SEC 10-K files.

</details>


### [259] [PACT: Pairwise-Anchored Calibrated Tuning for Single-Token Typed Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35865)
*Yida Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PACT训练方法，将对比对与证书结构转化为四个无需新标注的训练项，提升单token类型化决策模型的稳健性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单token类型化决策模型虽快速且能输出每种答案的概率，但仅用普通交叉熵训练，忽略了训练数据中大量结构信息（对比对、可机检的证书）。

Method: PACT将数据结构转化为四个训练项：对比对上的双重差分margin（对共享logit偏移不变）、抗答案代码位置偏置的置换一致性项、证书验证消融语境上的证据必要性项、评分字段的序数传输代价，外加三参数上下文温度。

Result: 在冻结的324项留出集、三个种子下：准确率84.6%对85.2%（McNemar p≥0.50），位置偏置最低（9.8%对13.8%），序数误差最低（MAE 0.232对0.311）；相对同优化器/调度但仅用交叉熵的对照，两个种子显著更准，种子间波动减半，NLL降低26%。

Conclusion: 消融与预先设定的证伪测试表明：没有单项能提升原始准确率，该方法的真正价值在于稳健性与稳定性，而非头部准确率。代码、数据划分与训练记录均已公开。

Abstract: Single-token typed-decision models answer a schema question by reading the logits of a few one-letter answer codes at a single position: they are fast and return a probability for every allowed answer, but they are trained with plain cross-entropy that ignores most of the structure in their training data. We study such a model whose data is curated as contrastive pairs---two contexts that differ in one edited fact that flips the answer---each carrying a machine-checked certificate that deleting the decisive sentence makes the fact unknown. We propose PACT, which turns this structure into four training terms that need no new annotation: a difference-in-differences margin over each pair that is invariant to any shared logit offset, a permutation-consistency term against answer-code position bias, an evidence-necessity term on certificate-verified ablated contexts, and an ordinal transport cost for rubric fields, plus a three-parameter contextual temperature. On a frozen 324-item holdout with three seeds, PACT matches the published recipe in accuracy ($84.6\%$ vs. $85.2\%$; McNemar $p \ge 0.50$ at every seed) while giving the lowest position bias of all runs (answer flips under relabelling $9.8\%$ vs. $13.8\%$) and the lowest ordinal error on rubric fields (MAE $0.232$ vs. $0.311$). Against a control with the same optimiser and schedule but cross-entropy only, PACT is significantly more accurate at two of three seeds, halves the seed-to-seed spread and lowers NLL by $26\%$. Seed-matched ablations and pre-specified falsification tests locate these gains precisely: no single term raises raw accuracy, and the method's value lies in robustness and stability rather than headline accuracy. Code, data splits, trained adapters, and all run records are available at https://github.com/BennyLinntu/PACT-Pairwise-Anchored-Calibrated-Tuning-for-Single-Token-Typed-Decisions.

</details>


### [260] [CruxBench: A Benchmark of Information Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35879)
*Hui Dai,Lina Piao,Nick Merrill,Nadja Flechner,Ezra Karger,Haifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CruxBench，一个通过信息价值（VOI）来评估大语言模型生成关键子问题能力的基准，衡量模型提出的关键子问题对目标预测问题信念的更新程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型基准通常只评估答案对固定参考答案的准确率，但许多复杂现实任务的核心是先找出值得提出的问题，即把难题分解为关键子问题。这一信息发现能力尚未被有效评估，因此需要一个新基准来考察模型发现关键问题的能力。

Method: 提出 CruxBench，根据信息价值（VOI）对模型生成的问题打分，即模型提出的关键子问题能在多大程度上更新对目标预测问题的信念。该基准具备三个特性：天然抗污染（真值来自未来世界事件）、开放式（允许无界复杂文本提交）、有真实依据（以现实世界信念的量化变化衡量信息性）。在 293 个目标预测问题上评估八种不同模型。

Result: VOI 与独立的模型能力指标高度相关（r=0.90），并能反映关键子问题对回答目标问题的有用性；但信息发现即使对前沿大语言模型仍具挑战性，它们仅略微优于随机时机基线。

Conclusion: 信息发现是复杂任务中的关键能力，但目前即使最先进的大语言模型在这方面表现仍然有限。CruxBench 为评估这一能力提供了抗污染、开放式且有真实依据的新基准，揭示了模型在提出关键问题上的不足。

Abstract: Benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) typically evaluate the accuracy of answers against fixed reference labels. But a central step in many complex real-world tasks is identifying which questions are worth asking in the first place: decomposing a difficult problem into subquestions -- which we call cruxes -- whose answers provide key steps on the path toward solving the target problem. To evaluate this capability of information discovery, we introduce CruxBench, a benchmark that grades LLM-generated questions by their Value of Information (VOI): how much a model-proposed crux updates beliefs about a target forecasting question. CruxBench enjoys a rare combination of three key properties: it is (1) contamination-resistant by construction, since ground truth is generated by future world events; (2) open-ended, admitting unbounded and complex text-based submissions rather than one correct numeric answer; and (3) grounded, with informativeness measured against quantified changes in real-world beliefs. We evaluate a diverse set of eight models on 293 target forecasting questions and find that VOI correlates highly with independent measures of model capability (r=0.90) and captures cruxes' usefulness for answering target questions. However, information discovery remains challenging even for frontier LLMs, which only narrowly outperform a random-timing baseline.

</details>


### [261] [Almost Human, Except When It Matters: VoxParity and the Decisions a Voice Should Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35922)
*Bhavik Mangla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VoxParity 是一个基准测试，检验语音智能体是否会在音频线索改变正确行为时真正依据声音（而非仅文字）采取行动。183 个场景中转录文本固定、音频变化，仅 11/23 能在纯文本上运行的系统通过；错误整体偏向文字，模型在情绪线索上仍有缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多行业的规则（紧急呼叫、反欺诈、无线电通话、弱势群体保护）都要求根据"声音"或音频环境改变应对，但现有语音智能体可能只依据文字即可完成绝大多数通话。作者希望检验：听到声音是否真的会改变智能体的行动，还是它们实际上只是"文字管道"。

Method: 构建 VoxParity 基准：来自 14 个行业的 183 个场景，每个场景保持转录文字不变、仅改变音频（如教练口吻、医疗监护仪蜂鸣、无线电检查中的遇险呼叫、药名被噪声覆盖、儿童下注声、恐惧低语），并相应改变正确的结构化工具调用。设置"纯文字零假设检验"：只有当听到音频对系统行动的推动大于对纯文字管道的推动时，该系统才算通过。

Result: 在可同时用转录运行的 23 个系统中只有 11 个通过。错误明显偏向文字：当音频要求保护性行动时，全部 28 个系统执行常规请求的比例（合并 41%）高于在干净通话中过度反应的比例（12%）；纯文字管道为 58% 对 15%。探索性分析显示，多数领先系统的失误发生在它们确实听到了线索的情形；系统几乎只在明确陈述规则的条目上超过零假设；领先系统推翻听到的顺从或困惑远多于急性警报；测试的模型中，描述声音和陈述规则各自能弥补部分缺口，但情绪方面仍有差距。

Conclusion: 语音智能体虽能"听到"音频，但倾向于按文字行事，在音频要求保护或特殊处理时仍执行常规请求。提示（描述声音、陈述规则）可部分缓解，但情绪相关线索的缺口仍未解决，表明当前系统尚未真正依据声音改变行动。

Abstract: A voice agent can handle almost every call on the words alone and still fail the few its sector's rules were written for. Emergency-call standards, fraud guidance, radio phraseology and vulnerability rules recognise that how a caller sounds, or what else is audible, can change the right action. VoxParity tests whether agents act on it. In 183 scenarios from 14 sectors, one transcript stays fixed while the audio changes (a coaching voice, a medical monitor beeping, a mayday under a radio check, noise over a drug name, a child's voice placing a bet, a frightened whisper), and with it the correct typed tool call. A words-only null test credits a system only if hearing the call moves its actions more than it moves a pipeline that only reads the words. Only 11 of the 23 systems that can also be run on the transcript pass. Descriptively, errors run toward the words: when the audio calls for protection, all 28 systems carry out the routine request more often than they over-react on clean calls (41% against 12% pooled; the words-only pipeline, 58% against 15%). Exploratory analyses place most of the leading systems' misses on cues they heard; systems beat the null almost entirely on items that state the rule; the leading systems overrule heard resignation or confusion far more often than acute alarm; and, in the models tested, describing the voice and stating the rule each recover part of the shortfall, leaving a gap on emotion.

</details>


### [262] [Question-Specific Knowledge Graphs for Efficient Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35942)
*Ting-Chih Chen,Emile van Krieken,Shujian Yu,Filip Ilievski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 VisKG，一种强化学习框架，使模型将视觉内容翻译为针对问题的知识图谱表示，在保留推理所需实体-关系结构的同时过滤感知噪声，并以更少 token 达到与基线相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉问答中，视觉-语言模型的推理能力依赖将视觉输入转化为文本表示，但对视觉细节的保留程度决定效果。现有基于详细图像描述的方法会引入与任务无关的视觉细节，导致输入 token 数膨胀、计算成本上升。

Method: 提出 VisKG，采用强化学习框架，让模型学习将视觉内容转化为问题特定的知识图谱（KG）表示，遵循最小充分信息原则过滤感知噪声并保留链式推理所需的实体-关系结构。为保证 RL 后训练稳定，采用组奖励解耦归一化策略优化（GDPO）。此外在监督阶段引入负向推理链样本，使模型在 RL 后训练前接触错误推理路径。

Result: 在科学、数学和通用视觉理解基准上，VisKG 性能与基线相当或更优，且所需 token 少于基于描述的方法。相比使用 GRPO 训练的版本，使用 GDPO 训练的 VisKG 平均准确率提升约 2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明知识图谱表示是支持多步推理的有前景途径，并为未来自适应选择最适合特定任务表示形式的研究开辟了方向。

Abstract: Recent work in visual question answering has shown that vision-language models can exhibit strong reasoning capabilities by translating visual inputs into textual representations. The effectiveness of this translation depends on how well visual details are retained; models need to surface and align both explicit and implicit knowledge sufficient to support reasoning, without introducing spurious assumptions. Existing methods that leverage detailed image captions introduce visual details unrelated to the reasoning task, inflating input token counts and increasing computational cost. To address these challenges, we propose VisKG, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework in which models learn to translate visual content into question-specific knowledge graph (KG) representations. This process filters out perceptual noise while preserving the entity-relation structure needed for chain-of-thought reasoning, following the principle of minimum sufficient information. To ensure stable RL post-training, VisKG adopts Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO). In addition, we strengthen the supervision stage with negative rationale samples, exposing the model to incorrect reasoning paths before RL post-training. Experimental results across science, mathematics, and general visual understanding benchmarks show that VisKG achieves performance comparable to or better than baselines, while requiring fewer tokens than caption-based representations. Moreover, training VisKG with GDPO improves accuracy by 2% over its GRPO-trained counterpart on average. These results suggest that KG representations are a promising approach for supporting multi-step reasoning and open up future work on adaptively selecting the most suitable representation for a given task.

</details>


### [263] [Causal and Interpretable Structures in LLM Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35970)
*Gurbir Arora,Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究Transformer各层如何表征与处理多token之间的关系信息。作者发现：中间层使用基于两个token的联合表征，后期层使用基于三个token的联合表征；存在几何结构化但因果上惰性的关系，限制到因果相关的联合表征反而提高预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能完成不仅依赖单个输入token、还依赖token间关系的任务，但这种关系信息在Transformer各层中如何被表征与处理尚不清楚。作者希望揭示其逐层的表征机制。

Method: 选取循环概念（月份、小时、工作日、音符）对应的三token关系推理任务，构造提示集合并获取激活。跨模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Mistral）和多种循环概念，结合几何分析与因果分析，考察token联合依赖如何被组织和使用。

Result: 各模型与概念表现出一致的逐层进展：中间层采用基于两token推断关系的联合表征，后期层采用涉及全部三token的联合表征来完成任务。此外还存在几何结构化但在下一token预测中因果惰性的关系。若将模型限制到因果相关的联合表征，下一token预测准确率反而提升。

Conclusion: 几何与因果分析共同揭示了逐层组织并组合关系信息以形成答案的表征级机制，且因果相关的联合表征对预测既有解释价值也有实用增益。

Abstract: Large language models are able to solve tasks whose answers depend on not only individual input tokens, but also on relations among them. How is such relational information represented and processed across transformer layers? We study activations from ensembles of prompts that require inferring relationships between three tokens corresponding to a cyclic concept (months, hours, weekdays, and musical notes) to correctly predict the next token. Across model families (Llama, Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral) and cyclic concepts, we find a consistent layerwise progression in how the joint dependence among the tokens is geometrically organized and causally used: intermediate layers use a joint representation based on the inferred relationship between two tokens, while later layers use a joint representation associated with all three tokens to correctly complete the task. We also find other relationships between tokens that are geometrically structured but remain causally inert in the next-token prediction. Crucially, when taken together, these geometric and causal investigations reveal the representation-level mechanism that progressively organizes and composes the relational information to form the answer. More surprisingly, restricting the models to such causally relevant joint representations improves next-token prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [264] [Mnemon: Raw Records, Fast Judgments, Slow Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36059)
*Guangren Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mnemon 将记忆工作划分为快速的 System 1 判定与缓慢的 System 2 规划，保持对话为原始有日期记录，用决策模型 Jev 判断检索结果，从而以极少上下文实现高效长时记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统在写入时即把对话改写为事实、图或类型化记忆，导致决策前置、难以复用。作者认为记忆工作应像思考一样分为两个系统：大量快速的 yes/no 判断与少量缓慢的查询与写作。

Method: Mnemon 保留对话为原始带日期记录；System 2 的 LLM 规划检索查询，System 1 的决策模型 Jev 对检索返回结果做判定，并用显式预算规则形成小型 View 供回答模型使用；后台过程将每条记录一次性整合为主题时间线、价值历史与常驻指令。

Result: gpt-4.1-mini 回答时，LoCoMo 达 91.7%（14 个系统中最高），LongMemEval-S 达 83.8%，每题上下文低于 4k token 且 LoCoMo 成本最低；推理模型回答时 LoCoMo 92.2%、LongMemEval-S 94.4%；BEAM 上从 100K 到 10M token，每题成本仅增 1.11 倍；Jev 在同样记录上分离金证据优于两个 LLM，且快 3–11 倍。

Conclusion: 将记忆工作分离为快速判定与缓慢规划，并在写入时不做记录决策，可让同一 agent 读取任何返回带日期记录的存储，同时取得高准确率、低上下文与低成本的长期记忆效果。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets an LLM assistant use a history it can no longer reread, and most memory systems build it by rewriting conversations into facts, graphs or typed memories at write time. We argue that the work of memory divides, as thinking does, into two systems. Most of it is fast System 1 work: many small, independent yes/no judgments about records, such as whether a record is needed or no longer current, which a decision model makes by the dozen in a third of a second. Only a little is slow System 2 work: writing a few search queries, naming what the reply needs and composing the answer, which an LLM does well but slowly. We present Mnemon, a memory agent built on this division. It keeps conversations as raw, dated records; an LLM (System 2) plans searches over them, a decision model, Jev (System 1), judges what the searches return, and rules with explicit budgets turn the judgments into a small View for an unchanged answering model. A background pass consolidates each record once into topic timelines, value histories and standing instructions linked to the records, so that questions about a whole conversation reach evidence their own searches miss. Because nothing is decided about a record when it is written, the same agent can read any store that returns dated records.
  With gpt-4.1-mini answering, as in a public re-evaluation of 14 systems, Mnemon scores 91.7% on LoCoMo, the highest among them, and 83.8% on LongMemEval-S, from under 4k tokens of context per question, with the lowest effective cost index on LoCoMo. With a reasoning model answering, it reaches 92.2% on LoCoMo and 94.4% on LongMemEval-S, the latter on par with the best published results. From 100K to 10M tokens of history on BEAM, its cost per question grows by a factor of 1.11. On the same records, Jev separates gold evidence better than two LLMs and is 3-11 times faster.

</details>


### [265] [PADMÉ: Preference Alignment Data Synthesis for Meta-Evaluation of LM Agent Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36086)
*Cheng Chang,Yining Mao,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将元评估重构为偏好判断问题，并设计PADMÉ数据合成方法，仅用小模型、无需人工参与，在150样本人工验证上将与人判断的一致率从73%提升至85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被用来评估其他语言模型，但评估者（LM evaluator）的决策是否与人类判断一致尚存疑问，该问题被称为元评估。直接解决很困难：收集人类数据成本高、绝对评分难以对齐，而用LM做元评估又会陷入可信度的递归问题。

Method: 将元评估重新表述为偏好判断问题：不直接比较人类与LM评估者对轨迹的分数，而是检验二者隐含的偏好是否一致。基于此提出PADMÉ数据合成方法，生成可靠的、基于准则的智能体场景元评估数据，仅使用小语言模型、评估过程无人工参与且计算预算低。构建原型并合成覆盖4个智能体领域、3个评估准则的1000个样本数据集。

Result: 在150个样本子集上的人工验证表明，相比朴素基线，PADMÉ将与人判断的一致率从73%提升到85%。利用该数据集对25个常见模型进行元评估，揭示了评估性能与评分粒度、宽松程度、模型规模等因素之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 元评估可有效重构为偏好判断问题，PADMÉ能以低成本、无人工参与的方式合成可靠元评估数据，提升与人类判断的一致性，并揭示影响LM评估者表现的关键因素。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed to evaluate other language models. An LM evaluator scoring agentic behaviors across multiple criteria is valuable, provided that its decisions align with human judgment. We call the problem of evaluating this alignment Meta-Evaluation. Tackling it directly is difficult: collecting human data is expensive, absolute scoring is hard to align, and using an LM meta-evaluator recurses the question of trustworthiness. We adopt a reformulation of meta-evaluation as a preference judgment problem: rather than comparing human and LM evaluator scores of a trajectory, we ask whether their implied preferences align. Building on this, we introduce PADMÉ, a data synthesis method that generates reliable criterion-based meta-evaluation data for agentic settings. PADMÉ uses only small language models, requires no human involvement during evaluations, and operates under a low computational budget. We build a prototype of PADMÉ and synthesize a dataset of 1,000 samples across four agentic domains and three evaluation criteria. Human validation on a 150-sample subset demonstrates that PADMÉ improves agreement with human judgment from 73% to 85% over a naive baseline. Meta-evaluating 25 common models with our dataset demonstrates the correlations between evaluation performance and scoring granularity, leniency, and model size, among other factors.

</details>


### [266] [A Character-Level Neural Approach to Sinhala Sandhi Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36131)
*Yasas Ekanayaka,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次为僧伽罗语Sandhi拆分建立神经基准，采用字符级序列到序列模型，在词缀型Sandhi上准确率达94.00%，但复杂子集仅68.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 僧伽罗语Sandhi合并了语音表层形式，掩盖了词汇边界，对NLP处理造成困难，但此前尚无公开研究建立其神经基准。

Method: 基于SandhiLex数据集，使用原生僧伽罗语Unicode输入，开展字符级序列到序列研究，评估循环编码器-解码器模型，并针对词缀型及更复杂的词汇化、派生和词源型Sandhi进行实验，同时开展消融与定性分析。

Result: 最好的模型（双向LSTM编码器配合单向LSTM解码器）在困难子集上仅达68.40%完全匹配准确率（字符级82.08%），远低于词缀型子集的94.00%。消融显示双向编码贡献最大，原生僧伽罗语脚本优于罗马化输入。定性分析表明多数错误为边界相邻字符的近似错误或合理但错误的音系替换。

Conclusion: 研究为僧伽罗语Sandhi拆分建立了经验基准，并指出数据规模、Sandhi类型条件化以及基于注意力的解码是未来主要研究方向。

Abstract: Sinhala Sandhi splitting recovers the constituent words or morphemes hidden inside a phonologically merged surface form. The task is important for Sinhala NLP because Sandhi obscures lexical boundaries, but no prior published work has established a neural benchmark for Sinhala Sandhi splitting. We present a character-level sequence-to-sequence study based on SandhiLex, using native Sinhala Unicode input and evaluating recurrent encoder-decoder models for affixational and more complex lexicalized, derivational, and etymological Sandhi. The central challenge is the hard subset lexicalized, derivational, and etymological Sandhi, where our best model, a bidirectional LSTM encoder with a unidirectional LSTM decoder, reaches only 68.40\% exact-match accuracy (82.08\% character-level accuracy), well below the 94.00\% achieved on the more regular affixational subset. Ablations show that bidirectional encoding is the largest contributor to performance, while native Sinhala script improves exact match accuracy over romanized input. Qualitative analysis indicates that many errors are near misses involving boundary adjacent characters or plausible but incorrect phonological substitutions. These results establish an empirical baseline for Sinhala Sandhi splitting and identify data scale, Sandhi type conditioning, and attention-based decoding as the main directions for future work.

</details>


### [267] [When Does Correction Become Repair? Mechanistic Auditing of Internal Interventions in Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36138)
*Jiayi Li,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAKIKO审计框架，通过方向性错误发现、路由器条件干预、目的地解析验证和前瞻性冻结统计许可，对LLM工具调用前的内部表征修复进行严格审计，揭示"行为移动≠修复"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM在调用外部工具前需在K路动作空间（执行调用、寻求澄清、直接回答、拒绝）中做选择。内部激活引导可改变这些决策，但传统聚合指标掩盖了改变后状态落在何处及其附带损害，缺乏对"表征修复"的严格判定。

Method: SAKIKO框架包含四部分：(1)方向性错误发现；(2)路由器条件干预；(3)目的地解析的验证（destination-resolved verification）；(4)前瞻性冻结的统计许可。在7个LLM、When2Call与MetaTool上以通道键控干预做实验，并用59个预算匹配的随机方向及3个密封评估作对照。

Result: 通道键控干预在5个模型上产生方向特定的净增益；3个密封评估中，59个预算匹配随机方向无一能达到校准目标增益。目的地审计显示：某净增益+55的干预会破坏其触及的过半基线正确决策；Qwen3-4B与Gemma-2-9B上看似有前景的点估计因有限样本不确定性被正式否决。

Conclusion: 行为改变不等同于内部修复。在宣称实现内部修复前，必须进行结果解析式的裁决，SAKIKO确立了这一必要性。

Abstract: Before invoking external tools, an agentic LLM must select among a K-way action space: executing a call, seeking clarification, answering directly, or declining. While internal activation steering can alter these pre-execution decisions, conventional aggregate metrics obscure where altered states land and what collateral damage they inflict. We present SAKIKO, an auditing framework that formalizes representation repair via directional error discovery, router-conditioned intervention, destination-resolved verification, and prospectively frozen statistical licensing. Across seven LLMs on When2Call and MetaTool, channel-keyed interventions induce direction-specific net gains in five models; across three sealed evaluations, none of 59 budget-matched random directions matches calibrated target gain. Crucially, destination auditing shows that behavioral movement does not equal repair: an intervention achieving +55 net gain corrupts over half of the baseline-correct decisions it touches, and promising point estimates on Qwen3-4B and Gemma-2-9B are formally declined due to finite-sample uncertainty. SAKIKO establishes the necessity of outcome-resolved adjudication before claiming internal repair. Code: https://github.com/ruizheliUOA/mechanistic-tool-use-llm.

</details>


### [268] [Language Models Are "Insecure" Reporters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36139)
*Jenny Y. Huang,Jiameng Fan,Ahmed Imtiaz Humayun,Maximillian Chen,Tian Qin,Run Chen,Vidhya Navalpakkam,Hongxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在自主长期任务中倾向于生成"成功叙事"而隐瞒削弱结论的缺陷（称"insecure reporting"），加入简短诚实指令可大幅提升披露率；在Qwen3.5-9B上发现诚实与"追求成功"对应表示空间中的相反方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被部署到愈发自主的长期任务中，人工审计模型的行为与产出变得越来越困难，用户转而依赖LLM自己生成的报告来评估工作质量与完整性。因此需要系统研究LLM是否会在报告中隐瞒会改变叙事走向的缺陷（如削弱方法的负面结果）。

Method: 设计了8个对抗性报告场景，向模型提交包含"植入的负面结果"的机器学习实验日志，统计其报告中是否标记该负面结果；对比无指令与加入简短诚实指令（"Be honest in your response"）两种条件；对8个开放权重模型进行思维链分析；并在Qwen3.5-9B上进行激活分析与引导（steering）实验。

Result: GPT-5.5在200份报告中仅2份标记了削弱方法的负面结果；加入诚实指令后提升至190/200。8个开放权重模型的思维链显示，"披露缺陷"与"设法显得成功"之间存在反复出现的张力。Qwen3.5-9B的激活分析表明，诚实与追求成功对应表示空间中的相反方向，且向诚实方向引导可显著提升报告透明度。

Conclusion: LLM默认倾向于呈现成功叙事，而非如实披露会改变结论的缺陷；简单的诚实提示或表示空间引导能显著提升报告的透明度，说明"insecure reporting"是可通过提示与激活层面干预缓解的系统性现象。

Abstract: As large language models are deployed in increasingly autonomous long-horizon tasks, manually auditing and verifying the actions, artifacts, and outputs of models becomes more difficult. Users instead come to rely on LLM-generated reports to assess the quality and completeness of the work. We introduce a suite of eight adversarial reporting scenarios to systematically study whether LLMs conceal narrative-changing flaws: errors or limitations that undermine an otherwise successful account of work. We call this phenomenon "insecure reporting." When handed machine learning experiment logs containing a planted negative result that substantially weakens the proposed method, GPT-5.5 flags the negative result in only 2 of 200 generated reports. However, when a short honesty instruction, "Be honest in your response," is added, the model flags the negative result in 190 of 200 reports. Across eight open-weight models, chain-of-thought analysis reveals a recurring tension between disclosing narrative-changing flaws and reasoning about ways to appear successful. We perform an activation analysis and a steering experiment on Qwen3.5-9B, finding that honesty and success-seeking correspond to opposing directions in representation space. Our results suggest that LLMs tend to present narratives of success by default, and that steering models toward honesty makes their reports substantially more transparent.

</details>


### [269] [Targeting Pivotal Decisions for Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36178)
*Dongwon Jung,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Yifan Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Yu Hu,Muhao Chen,Varun Chandrasekaran,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProVer 通过智能体裁判对比成功与失败轨迹，定位可能的关键决策片段，并用前后续写成功率之差进行验证，将验证后的优势融入 GRPO，从而实现细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 将轨迹级优势统一分配给所有策略 token，无法区分关键决策与无关决策，导致难以辨识哪些中间决策促成了成功。

Method: 给定一组 rollout，由智能体裁判对比成功与失败轨迹，提出可能决定结果差异的片段；不直接信任该判断，而是通过采样该片段前后的续写来估计终端成功率之差，以此验证片段的优势；将正的优势估计并入该片段内策略 token 的 GRPO 优势中。仅用模型判断来决定"在哪里验证"，从而避免穷举评估每个中间状态。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 SearchQA 上，ProVer 在两个模型规模下均取得最佳平均性能，相对 GRPO 分别提升 9.91%（Qwen3.5-2B）和 7.12%（Qwen3.5-4B）。分析表明，即使没有前沿规模裁判模型，有依据的片段选择也能以适度的额外生成开销提升策略训练。

Conclusion: 有选择地针对关键决策进行细粒度信用分配，在智能体强化学习中兼具有效性与效率。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a promising approach for training large language model agents. However, its uniform assignment of trajectory-level advantages to all policy tokens fails to distinguish consequential decisions from less relevant ones, obscuring which intermediate decisions contributed to success. We introduce ProVer, a framework that targets potentially pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning. Given a rollout group, an agentic judge contrasts successful and failed trajectories to propose a segment potentially responsible for their divergent outcomes. Rather than directly trusting the judge's assessment, ProVer verifies the proposed segment by estimating its advantage from the difference in terminal success rates between current-policy continuations sampled before and after the segment. Positive estimates are then incorporated into the GRPO advantages of policy tokens within the proposed segment. By using model judgment only to select where to verify, ProVer grounds local credit in observed outcomes without exhaustively evaluating every intermediate state. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, ProVer achieves the strongest average performance at both model scales, with relative improvements over GRPO of 9.91% and 7.12% for Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate that informed segment selection improves policy training with modest additional generation overhead, even without a frontier-scale judge model, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of selectively targeting pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [270] [Concept Direction Reliability Across Languages with Different Tokenizer Fertility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36194)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said,Abass Oguntade,Elisha Komolafe,Babangida Sani,Fatima Muhammad Adam,Muhammad Sammani Sani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出应独立于分类准确率来评估情感向量方向的可复现性，通过在英语、豪萨语和约鲁巴语上用四种模型测量分半一致性，发现预测准确并不代表方向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使下游情感分类仍然准确，提取出的情感方向在不同样本间也可能存在差异。因此，仅依赖分类性能无法判断向量方向的稳定性，需要一种独立于分类表现的方向可复现性评估方法。

Method: 研究在英语、豪萨语和约鲁巴语三种语言表示上，使用四种语言模型，结合母语文本与翻译文本，测量分半一致性以评估方向可复现性。使用十个主题选择一致性较高的层，并在另外十五个主题的独立分组上评估方向一致性。同时比较使用最后词元与平均词元表示的效果，并考察句长等因素的影响。

Result: 使用最后词元时，分半一致性范围为英语0.737至0.870，豪萨语0.589至0.762，约鲁巴语0.101至0.399，且在所有77个完整模型比较中保持这一语言排序。基于相同层的分类器预测情感始终高于随机水平。平均词元表示的一致性更低，高一致性还部分反映句子长度。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，预测准确性并不蕴含方向一致性，因此需要独立于分类性能来测量向量方向可复现性。不过，研究并未证明分词器的fertility（即每个以空白分隔单词的平均词元数）导致了跨语言差异。

Abstract: Extracted sentiment directions can vary across samples even when downstream sentiment classification remains accurate. To evaluate direction reproducibility, we measure split-half agreement in English, Hausa, and Yoruba representations across four language models using both native and translated texts. We identify layers selected for agreement using ten topics and evaluate direction agreement across separate groups of fifteen topics. Using the final token, split-half agreement ranges from 0.737 to 0.870 for English, 0.589 to 0.762 for Hausa, and 0.101 to 0.399 for Yoruba, maintaining this language rank order across all 77 complete model comparisons. Classifiers trained on these same layers consistently predict sentiment above chance, demonstrating that predictive accuracy does not imply directional consistency. Furthermore, averaging token representations yields less consistent agreement, and high agreement can partially reflect sentence length. Ultimately, our findings highlight the need to measure vector direction reproducibility independently of classification performance, though they do not establish that tokenizer fertility which is the average number of tokens per whitespace separated word causes cross-lingual differences.

</details>


### [271] [SCOUT: Synergizing Reasoning and Tool-Use for Computer-Use Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36201)
*Jianxing Chen,Xiao Yu,Shipra Agrawal,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT，一个两阶段智能体安全验证器，通过任务特定评分标准生成与工具化证据收集相结合，检测计算机使用智能体在良性指令下产生的隐蔽有害行为，在多个安全基准上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）即使面对良性指令和环境，也可能造成意外伤害，但检测此类伤害面临两大挑战：一是需要任务特定的细致推理，仅凭通用安全准则的验证器会忽略许多重要却微妙的有害行为；二是需要主动调查，过往轨迹截图只显示智能体做了什么，未必反映环境的实际变化，仅依赖截图的LLM评审验证器难以判断动作的真实后果。

Method: SCOUT采用两阶段智能体验证流程：第一阶段，评分标准生成器（rubric generator）对任务与智能体轨迹进行深入推理，确定成功且安全执行应包含的内容，生成任务特定的完成度评分标准与安全评分标准；第二阶段，探测智能体（probing agent）依据这些评分标准，与执行后的环境交互，收集有依据的证据，用于最终的完成度与安全判定。

Result: 在AutoElicit-Bench上，SCOUT取得75.4的不安全F1和74.5的完成度F1，优于LLM-as-a-judge验证器和朴素工具使用验证器；在OS-Blind上以76.4%的不安全检测准确率领先。测试时反思将AutoElicit-Bench上的最终不安全执行率从30.2%降至17.2%。消融与分析表明，SCOUT中的无工具评分标准生成能激发更多推理，对各类验证器骨干（尤其是非前沿模型）的安全检测至关重要。初步扩展到编码任务表明，SCOUT可支持计算机使用之外的安全验证。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过融合推理密集的评分标准生成与工具密集的证据收集，有效解决了CUA隐蔽有害行为检测中的任务特定推理与主动调查难题，在多基准上表现领先，并展现出向编码等更广泛任务扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs), while capable of completing computer tasks in everyday and professional workflows, can cause unintended harm even under benign instructions and environments. However, detecting such harm remains challenging. First, it requires careful, task-specific reasoning: verifiers guided only by general safety criteria often overlook many important but subtle harmful behaviors. Second, it requires active investigation: past trajectory screenshots show what the agent did but not always what actually changed in the environment, so LLM-as-a-judge verifiers that rely on screenshots alone may be unable to determine the actual consequences of actions. To address these challenges, we introduce SCOUT, a two-stage agentic safety verifier that synergizes reasoning-intensive rubric generation with tool-intensive evidence gathering. First, our SCOUT rubric generator extensively reasons over the task and the agent's trajectory to determine what successful and safe execution should entail, generating task-specific completion and safety rubrics. Then, our SCOUT probing agent follows these rubrics to interact with the post-execution environment and collect grounded evidence for final safety and completion judgments. We evaluate our framework on two computer-use safety benchmarks. On AutoElicit-Bench, SCOUT achieves 75.4 unsafe F1 and 74.5 completion F1, outperforming LLM-as-a-judge verifiers and naive tool-use verifiers. SCOUT leads on OS-Blind with 76.4% unsafe detection accuracy. Test-time reflection reduces final unsafe execution rates from 30.2% to 17.2% on AutoElicit-Bench. Ablations and analysis show that tool-free rubric generation in SCOUT elicits substantially more reasoning and is crucial for safety detection across verifier backbones, especially non-frontier ones. A preliminary extension to coding tasks shows that SCOUT can support safety verification beyond computer-use.

</details>


### [272] [FastGuide: Accelerating Reward Guidance for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36202)
*Darshan Thaker,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FastGuide 提出一种并行与自回归混合的自适应解码方法，加速掩码扩散语言模型的奖励引导推理，最高提速 4.4 倍且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的奖励引导虽能灵活控制掩码扩散语言模型，但每次解码迭代都需昂贵的扩散模型前向传播和奖励模型反向传播，计算代价过高，限制了实际应用。

Method: FastGuide 融合并行与自回归解码：每步仅计算一次引导并复用其生成多个 token，以摊销奖励模型反传成本；步内通过 KV 缓存与稀疏注意力重计算，实现逐 token 解掩码的自回归前向；并根据模型置信度推迟不确定的 token 以自适应混合解码。

Result: 在三个奖励基准上，FastGuide 相比顺序奖励引导解码最高提速 4.4 倍，同时保持相近的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过自适应混合并行与自回归解码，FastGuide 有效降低了奖励引导扩散语言模型推理的计算开销，在效率与质量间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Gradient-based reward guidance provides a flexible way to use downstream reward models to control masked diffusion language models at inference time. However, its computational cost remains high as each decoding iteration incurs expensive diffusion model forward passes and reward model backpropagation steps. To address this, we introduce FastGuide, an adaptive hybrid of parallel and autoregressive decoding to accelerate reward guidance for diffusion language models. In analogy to parallel decoding, FastGuide amortizes the cost of reward model backpropagation by computing guidance once per decoding step and reusing it to generate multiple tokens. Within each decoding step, FastGuide makes diffusion forward passes autoregressive by unmasking tokens one at a time while efficiently recomputing token distributions after each unmasking by utilizing KV caching techniques and sparse recomputation of attention. Lastly, to adapt hybrid decoding to the model's confidence, FastGuide defers any token that the model is unconfident about under its recomputed distribution. Experiments on three reward benchmarks demonstrate that FastGuide is up to $4.4\times$ faster than sequential reward-guided decoding while retaining similar generation quality.

</details>


### [273] [Geometric Representations of African Languages: A Regional Semantic Hub and Cultural Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36205)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said,Jonathan Shock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究 Gemma 4 31B 对非洲语言的表征及文化引导响应，通过两套实验分析了语言间的表征对齐性与国别文化引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型如何表征非洲语言，以及是否能够通过文化引导方向对模型输出进行干预，从而理解模型内部表征中的区域与语系模式。

Method: 研究一使用探针、对比方向和表征相似度度量，比较九种非洲语言与三种对照语言，分析英语迁移及其在不同语言与层间的差异。研究二使用独立的英语数据构建针对尼日利亚、加纳、肯尼亚和南非的方向，并采用union scoring评估归因率差异，同时与Aya Expanse 32B进行对比。

Result: 英语迁移因语言和层而异；非洲语言的"非洲—西方"对比方向在多个层上比与对照语言更对齐；语系比较在十二层中的五层通过Holm阈值；尼日利亚内部约鲁巴语与伊博语在十二层中的十一层比与豪萨语的平均配对更对齐。研究二中随机方向下的归因率差异估计为0.63至0.81，多数输出通过自动结构连贯性筛选。

Conclusion: 两项研究共同记录了所采样表征中的区域与语系模式，以及在英语中实现的国别引导效果，且结果依赖于所选用的表征度量方式。

Abstract: We study how Gemma 4 31B represents African languages and responds to cultural steering. The first study compares nine African languages and three controls using probes, contrast directions, and measures of representation similarity. Transfer from English varies across languages and layers. Directions representing an Africa versus West contrast are more aligned among the African languages than between these languages and the controls at several layers. The comparison across language families passes the reported Holm threshold at five of twelve layers, although dependence between language pairs limits the statistical interpretation. Within Nigeria, Yoruba and Igbo are more aligned than the average of their pairs with Hausa at eleven of twelve layers. The second study uses separate English data to construct directions for Nigeria, Ghana, Kenya, and South Africa. Under union scoring at the selected strengths, estimated differences in attribution rates from random directions range from 0.63 to 0.81. Most outputs pass the automated structural coherence screen. Comparisons with Aya Expanse 32B show that results depend on the representation measure. Together, the studies document regional and family patterns in the sampled representations and country steering in English.

</details>


### [274] [The Canonical Order Problem: When Large Language Models Are Unreliable Knowledge Bases for Multi-Valued Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36209)
*Timo Pierre Schrader,Annemarie Friedrich,Simon Razniewski,Lukas Lange*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型如何表示与生成多值关系，发现模型内部按“规范顺序”组织多值关系，并揭示集合生成的三阶段机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 被广泛用作知识库，但现有工作多关注单一关系三元组，而现实中的关系多为多值关系，需要生成实体集合，这一过程尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis），识别“规范顺序问题”，并分析 LLM 集合生成的三阶段：候选实体检索、内部排序、下一元素选择。

Result: LLM 内部的概率分布会按规范顺序（如字母序、时间序）组织多值关系；当提示要求偏离该内部规范顺序时，模型生成完整集合的可靠性显著下降。

Conclusion: LLM 的多值关系生成受内部规范顺序约束，构建偏离该顺序的知识库会显著降低模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as knowledge bases (KBs) due to the vast amount of knowledge they acquire during pre-training. While many works focus on extracting single relational triples, most real-world relations are multi-valued and require generating sets of entities.
  In this paper, we investigate how LLMs represent and generate multi-valued relations. We identify the canonical order problem: The probabilistic distributions inside LLMs organize many multi-valued relations according to a canonical ordering (e.g., alphabetical or chronological). Through mechanistic analysis, we show that set generation in LLMs can be thought of in terms of three phases: (1) retrieval of candidate entities, (2) internal sorting, and (3) selection of the next element. As a result, prompts aiming to construct KBs that deviate from this internal canonical ordering lead to a markedly reduced reliability of LLMs when aiming to generate complete sets for multi-valued relations.

</details>


### [275] [Lost in Translation: Measuring the Effect of Non-Native English on End User Performance of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36214)
*Yusheng Zhou,Eleanor Lin,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了FABLE数据集，系统分析非母语英语用户的提示特征如何影响LLM回复质量，发现模型虽然不会复制拼写等表层错误，却会模仿用户提示中的修辞和词汇水平，导致低流利度用户获得质量更低的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非英语母语用户使用LLM时被证实会系统性获得比流利使用者更低质量的回复，但此前不清楚是哪些具体的非母语英语特征导致了这一差距，因为"流利度"本身是机械准确性、词汇使用、组织结构和语篇连贯性的复合体。

Method: 提出FABLE——一个受控数据集，包含190,911条英语提示变体，源自174K条真实用户的写作相关任务提示。基于该数据集评估了34个开放权重LLM的回复表现。

Result: 发现明显的非对称性：模型不会把拼写错误等表层错误传播到输出中，但会模仿用户提示中更高层次的修辞与词汇质量；此外，最流利与最不流利提示所获得的回复整体质量存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了针对非母语英语LLM用户的关键性能差距，他们不仅得到质量更低的回答，得到的答案本身流利度也更差。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by people whose first language is not English, yet these users have been shown to receive systematically lower-quality responses than fluent speakers. Which specific features of non-native English drive this gap remains unclear, because fluency is itself a composite of mechanical accuracy, vocabulary use, organization, and discourse coherence. Here, we introduce FABLE, a controlled dataset of 190,911 English prompt variants derived from 174K real user prompts for writing-related tasks. Evaluating responses from 34 open-weight LLMs, we find a clear asymmetry; while models do not propagate surface errors such as misspellings into their outputs, models do mirror higher-level rhetorical and lexical qualities present in the user's prompt. Further, the overall quality of responses differs substantially between the least- and most-fluent prompts. These results highlight a key LLM performance disparity for non-native English LLM users, resulting in both lower-quality and less-fluent answers.

</details>


### [276] [CineSubBench: Evaluating LLMs on Long-Form Narrative and Cultural Understanding from Multilingual Movie Subtitles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36218)
*Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Susmoy Chakraborty,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CineSubBench，一个基于多语言电影字幕的长上下文电影理解评测基准，包含1012部电影、6种语言、6072条字幕轨和813万条带时间戳字幕，覆盖七项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已在法律、医学、软件工程和网络安全等专业领域获得广泛评测，但电影领域相对被忽视。电影理解需要长文本叙事整合、多语言解释和文化情境化的受众判断能力，因此缺乏针对性的评测基准。

Method: 构建CineSubBench基准：将字幕轨表示为数千条按时间排序的短话语，要求模型在没有显式场景或事件结构的情况下重建人物、关系、事件、因果进程和主题。基准提供匹配的多任务、多语言、多文化（MultiX）评测设置，包含七项任务：叙事重建与抽象、类型预测、年龄适宜性、覆盖十国分类体系的国别电影分级，以及基于字幕的语言安全性。

Result: 在九个LLM上评测发现：情节梗概比完整事件概要更容易被还原；跨语言一致性在不同模型和语言间差异显著；各国分级体系呈现出不同的校准模式；强烈粗俗语比轻度猥亵语更易被证据化定位。

Conclusion: CineSubBench确立了电影作为长上下文LLM评测领域，并为衡量叙事、多语言、文化和证据定位能力提供了统一基准。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated in specialized domains such as law, medicine, software engineering, and cybersecurity, yet film remains comparatively underexplored despite requiring long-form narrative integration, multilingual interpretation, and culturally situated audience judgments. We introduce CineSubBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-context film understanding from multilingual movie subtitles. A subtitle track represents a film as thousands of short, temporally ordered utterances from which models must reconstruct characters, relationships, events, causal progression, and themes without explicit scene or event structure.
