We find that a short round of training on corrections beats the same amount of training on generic chat data, that corrections on other medical prompts do roughly as well as corrections of the poisoned prompts themselves, and that instructing the correction writer to model a careful, harm-avoiding assistant adds no measurable benefit over plain corrections. In the settings we tested, correcting harmful training data reduces EM more than deleting it.

</details>


### [348] [Co-Linguistics: AI-augmented Theory Construction in Linguistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37635)
*Emmanuel Chemla,Benjamin Spector,Alexandros Kalomoiros,Philippe Schlenker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI用作"共科学家"（Co-Linguistics），帮助构建和评估语言学理论，但强调人类仍需主导研究方向与概念评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多把LLM视为人类语言能力的潜在模型，本文希望探讨AI在语言学中的全新用途：作为协助构建和评估语言学理论的合作者。

Method: 基于语言学理论可数学形式化（形式语言理论、模型论）的特点，提出利用AI使现有理论完全显式化、比较竞争理论、通过程序归纳提出新理论，并借助主动学习加速关键预测的识别与检验。

Result: AI有望加速语言学研究，形成从理论评估到理论构建的循环，甚至可能带来递归、自主的理论改进。

Conclusion: 人类仍是核心：语言学家提供科学方向并进行概念性评估，实验参与者用于检验超出LLM能力的经验预测。

Abstract: LLMs have been studied in recent linguistics as potential models of humans' linguistic abilities. Here we discuss an entirely different use of AI, namely as a co-scientist, to help construct and assess linguistic theories (we refer to the result as "Co-Linguistics"). Since the 1960s, linguistics has developed theories that are in principle mathematically formalizable, often in the language of formal language theory or model theory. The AI revolution in mathematics will thus have consequences in linguistics-but with an essential twist: proving new theorems is rarely the linguist's goal. Rather, one seeks to find the best set of axioms to derive empirical statements. AI could accelerate research by making existing theories fully explicit, by comparing competing theories, and more ambitiously, by proposing new theories (in machine learning, this relates to "program induction"). It will also help assess theories by accelerating the identification and test of crucial predictions, thanks to unparalleled access to data (in machine learning, this relates to "active learning"). While the cycle from theory evaluation to theory construction may give rise to recursive and possibly autonomous improvement of linguistic theories, humans remain central: linguists provide scientific directions and evaluate theories conceptually, and experimental participants are needed to assess empirical predictions that are outside the reach of LLMs.

</details>


### [349] [Evaluating and Benchmarking the System One Model Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37647)
*Tobias Deußer,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文评估了 TypeSafe AI 的商业系统模型 Jev（jev-1.13.0），该模型不生成文本，而是在给定状态和类型化问题下返回固定选项、评分位置或概率。作者在 37 个数据集、346,009 次请求、成本低于 10 美元的设置下零样本评测 Jev，并与 Qwen3.8-27B 和 Gemma-4-E4B 对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息获取流水线中存在大量小型决策（如查询路由、接地检查、内容审核、评分标准打分），这类任务不需要生成文本，而需要校准良好的概率与选项判断。现有评测多针对生成式大模型，缺乏对这类专用判别式商业模型的系统评估，因此作者希望量化 Jev 的能力边界、校准性与失败模式。

Method: 对 Jev（jev-1.13.0）进行零样本评测，覆盖 37 个数据集，涉及分类、路由、自然语言推理、阅读理解、常识推理、审核、法律条款分析和评分标准打分；每个数据集使用一个冻结模板并采用完整评估划分，共 346,009 次请求，成本低于 10 美元。作为参照，在完全相同的请求上，通过选项的精确下一词概率对 Qwen3.8-27B 与 Gemma-4-E4B 打分。

Result: Jev 在 IMDB、SST-2、HellaSwag、ARC 上达到 95-99% 准确率，在覆盖 122 种语言的 Belebele 上达到 86.7%。它在 37 个数据集中的 27 个上优于 Qwen，且 Qwen 的九个领先均落在 bootstrap 区间之外；对 Gemma 则在全部 37 个上胜出。三个模型均在低资源语言、细粒度或噪声标签以及基于评分标准的质量判断上退化。Jev 的选择概率校准良好并支持选择性预测；二元概率排序好但相对于固定 0.5 阈值放置不佳，用训练数据调阈值可将 UNFAIR-ToS 的 micro-F1 从 0.50 提升到 0.75。Jev 在 MMLU 计算题上比其他题更准（94% vs. 91%），而两个开放模型以及三者在中国 C-Eval 上则相反。旋转选项不改变 Jev 准确率，抽掉问题则降至接近随机，排除浅层记忆但无法排除记忆的问答对。

Conclusion: Jev 在广泛的判别式任务上表现强劲、成本低且概率校准良好，适合信息获取流水线中的小型决策与选择性预测，但仍受低资源语言、细粒度标签与主观评分标准限制，其高准确率可能部分来自记忆的问答对。作者公开了代码、评测框架与全部原始响应。

Abstract: Jev is a commercial System One model from TypeSafe AI that does not generate text: given a state and typed questions, it returns a choice from fixed options, a position on a rubric, or the probability that a statement is true, with probabilities the vendor describes as calibrated. Such models target small decisions in information access pipelines, such as routing queries, checking grounding, moderating content, or rating against a rubric. We evaluate Jev (jev-1.13.0) zero-shot on 37 datasets spanning classification, routing, natural language inference, reading comprehension, commonsense reasoning, moderation, legal clause analysis and rubric scoring, with one frozen template per dataset and full evaluation splits: 346,009 requests for under USD 10. For reference, we score Qwen3.8-27B and Gemma-4-E4B on identical requests via their exact next-token probabilities over the options. Jev reaches 95-99% accuracy on IMDB, SST-2, HellaSwag and ARC and 86.7% on Belebele across 122 languages. It beats Qwen on 27 of 37 datasets, with none of Qwen's nine leads outside the bootstrap intervals, and Gemma on all 37. All three models degrade on low-resource languages, fine-grained or noisy labels, and rubric-based quality judgments. Jev's choice probabilities are well calibrated and support selective prediction. Binary probabilities rank well but are poorly placed relative to a fixed 0.5 threshold; thresholds tuned on training data raise micro-F1 on UNFAIR-ToS from 0.50 to 0.75. Jev answers MMLU's calculation-heavy questions more accurately than other MMLU questions (94% vs. 91%), whereas both open models, and all three on C-Eval, find them harder. Rotating the options leaves Jev's accuracy unchanged and withholding the question drops it to near chance, ruling out shallow memorization but not memorized question-answer pairs. We release the code, harness and all raw responses.

</details>


### [350] [Corpus-Guided Dual-Path Propagation for Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37661)
*Baoxian Liu,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NexusRAG，在图检索增强生成中引入语料库级实体邻域结构，通过语义传播与结构传播双路径扩展实体前沿，并改进PPR初始化，在多跳QA基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无关系图检索方法主要依赖查询-句子相似度来搜索证据，容易遗漏查询相似度低但有用的桥接证据，同时会激活与推理链无关的偶发实体，影响多跳检索效果。

Method: 在无关系的Tri-Graph上增加由实体共现与语义相似度联合导出的语料库级实体邻域结构；利用该结构进行两条互补传播路径：基于邻域约束的句子语义传播用于识别与查询相关的实体前沿，邻域实体间的直接结构传播用于将该前沿扩展到结构相关实体；传播得到的实体权重用于邻域感知的段落初始化以驱动Personalized PageRank。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准和GraphRAG-Bench的领域特定子集上，NexusRAG一致优于现有方法；在GraphRAG-Bench子集上所有问题类别均取得最高证据召回率，超过基线4.2-8.1个百分点。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过引入实体邻域结构与双路径传播并改进PPR初始化，可有效缓解低相似度桥接证据被忽略和无关实体被激活的问题，提升多跳检索与问答表现。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation supports multi-hop retrieval by organizing corpus information into graphs. However, existing relation-free graph retrieval methods rely primarily on query-sentence similarity to search for evidence. This can exclude useful bridging evidence with low query similarity and activate incidental entities unrelated to the reasoning chain. In this paper, we propose a simple and effective approach called NexusRAG, which augments the relation-free Tri-Graph with a corpus-level entity neighborhood structure derived from joint entity co-occurrence and semantic similarity. NexusRAG employs this structure to guide two complementary propagation paths: neighborhood-constrained semantic propagation through sentences identifies the query-relevant entity frontier, while direct structural propagation between neighboring entities expands that frontier to structurally related entities. The propagated entity weights also inform neighborhood-aware passage initialization for Personalized PageRank. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks and a domain-specific subset of GraphRAG-Bench show that NexusRAG consistently outperforms existing approaches. On the GraphRAG-Bench subset, NexusRAG achieves the highest evidence recall in all question categories, exceeding baselines by 4.2-8.1 points. The implementation code is available at https://github.com/Jacob-biu/NexusRAG.

</details>


### [351] [Reader Proficiency Shapes Layer-wise Surprisal Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37688)
*Akio Hayakawa,Horacio Saggion*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用12个大语言模型的分层惊奇度（surprisal）预测眼动注视行为，发现低词汇水平读者在首次注视时长（FPGD）上表现出更深的“预测深度”，而总注视时长（TGD）的预测深度整体更深，且该分层模式与读者水平和注视指标相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阅读行为既受语言输入影响，也受读者语言水平影响。以往研究多用LLM惊奇度预测阅读行为，但较少考察不同模型层与不同读者水平、不同注视指标之间的关系是否不同，因此本文探究分层惊奇度与人类注视行为的关系是否随读者水平和注视指标变化。

Method: 使用MECO L2语料库的眼动数据，比较高词汇水平与低词汇水平读者在FPGD和TGD上的表现；用Predictive Depth量化惊奇度预测力在模型各层的分布；共测试12个LLM，并做留一法（leave-one-out）分析检验内部层的优势能否推广到未见文本。

Result: 在12个LLM中，低词汇水平读者在FPGD上倾向于表现出更深的Predictive Depth，而这一差异在TGD上较小；两组读者中TGD的Predictive Depth均比FPGD更深。留一分析显示，有信息量的内部层的优势可推广到未见文本，但实际预测性能提升有限。

Conclusion: 预测力在LLM各层的集中位置可能与不同注视指标所反映的阅读加工的时间进程和广度有关，并随读者水平变化。分层LLM惊奇度为理解读者群体和注视指标间的阅读行为差异提供了有用视角。

Abstract: Reading behaviour varies not only with linguistic input, but also with reader proficiency. In this study, we investigate whether the layer-wise relationship between surprisal from large language models (LLMs) and human gaze behaviour differs across readers with different levels of proficiency and across gaze measures. Using eye-tracking data from the MECO L2 corpus, we compare readers with high and low vocabulary proficiency on first-pass gaze duration (FPGD) and total gaze duration (TGD). We quantify the distribution of the predictive power of surprisal across model layers using Predictive Depth. Across 12 tested LLMs, we find that readers with lower vocabulary proficiency tend to show deeper Predictive Depth for FPGD, while this difference is smaller for TGD. Also, TGD itself shows deeper Predictive Depth than FPGD in both proficiency groups. These patterns suggest that where predictive power is concentrated across LLM layers may be related to the timing and breadth of the reading processes captured by different gaze measures, and that this relationship can vary with reader proficiency. Our leave-one-out analysis further shows that the advantage of informative internal layers extends to unseen texts, although the practical improvements in prediction are limited. Overall, our results show that layer-wise LLM surprisal provides a useful perspective on variation in reading behaviour across both reader groups and gaze measures.

</details>


### [352] [Billiger.de Products: A Bilingual Entity Matching Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37713)
*Aaron Steiner,Ksenia Elagin,Ralph Peeters,Johannes Knopp,Christian Bizer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Billiger.de Products，一个德英双语实体匹配基准，涵盖13类消费品，基于德国比价平台billiger.de构建，并通过多种匹配器验证了其难度与语言差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有产品匹配基准主要包含英文产品数据，且常被单一品类（如电子产品）主导，缺乏跨语言、多品类的评测资源。

Method: 基于billiger.de平台数据构建德英双语基准，沿用WDC Products设计，提供多种变体（在边界案例比例、开发集大小、训练未见实体比例上不同）；每个报价配有对齐的英文翻译，保持配对、划分和标签不变，并设置跨语言测试集；使用6种有监督匹配器和零样本GPT-5.2在两语言版本及跨语言测试集上进行验证。

Result: 基准具有较高难度；多数匹配器在英文版本上的得分平均高于德文版本，RoBERTa与HierGAT差异最大，而零样本LLM对语言基本不敏感；PLM匹配器在英文版上的F1低于其在WDC Products、Abt-Buy等现有英文基准上的表现，表明本基准更难。

Conclusion: Billiger.de Products是一个更具挑战性的双语多品类产品匹配基准，能够揭示语言因素对匹配性能的影响，并为跨语言实体匹配研究提供评测基础。

Abstract: Existing product matching benchmarks primarily contain English-language product data and are often dominated by a single product category, such as electronics. This paper introduces Billiger.de Products, a bilingual German and English entity matching benchmark covering thirteen consumer product categories, including difficult-to-handle categories such as clothing and furniture. The benchmark data originates from the German price comparison platform billiger.de. Following the design of WDC Products, the benchmark offers multiple variants that differ in the fraction of corner cases, the size of the development set, and the fraction of entities unseen during training. An aligned English translation of every offer keeps all pairs, splits, and labels fixed, while cross-language test sets combine German and English records within individual pairs. We validate the benchmark using six supervised matchers and zero-shot GPT-5.2 on both language versions and the cross-language test sets. The validation shows the difficulty of the benchmark. The comparison of the results on the English version of the benchmark to the results on the German version shows that most matchers score on average higher on the English version. The difference is largest for RoBERTa and HierGAT, while the zero-shot LLM runs are largely insensitive to the language. Comparing the F1 scores achieved by PLM-based matchers on the English version of Billiger.de Products with their performance on existing English-language benchmarks, such as WDC Products and Abt-Buy, shows that Billiger.de Products is more difficult than these benchmarks.

</details>


### [353] [Which papyrus HTR is good enough? Character-error-rate tolerance of four papyrological tasks on Greek texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37755)
*Anton Repushko,Elena Chepel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究以papyri.info的63,846份希腊文纸草文献为基准，通过人工构造不同字符错误率（CER）的文本，测试四类纸草学任务对OCR/HTR错误的容忍度，并给出每类任务可接受的CER目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量希腊文纸草文献尚未出版和数字化，自动手写文本识别（HTR）流水线有助于学者发现未被阅读的文献。但古希腊纸草识别系统尚处起步阶段，对于特定纸草学任务所需达到的准确率尚无研究。因此需要建立希腊纸草HTR的基准。

Method: 从papyri.info的63,846份希腊文文献版本中，去除编辑层以模拟“完美HTR”的纯字母输出，再通过带种子的算法将其退化到1%–50%的精确CER，并引入丢失行和四种错误形态变体。基于这些数据训练小型模型（TF-IDF、fastText、字符CNN、ByT5-small），用于文档类型、年代、文献与文学分类，并应用八种关键词检索方法。比较清洁文本训练的模型与在特定CER水平上重新训练的模型，并在不同CER下进行评估。

Result: 不同任务的容忍度不同。使用清洁文本训练的模型时，文献与文学分类在20% CER下仍保留90%的指标；文档类型为7.5%；子类型和检索为5%；年代判定仅为3%。在含有字符错误的文本上重新训练，可大幅消除超过15% CER后出现的急剧性能下降。模型通常对长文档中的集中性损伤比短文本中分散的小错误更具容忍度。

Conclusion: 研究为四类任务分别提供了CER目标，并表明在噪声文本上训练的模型可使当前不完美的文本识别对这些任务具有实用价值。

Abstract: Purpose: Most Greek papyri remain unpublished and undigitised; a handwritten text recognition (HTR) pipeline that transcribes them automatically would let scholars discover documents and literary works that have so far gone unread. Recognition systems for Ancient Greek papyri are in statu nascendi, and how accurate they must be for a given papyrological task has not been examined. To answer this and set a benchmark for Greek papyrus HTR, we test a range of character error rates (CER) against four papyrological tasks, using published editions as ground truth. Methods: From 63,846 current editions of Greek texts in papyri.info, we imitate a letters-only "perfect HTR" output by removing the editorial layer, then degrade it with a seeded algorithm to exact CERs of 1 - 50%, with lost lines and four error-shape variants. On these data we train small models (TF-IDF, fastText, a character CNN, ByT5-small) for document type, dating and documentary-versus-literary classification, and apply eight keyword search methods. We compare models trained on clean text with models retrained at a specific CER level, and evaluate across CERs. Results: Tolerance differs by task. With clean-trained models, documentary-versus-literary classification retains 90% of its metric up to 20% CER; document type up to 7.5%; subtypes and search up to 5%; dating only up to 3%. Retraining on text containing character errors largely eliminates the sharp degradation that otherwise sets in above 15% CER. Models generally tolerate concentrated damage in a long document better than small errors spread across a short text. Conclusion: The study provides a CER target for each of the four tasks and shows that models trained on noisy text make current, imperfect text recognition useful for them.

</details>


### [354] [Selecting What Matters: Semantic Compression-Guided Selective Pooling for Long-Context Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37782)
*Zifeng Cheng,Jie Zheng,Zhiwei Jiang,Shuwen Wang,Fei Shen,Shiping Ge,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCSP，一种免训练的长文本嵌入框架，通过语义压缩提示识别重要token并聚合其表示，替代均匀平均池化，可即插即用地提升零样本与微调模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的长文本嵌入方法多在因果注意力下改进信息流，并采用对所有token表示均匀平均的方式构造嵌入。但对于长文档，均值池化会被大量冗余或弱信息内容稀释，导致显著语义信息被掩盖。

Method: SCSP先按句子将文档切分为块，并为每块附加语义压缩提示；通过提示隔离注意力掩码，在保持文档token间信息流的同时将每个提示限制在其局部上下文。随后利用这些提示引发的注意力模式估计token重要性，筛选信息量大的token，并聚合其中间层表示形成最终嵌入。

Result: 在长文本嵌入基准上的大量实验表明，SCSP能以即插即用方式集成到零样本与微调模型中，并持续提升其性能。

Conclusion: SCSP表明，以语义压缩驱动的重要token选择可有效缓解长文档嵌入中的信息稀释问题，是一种通用、免训练且易于集成的长文本嵌入增强方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential as training-free text encoders for long-context embeddings. Existing approaches primarily improve information flow under causal attention and typically construct embeddings by uniformly averaging all token representations. However, for long documents, such mean pooling can dilute salient semantic information with abundant redundant or weakly informative content. To this end, we propose SCSP, a training-free framework that leverages semantic compression for informative token selection in long-context embedding. Specifically, SCSP first partitions a document into sentence-aware chunks and appends a semantic compression prompt to each chunk. A prompt-isolated attention mask preserves information flow among document tokens while restricting each prompt to its corresponding local context. We then use the attention patterns elicited by these prompts to estimate token importance, select informative tokens, and aggregate their intermediate-layer representations into the final embedding. Extensive experiments on long-context embedding benchmarks demonstrate that SCSP can be integrated into both zero-shot and fine-tuned models in a plug-and-play manner, consistently improving their performance.

</details>


### [355] [A Proposed Rubric for Evaluating Expressed Clinical Reasoning in Large Language Model Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37788)
*Zhangshu Joshua Jiang,Zina Ibrahim,James T. Teo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于评估语言模型回答中临床推理表达的多维评分量规，整合医学教育评估框架、临床LLM基准与通用推理评估研究，但目前尚未进行信效度检验，其直接目的是使评估决策明确化、可被审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的评估需要结构化标准，而临床推理的表达评估尤为复杂。现有医学教育评估框架、临床LLM基准和通用推理评估研究各有贡献，但缺乏一个整合性的、面向临床推理表达的评分框架，因此需要提出一个明确、可审查的量规。

Method: 基于三方面工作构建量规：医学教育评估框架（ART、SCT、Key Feature Problems、OSCE）；临床LLM基准（MedR-Bench、HealthBench、TIMER-Bench、DR.BENCH、PrIME-LLM、PatientSafeBench）；通用LLM推理评估研究（Factuality-Validity-Coherence-Utility分类法、FaithCoT-Bench、C2-Faith）。将groundedness作为对分类法中factuality类别的临床导向改编，形成对金标准临床情景自由文本回答的多维评分框架，包含临时行为锚点、适用规则及针对病例特定安全关键错误的单独标记。

Result: 量规整合了上述概念，形成多维评分框架，并包含临时行为锚点、适用规则和安全关键错误标记。通用领域框架仅用于设计参考，不被视为经过验证的临床工具。

Conclusion: 该量规不替代病例特定参考标准或现有基准的任务特定指标，尚未经过评分者间信度、结构效度或临床实用性检验。其直接目的是在实证测试之前，使评估决策明确且可被审查。

Abstract: Rubrics support the structured evaluation of language models. We propose a rubric for assessing expressed clinical reasoning in model responses, drawing on three bodies of work: medical education assessment frameworks (ART, SCT, Key Feature Problems and OSCE); clinical LLM benchmarks (MedR-Bench, HealthBench, TIMER-Bench, DR.BENCH, PrIME-LLM and PatientSafeBench); and general LLM reasoning evaluation research, including the Factuality-Validity-Coherence-Utility taxonomy, FaithCoT-Bench and C2-Faith. We use groundedness as a clinically oriented adaptation of the taxonomy's factuality category. The rubric brings these concepts together in a multidimensional framework for scoring free-text responses to gold-standard clinical vignettes. It includes provisional behavioural anchors, applicability rules and a separate flag for case-specific safety-critical errors. General-domain frameworks inform its design but are not treated as validated clinical instruments. The rubric does not replace case-specific reference criteria or the task-specific metrics of existing benchmarks. It has not yet been tested for inter-rater reliability, construct validity or clinical utility. Its immediate purpose is to make evaluation decisions explicit and open to scrutiny before empirical testing.

</details>


### [356] [CompOrca: Corpus-Scale Compliance Labelling of Instruction-Tuning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37807)
*Philipp E. Glass,Alina Miron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发布了CompOrca，对全部4,233,923条OpenOrca语料进行合规/不合规标注，由同开放权重LLM（LongCat-2.0）五次独立判断完成，并公开一致合规（94.75%）、一致不合规（1.28%）和非一致（3.97%）子集及原始投票数，用于研究微调对拒绝与不服从行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究微调如何塑造模型的拒绝与不服从行为，需要识别训练样本中拒绝、回避或未完成任务的内容，但现有标注最多只覆盖数千条提示的评测集，规模严重不足，无法支撑对大规模训练语料的合规性分析。

Method: 对4,233,923条OpenOrca语料全部进行合规标注：由同开放权重LLM裁判（LongCat-2.0，1.6T参数）进行五次独立判断，每条样本被划分为合规或不合规；发布时按一致合规、一致不合规和非一致行分类，并附原始投票数。与450条人工标注样本（其中150条标注两次，人工一致性κ=0.93）进行验证。

Result: 单次判断将2.7%–3.2%的语料标为不合规，而五次全部标为不合规的仅1.28%。相对人工标注，一致合规与一致不合规标签的精确率分别为97.3%和86.7%，后者是高质量子集而非不合规的完整枚举。现有拒绝检测方法对不合规类的召回率仅在0.4%–94.1%之间。

Conclusion: CompOrca提供了大规模、带逐行标签和投票数的合规标注语料，可用于过滤最模糊样本并支撑微调对拒绝/不服从行为影响的研究；同时表明现有人工标注与拒绝检测方法在覆盖面和召回率上存在明显局限。语料已在Hugging Face公开。

Abstract: Studying how fine-tuning shapes refusal and noncompliance behaviour requires identifying training examples that refuse, evade or otherwise fail to fulfil the requested task. But existing annotation covers evaluation sets of a few thousand prompts at most. We present CompOrca, a compliance labelling over the entirety of the 4,233,923-example OpenOrca corpus. Every example was classified as compliant or noncompliant by five independent passes of an open-weight LLM judge (LongCat-2.0, 1.6T parameters), and the corpus is released as unanimous compliance (94.75%), unanimous noncompliance (1.28%), and nonunanimous rows (3.97%) along with the raw vote counts. A single pass flags 2.7-3.2% of the corpus as noncompliant, while only 1.28% is flagged by all five, allowing for filtering the most ambiguous samples. Against 450 human-annotated examples, 150 of them annotated twice (human-human $κ= 0.93$), the unanimous compliance and noncompliance labels are 97.3% and 86.7% precise, the latter a high-precision subset, not a complete enumeration, of noncompliance. Published refusal-detection methods recall only between 0.4% and 94.1% of the noncompliance class. We release the full corpus with its per-row labels and vote counts at https://huggingface.co/datasets/cemiu/CompOrca

</details>


### [357] [Thinking in Depth, Speaking Directly: Recurrent Latent Reasoning for Paralinguistically Grounded Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37818)
*Shengbo Cai,Yuxiang Wang,Jingran Xie,Zhisheng Zhang,Shun Lei,Di Cao,Teddy Sun,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LoopSLM：基于循环 Transformer 的潜在推理框架，通过两阶段训练缩小"感知-推理鸿沟"，在共情语音对话中无需 CoT 即可直接推理，兼顾性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情语音对话需同时利用"说了什么"和"怎么说"来决定回应。显式 CoT 虽能提升副语言感知并让声学线索在回复中更明确，却不能保证其在回应规划中被有效使用，作者将这一不匹配称为"感知-推理鸿沟"。此外，CoT 也难以用文字充分捕捉声学线索，且会带来额外推理延迟。

Method: 构建于循环 Transformer 之上，复用同一解码器块进行潜在推理，每一轮都以声学信息为锚点精炼隐藏状态；采用两阶段训练，将"学习推理"与"学习回应"分离，从而在推理时无需生成 CoT。

Result: 在 EchoMind 上，副语言理解、推理与回复质量均优于 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B；相比 CoT-SFT 基线，推理准确率提升 20 分以上，同时生成 token 减少 64.5%、延迟减半；在多数共情回复指标上超过 Qwen3-Omni-Thinking，延迟低 34 倍；仅用对话数据训练，却在通用音频基准上也有提升。

Conclusion: 以循环潜在推理取代显式 CoT，可在有效利用声学线索的同时大幅降低推理开销，并具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Empathetic spoken dialogue requires models to use both what is said and how it is said to decide how to respond. Explicit CoT can improve paralinguistic perception and make acoustic cues more explicit in replies, yet does not ensure their effective use in response planning. We call this mismatch the perception-reasoning gap. In addition, CoT may not fully capture acoustic cues in words, and generating it adds inference latency. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopSLM, which builds on looped Transformers for latent reasoning, reusing a decoder block to refine hidden states with acoustic grounding at every pass. Its two-stage training further narrows the perception-reasoning gap by separating learning to reason from learning to respond, enabling direct inference without CoT. On EchoMind, LoopSLM improves paralinguistic understanding, reasoning, and reply quality over Qwen2.5-Omni-7B. Against the CoT-SFT baseline, LoopSLM gains over 20 points in reasoning accuracy while generating 64.5% fewer tokens at half the latency. It also outperforms Qwen3-Omni-Thinking on most empathetic reply metrics with 34x lower latency. Despite training only on dialogue data, LoopSLM improves accuracy on general audio benchmarks.

</details>


### [358] [The Geometry of Inference in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37824)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较Transformer中间残差状态与其最终状态及跨语境最终状态库，揭示推理过程中表征几何"特异性"的逐步形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型通过连续的残差更新逐步构建预测，但其表征如何变得"特定于"某个最终结果尚不清楚。作者希望刻画这一过程中几何特异性的形成。

Method: 对六个预训练语言模型，将中间残差状态与自身最终状态、以及来自其他语境的最终状态库进行对比；分析端点偏好、竞争者集合规模与成员变化、方向对齐与欧氏距离/余弦距离的区别；构建一个简单的高维模型分离范数、对齐与端点几何的作用；并从理论上证明朝向自身端点的直线路径在欧氏或余弦距离下不会引入新竞争者。

Result: 自身端点比平均替代端点更早占优，但许多个体端点仍然更接近；竞争集合整体随深度收缩，但其成员不断变化，且存活的端点未必变得更相似；方向对齐与端点排名可以改善而欧氏距离几乎不变；观察到偏离直线收敛的情况；在所有模型中，排名较低的输出token对应的端点余弦距离更远。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了Transformer推理中不断增强的几何特异性，说明距离、竞争者数量与存活端点集中度是看待该过程的三种不同视角，并将残差几何与输出组织联系起来。

Abstract: Transformer language models build predictions through successive residual updates, but how their representations become specific to an eventual outcome remains unclear. We study this process by comparing intermediate residual states with their own final states and an empirical bank of final states from other contexts. Across six pretrained language models, the own endpoint becomes preferable to the average alternative early, while many individual endpoints remain closer. These competing sets generally shrink with depth, but their membership changes and their surviving endpoints need not become more similar to one another. Directional alignment and endpoint rank can therefore improve while Euclidean distance to the final state changes little. We develop a simple high-dimensional model that separates the roles of norm, alignment, and endpoint geometry, showing how gradual directional changes can produce sharp reductions in competition. We also prove that a straight path toward the own endpoint cannot introduce new competitors under either Euclidean or cosine distance; observed entries thus establish departures from straight-line convergence. Finally, endpoints associated with lower-ranked output tokens tend to lie farther away in cosine distance across all studied models, connecting residual geometry to output organization. Together, these findings characterize increasing geometric specificity during transformer inference and explain why distance, competitor count, and concentration of the surviving endpoints provide distinct views of that process.

</details>


### [359] [Can Vision-Language Models Stay Helpful When Facing Implicit Risks? Intent-Privilege OPSD for Efficient Safety-Helpfulness Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37837)
*Haotian Deng,Wenbin Xing,Gang Xu,Tao He,Jinkai Zheng,Chun Li,Zheng Zhu,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出意图特权在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD），利用意图作为特权监督训练视觉语言模型识别跨模态隐式风险，兼顾安全与有用性并大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型存在跨模态隐式风险：单独看似无害的视觉与文本输入组合后可能诱发不安全回答。现有安全方法依赖大规模偏好数据、昂贵的多次采样训练或额外的推理期防护模块，且常因直接拒答而牺牲有用性。

Method: OPSD 在训练时以基于证据的意图作为特权监督，将教师模型在意图条件下的响应偏好蒸馏给学生模型，每个提示仅需一次采样；学生模型在推理时无需意图标注或额外安全模块。

Result: 仅用 1,447 条安全样本（比标准偏好数据集少 95%），训练时间较多次采样的 GRPO 类方法缩短 5 倍，平均推理长度降低 7%。在全部五个评测组中均取得最高的安全-有用联合成功率；在 SIUO+HoliSafe 汇总上，该成功率从 43.9% 提升至 53.5%。

Conclusion: 训练期的意图监督能同时提升安全性与有用性，并显著降低数据、训练与推理成本。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain vulnerable to cross-modal implicit risks: visual and textual inputs that appear benign in isolation can jointly elicit unsafe responses. Existing safety methods often require large preference datasets, costly multi-rollout training, or additional safeguards at inference time. They may also sacrifice helpfulness by directly refusing requests that could be answered safely. In this paper, we propose Intent-Privilege On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), which leverages evidence-grounded intent as privileged supervision during training to help VLMs recognize implicit risks and provide safe, useful responses instead of blanket refusals. OPSD distills a teacher's intent-conditioned preferences over responses into a student using a single rollout per prompt; the student then responds without intent annotations or an additional safety module. With only 1,447 safety-specific examples - 95% fewer than standard preference datasets - OPSD reduces training time by 5x relative to multi-rollout GRPO-style training and average inference length by 7%. It attains the highest ratio for joint safety-helpfulness success, which measures the proportion of responses that are both safe and helpful, across all five evaluation groups. Remarkably, on pooled SIUO+HoliSafe, this success ratio rises from 43.9% to 53.5%. These results show that training-time intent supervision can improve both safety and helpfulness while substantially reducing data, training, and inference costs.

</details>


### [360] [AnthroDial: Benchmarking LLM Anthropomorphism in Autonomous Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37853)
*Wentao Liu,Xi Chen,Siyu Song,Biao Yuan,Yu Zhang,Zhou Zhuotong,Jingying Zhou,Guohao Feng,Shasha Hu,Tianfu Wang,Shangshang Yang,Haoyang Liu,Youjia Li,Xiaokun Wang,Min Ji,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AnthroDial统一框架，用于构建拟人化社交智能体，涵盖交互机制（MindFlow）、评估体系（CAPS-Eval）与训练范式（SEEDS+DiAPO），并构建多场景数据集，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为社交智能体日益普及，但类人交互不仅需要流畅回答或人格一致性，还需自主决定是否、何时及如何沟通，并适应不断变化的语境、目标与关系。现有研究缺乏统一的方法来支持、评估与改进这种连续开放式交互能力。

Method: 提出AnthroDial统一框架，从三方面构建拟人社交智能体：（1）MindFlow，基于动态Mind Buffer的轻量级交互机制，实现自主、异步、自适应沟通；（2）CAPS-Eval，基于理论构建的评估框架，覆盖认知、情感与行为维度；（3）可扩展训练范式，结合用于环境扩展的SEEDS与用于自适应能力优化的DiAPO。同时构建覆盖日常交流、游戏互动与长程角色互动的评估数据集。

Result: 在多种模型与场景上的大量实验表明，交互自主性与自然度得到提升；CAPS-Eval在可靠性、区分度及与人类排序的一致性方面得到验证；训练范式的有效性也得到确认。

Conclusion: 该框架的各个组件共同构成一套统一方案，可用于在开放式交互中开发可信的类人社交智能体。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as social agents, yet credible human-like interaction requires more than fluent responses or persona consistency. Agents must autonomously decide whether, when, and how to communicate while adapting to evolving contexts, goals, and relationships. Existing research, however, lacks a unified approach to enabling, evaluating, and improving such capabilities in continuous, open-ended interaction. We introduce AnthroDial, a unified framework for developing anthropomorphic social agents from three complementary aspects: MindFlow, a lightweight interaction harness that enables autonomous, asynchronous, and adaptive communication through a dynamic Mind Buffer; CAPS-Eval, a theory-grounded framework for evaluating cognitive, affective, and behavioral dimensions of anthropomorphic interaction; and a scalable training paradigm that combines SEEDS for environment expansion with DiAPO for adaptive capability optimization. We further construct evaluation datasets covering everyday communication, game interaction, and long-horizon character interaction. Extensive experiments across diverse models and scenarios demonstrate improved interaction autonomy and naturalness, validate the reliability, discriminativeness, and agreement with human rankings of CAPS-Eval, and confirm the effectiveness of our training paradigm. Together, these components provide a unified framework for developing credible human-like social agents in open-ended interaction.

</details>


### [361] [One Threshold Does Not Fit All Languages: Language-Conditional Deferral for Reliable and Efficient Low-Resource Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37861)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Vangala Navya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在低资源多语言场景下，单一全局阈值的一致性预测无法为每种语言兑现覆盖保证，按语言分别校准阈值可修复该问题且成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方（非洲、亚洲、拉美）部署的文本分类器通常在普通CPU上运行、用单一模型服务多语言、标注样本极少并依赖人工纠错。这类系统只有在能给出明确的错误率承诺（自主标注中错误比例不超过固定值，其余交人工）时才有用。而Split conformal prediction通常用跨语言汇总的验证数据估计单一置信阈值，作者质疑这一承诺是否对每种语言都成立。

Method: 在MasakhaNEWS（16种非洲语言）和AfriSenti（12种语言，另含2种训练中未见过的语言）上评估池化阈值与按语言分别估计阈值的split conformal prediction；对比各语言的实际覆盖率、需转交人工的比例与所需标注量、训练成本。

Result: 池化阈值平均能达到90%目标，但索马里语仅77.5%、提格里尼亚语83.7%，两种未见语言分别为77.5%和81.2%。按语言分别估计阈值后，所有语言覆盖率落在89.1%–91.0%之间，且无需重训练。该方案也揭示了承诺代价的不平等：维持承诺需将43%的索马里语新闻和超80%的阿姆哈拉语、聪加语推文转人工，而尼日利亚皮金语新闻不足8%。每语言仅需100–200条标注，模型在单CPU核心上数分钟即可训练完成。

Conclusion: 全球性覆盖保证并不等于逐语言的覆盖保证，池化校准会系统性损害部分语言。应按语言分别校准、如实报告并逐语言规划人工复核预算；该修复方案廉价且可行。

Abstract: In the Global South, the lower-income countries of Africa, Asia, and Latin America where most of the world's languages are spoken, a deployed text classifier usually runs on ordinary CPUs, serves many languages with a single model, has few labeled examples in any of them, and relies on people to catch its mistakes. Such a system is only useful if it can promise how often it will be wrong: at most a fixed fraction of the labels it assigns on its own may be incorrect, and everything else must go to a person. Split conformal prediction delivers this promise through a single confidence threshold, normally estimated on validation data pooled across languages. We ask whether the promise reaches every language, and it does not. On MasakhaNEWS (16 African languages) and AfriSenti (12 languages plus two never seen in training), a pooled threshold meets the 90% target on average but covers Somali at 77.5%, Tigrinya at 83.7%, and the two unseen languages at 77.5% and 81.2%. Estimating one threshold per language brings every language to between 89.1% and 91.0% without retraining, and it shows how unequal the cost of the promise is: keeping it means sending 43% of Somali news and over 80% of Amharic and Xitsonga tweets to a person, against under 8% of Nigerian Pidgin news. One or two hundred labels per language are enough and the models train in minutes on one CPU core, so the fix is affordable: calibrate, report, and budget human review one language at a time.

</details>


### [362] [It's Not What the Image Shows: Irrelevant Context Destabilises VLM Judges Without Informing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37863)
*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Kinan Ibraheem,Lotem Peled-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIST用200个可作字面/比喻两解的句子搭配对齐、误导或无图三种条件测试13个视觉语言模型，发现图像存在本身（而非其内容）最影响判断，说明可替代性结论反映的是评测配置而非仅模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型日益被用作人类标注者的替代品，因此可替代性测试应反映模型本身而非偶发的评测条件；需要检验图像是否会在本应无关的判断中干扰模型。

Method: 构建MIST压力测试：200条英文句子，每条含可字面或比喻理解的短语，配对齐图像、误导图像或无图三种条件；标注规则要求仅凭句子判断。测试13个VLM评判者，并对比删除"忽略图像"指令的对照条件，以及通过/未通过alt-test的两组模型。

Result: 对齐图像改变20.5%标签，误导图像改变19.4%，各模型相近，均高于删除忽略图像指令时的11.6%；但两图间差异标签中仅37%朝所显语义移动，且与人类标注者一致率在无图、对齐、误导条件下无变化。效应在通过alt-test的7个模型中较小，但所有模型均存在。

Conclusion: 推动判断的是图像的存在本身，而非图像内容；因此可替代性结论描述的是评测配置，同样也是在描述模型。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in place of human annotators, making it important that substitutability tests reflect the model rather than incidental evaluation conditions. We introduce MIST, the Misleading-Image Stress Test: 200 English sentences, each built around a phrase readable either figuratively or literally and shown with an aligned image depicting its reading, a misleading image depicting the opposite, or no image at all. The guidelines require the label to be decided from the sentence alone, so no image should change any answer. We expected each image to pull a judge's labels toward the sense it depicts, and neither kind did. Across thirteen VLM judges, an aligned image changed 20.5% of labels and a misleading one 19.4%, close for every judge and both above the 11.6% produced by deleting the ignore-the-image instruction with the image left in place. Yet only 37% of the labels that differ between the two images moved toward the sense shown, and agreement with our human annotators is unchanged whether the image is absent, aligned or misleading. The effect is smaller in the seven judges that pass the alt-test than in the six that never do, but present in all of them: what moves a judge is that an image is there, not which of the two it is, so a substitutability verdict describes a configuration as much as a model.

</details>


### [363] [Retrieval Capacity of Self-Attention Under Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37879)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过保留自注意力权重最高的token并测量NLL增幅，可估计语言模型实际使用的有效注意力集合大小，该大小受上下文长度、竞争与聚合方式影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在上下文中实际使用了多少token，以及什么因素决定这一数量，对于理解模型的注意力机制与长上下文处理能力具有重要意义。

Method: 无需重训练，在每个注意力头、层和查询处仅保留注意力权重最高的token且保持原始权重不变，通过改变选择集合大小并测量负对数似然(NLL)增幅来估计有效注意力集合大小；同时进行固定支持事实实验与条件理论建模。

Result: 较小的选择集合即可使NLL接近全注意力基线（所需大小因模型而异）；基于注意力的选择显著优于随机选择；选择集合呈几何结构；延长上下文会增加所需集合大小但其占上下文比例下降；额外背景会将支持事实token的注意力排名与质量压低；重归一化保留权重可显著减小所需集合大小。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种测量语言模型有效注意力集合大小的方法，并揭示了其对上下文、竞争与聚合方式的依赖关系。

Abstract: How many tokens from its context does a language model actually use, and what determines that number? We study this question through self-attention. Without retraining, we retain only the tokens with the highest attention weights at each head, layer, and query, keeping their original weights unchanged. By varying the selected set size and measuring the increase in negative log-likelihood (NLL), we estimate the effective attention set size needed to stay within a chosen loss tolerance. Relatively small selected sets can keep NLL close to the full-attention baseline, although the required size varies across models. Attention-based selection substantially outperforms random selection. Selected sets exhibit geometric structure, although geometric separation alone does not establish that model loss is preserved. Extending context while evaluating the same prediction targets increases the required set size, while its fraction of context decreases over the tested range. Experiments with a fixed supporting fact show that additional background pushes its tokens down the attention ranking and reduces their attention mass. Renormalizing the retained weights can substantially reduce the required set size, showing that it also depends on how selected representations are combined. Conditional theoretical models explain how competition and attention-mass retention can produce growing set sizes without more distinct information to retrieve. These results provide a way to measure effective attention set size in language models and investigate its dependence on context, competition, and aggregation.

</details>


### [364] [How Many Labels Does a Language Need? Annotation Budgets and Cross-Lingual Pooling for African-Language Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37882)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Sowmya Guda,Navya Vangala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 用字符 n-gram 线性模型在 28 个非洲语言-任务对上绘制学习曲线与零样本迁移矩阵，给出标注预算与跨语言借用标签的实证指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言文本分类面临核心资源问题：需要多少标注样本，能否用其他非洲语言的标签替代。论文针对这两个预算与迁移问题做系统的实证回答。

Method: 在 MasakhaNEWS（16 种语言，新闻主题分类）和 AfriSenti（12 种语言，推文情感）共 28 个语言-任务对上，使用字符 n-gram 线性模型（无需预训练权重、无需加速器，双 CPU 核数秒可训练）。进行 25 到数千标签的单语学习曲线实验，并构建完整零样本迁移矩阵。

Result: 主题分类在中位语言约 400 标签即达全量 macro-F1 的 90%；情感在 12 种语言中 11 种在全量训练规模仍在提升，需要数千标签。池化其他语言数据在小预算下收益显著（25 目标标签时新闻平均 +0.20，Lingala 最高 +0.43；情感 +0.08），但收益在约 800 标签时衰减为零，全量规模下新闻 16 种中 9 种、情感 12 种中 8 种出现负迁移。25 个目标标签加池化数据在多数新闻语言上可媲美 100–400 个单语标签。零样本迁移仅恢复多数类基线与语言内模型差距的中位数 13%（新闻）和 4%（情感），例外主要由共享文字（阿姆哈拉语与提格里尼亚语）、共享词汇（英语与尼日利亚皮钦语、阿拉伯语方言）或共享标签先验解释，而非语言谱系。

Conclusion: 跨语言标签借用只在小标注预算下有效，随目标标签增加迅速失效甚至有害；零样本迁移收益有限且由文字、词汇与标签先验而非语系决定。论文公开可复现代码，并为无 GPU 的非洲语言分类团队提供具体标注建议。

Abstract: Every text classifier for an African language begins with a budgeting question: how many labelled examples are needed, and can labels from other African languages stand in for them? We answer both questions empirically for 28 language-task pairs, news topic classification in 16 languages (MasakhaNEWS) and tweet sentiment in 12 languages (AfriSenti), using a character n-gram linear model that trains in seconds on two CPU cores with no pretrained weights and no accelerator. Monolingual learning curves at budgets from 25 to several thousand labels show that topic classification reaches 90\% of its full-data macro-F1 with about 400 labels in the median language, while sentiment is still improving at the full training size in 11 of 12 languages and needs thousands of labels. Pooling the full training data of the other languages in the benchmark is worth a great deal at small budgets and nothing at large ones: at 25 target labels it adds 0.20 macro-F1 on average for news (up to 0.43 for Lingala) and 0.08 for sentiment, the gain decays to zero by 800 labels, and at full size pooling hurts in 9 of 16 and 8 of 12 languages. Twenty-five target labels plus pooled data match what 100 to 400 monolingual labels achieve for most news languages. A complete zero-shot transfer matrix shows that transfer without any target labels recovers a median of only 13\% (news) and 4\% (sentiment) of the gap between a majority-class predictor and the in-language model, with the exceptions explained by shared script (Amharic and Tigrinya), shared lexicon (English and Nigerian Pidgin, the Arabic dialects), or a shared label prior rather than by language family. We release code that regenerates every number from the public benchmark files and translate the results into concrete annotation guidance for teams building African-language classifiers without GPUs.

</details>


### [365] [Zero-shot Dependency Parsing with Unsupervised Cross-Lingual Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37883)
*Lalita Lowphansirikul,Attapol Rutherford,Jian Gang Ngui,Sarana Nutanong,Peerat Limkonchotiwat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨语言无监督自举方法，增强预训练语言模型中的句法知识，显著提升低资源语言的零样本依存句法分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于编码器架构的预训练语言模型在零样本跨语言迁移的多种语言理解任务中表现出色，但由于依存句法分析的句法特性，将其应用于该任务仍面临重大挑战。为提升模型跨语言类型学的泛化能力，需要增强模型内部的句法知识。

Method: 提出一种跨语言无监督自举（bootstrapping）方法，用于提升预训练语言模型内部的句法知识。

Result: 该方法在低资源语言的零样本句法分析性能上取得显著提升；对自举后模型的分析表明，其在识别句法结构方面的鲁棒性增强，体现为无参数树探测测试中更高的得分。

Conclusion: 跨语言无监督自举能有效增强预训练语言模型的句法知识，提升零样本句法分析的跨语言泛化能力与句法结构识别鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pre-trained language models (PLMs) with encoder-based architectures have shown impressive capabilities in zero-shot cross-lingual transfer for various language understanding tasks. However, applying this technique to dependency parsing remains a significant challenge due to its syntactic nature. To boost model generalizability across linguistic typologies, we propose a cross-lingual unsupervised bootstrapping method to improve syntactic knowledge within the PLM. We show that our method achieves a significant improvement in zero-shot parsing performance in low-resource languages. Analysis of these bootstrapped models uncovers increased robustness in recognizing syntactic structures, evidenced by higher scores in parameter-free tree probing tests.

</details>


### [366] [It's All Training: A Fully Synthetic Single-Stage Recipe for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37891)
*Pierre-Carl Langlais,Pieter Delobelle,Yannick Detrois,Pavel Chizhov,Carlos Rosas-Hinostroza,Neil Si Smail,Benjamin Burtin,Hanna Shcharbakova,Ivan Yamshchikov,Anastasia Stasenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SYNTH，首个从58,698篇维基百科文章衍生的开源合成语料库，将预训练、中期训练和后训练整合为单一训练阶段，通过结构化放大精选百科种子数据，显著提升数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预训练数据集源自网络爬取，存在诸多问题且缺乏显式推理内容，无法支持中后期训练流程。许多前沿实验室开始从顶尖模型出发构建内部数据集以增强预训练数据混合，但均未公开，且合成数据对语言模型知识技能获取的影响尚不明确。

Method: 从精选百科种子出发进行结构化数据放大，构建SYNTH合成语料库，采用反向翻译生成有据可依的文本，训练一系列不同规模模型进行验证：56M小模型（Monad）、0.3B-0.6B密集模型（Baguettotron）以及13B/1B激活MoE模型，并在等计算量条件下与过滤网络数据对比。

Result: 在等计算量下SYNTH优于过滤网络数据，模型与同规模开源基线具有竞争力；得益于反向翻译，SYNTH训练的模型在使用10-140倍更少训练token的情况下实现高事实精度，记忆效应通过种子语料库进行定向控制。

Conclusion: 合成数据集能够以极小部分训练数据产生有竞争力的通用模型，实现快速迭代；为提升数据效率的通用模型以及缺乏指令或对话数据的领域专用模型开辟了新可能。研究公开了SYNTH数据集和Baguettotron系列模型以支持开源语言模型发展。

Abstract: Current pre-training datasets are derived from web crawls, with all their issues, and were not designed to support mid- and post-training pipelines--for instance, they contain little explicit reasoning. Thus, many frontier labs have begun to develop their own internal datasets, starting from state-of-the-art models, to augment their pre-training data mix, eg, with reasoning traces to address cold-start problems. While demonstratively effective, none of these datasets are public, and the effect of this so-called synthetic data on knowledge and skill acquisition of language models, including small ones, remains poorly understood. We present SYNTH, the first open-source synthetic corpus derived from 58,698 Wikipedia articles that collapses pre-, mid-, and post-training into a single training stage via structured amplification of curated encyclopedic seeds. We evaluate SYNTH by training a suite of models: a 56M tiny model (Monad), 0.3B-0.6B dense models (Baguettotron), and a 13B / 1B-active MoE. At iso-compute, SYNTH outperforms filtered web data, and our models remain competitive with similarly-sized open-weight baselines. Because SYNTH is back-translated from grounded passages, SYNTH-trained models achieve high factual precision despite 10-140x fewer training tokens, with memorization targeted by the seed corpus. These results show that synthetic datasets, including our SYNTH dataset, are capable of producing competitive generalist models from a fraction of the training data, enabling rapid iteration as the frontier advances. These findings open up possibilities for both generalist models with significantly increased data efficiency, as well as domain-specific models where no instruction or conversational data is available. Finally, we publicly release our SYNTH dataset and the suite of Baguettotron models under a permissive license, thus supporting open-source language model development.

</details>


### [367] [The Unequal Influence of Bad Advice: Using Training Data Attribution to Modulate Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37914)
*Gonçalo Paulo,Louis Jaburi,Nora Belrose,Lucia Quirke,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用训练数据归因（TDA）量化每个有害样本对"涌现性失准（EM）"的贡献，并通过重训练验证归因质量，发现基于分数的数据过滤能显著增强或减弱EM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大语言模型于狭窄且未对齐的任务会破坏后训练对齐效果，引发新的失准行为（即涌现性失准EM）。EM被认为与"人格化"表征有关，但尚不清楚训练数据中哪些性质驱动这一效应：是否所有有害样本贡献大致相当，以及不同模型是否对相同微调样本同样敏感。

Method: 采用训练数据归因（TDA）方法，定量估计每个有害样本对EM的贡献。通过重训练来评测归因质量——理论上合理的归因分数应能通过按分数过滤数据来增强或削弱EM。同时对比数据归因分数与黑盒有害性分数，并考察跨模型的归因分数泛化性。

Result: 基于分数的过滤能显著增强或削弱EM；数据归因分数与黑盒有害性分数都能识别出关键样本。所有被测模型在相同数据集上训练后都会失准；影响分数在过滤由同一模型计算的数据时表现最佳。影响分数在测试的三个模型族之间存在跨模型泛化，但这种泛化无法达到同模型过滤的性能水平。

Conclusion: 有害样本对EM的贡献并不均等，可通过归因分数识别高影响力样本；归因的有效性依赖模型自身，跨模型虽有一定迁移性但效果不及同模型场景，表明EM的驱动因素具有模型依赖性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on narrow, misaligned tasks can undo their post-training alignment and induce novel misaligned behaviors -- a phenomenon known as \emph{emergent misalignment} (EM). EM has been linked to persona-like representations, where fine-tuning might reduce loss by amplifying a harmful or 'evil' persona. It remains unclear which properties of the training data drive this effect: whether all harmful examples contribute approximately equally to misalignment and whether different models are equally affected by the same fine-tuning examples. In this work, we use training data attribution to quantitatively estimate how much each harmful example contributes to EM. We benchmark the quality of the attribution via retraining -- a sound attribution score should enable us to enhance or attenuate EM by filtering data on that score. Score-based filtering can substantially enhance or attenuate EM; we find that both data-attribution scores and a black-box harmfulness score can identify consequential examples. All models we test become misaligned when trained on the same dataset, and influence scores perform best when filtering data from the same model that computed them. We find cross-model generalization of influence scores from scores derived from the three model families we tested, but this generalization does not recover same model filtering performance.

</details>


### [368] [Learning What to Remember: Long-horizon Counterfactual Memory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37930)
*Jiaming Tang,Mingyan Liu,Armin Sarabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Memory Gain Policy Optimization (MGPO)，通过将记忆重写的增量价值归因于其对当前及未来下游效用的边际贡献，把延迟的记忆效用转化为直接的学习信号，从而优化语言模型应当持久保留哪些信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久文本记忆能让语言模型在长交互中携带信息，但"该记住什么"本质上是一个信用分配问题：一次记忆重写可能要在很多步之后才有用，而且观察到的效用大部分可能继承自重写之前已存储的信息。因此需要一个能剥离继承效应、识别记忆更新真正增量价值的方法。

Method: MGPO 隔离每次记忆重写的增量价值，将其归因于对当前和未来下游效用的边际贡献，从而把延迟出现的记忆效用转化为直接的学习信号，用于优化应持久保留的信息。作者在文档级信息抽取任务上研究该方法，因为结构化监督使得单个记忆更新的效果可直接测量。

Result: MGPO 在提升抽取效果的同时，将平均记忆长度相对优化前的初始记忆策略减少近 80%。学习到的记忆策略还支持跨领域、跨下游模型的复用与迁移，且无需进一步训练。

Conclusion: 有效的记忆学习不仅依赖于保存有用信息，更关键在于识别哪些记忆更新能产生持久的增量价值。

Abstract: Persistent textual memory allows language models to carry information across long interactions, but learning what to remember is fundamentally a credit-assignment problem. A memory rewrite may only become useful many steps later, while much of the observed utility may be inherited from information already stored before the rewrite. We introduce Memory Gain Policy Optimization (MGPO), which isolates the incremental value of each memory rewrite by crediting it for its marginal contribution to current and future downstream utility. This turns delayed memory utility into a direct learning signal for optimizing what information should persist. We study MGPO on document-level information extraction, where structured supervision makes the effects of individual memory updates directly measurable. MGPO improves extraction while reducing average memory length by nearly 80% relative to the initial memory policy before optimization. The learned memory policy also supports reuse and transfer across domains, downstream models without further training. These results show that effective memory learning depends not only on preserving useful information, but on identifying which memory updates create lasting incremental value.

</details>


### [369] [BITEM at the NTCIR-19 R2C2 Task: Predicting Confidence from Agentic RAG Pipeline Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37993)
*Julien Knafou,Luc Mottin,Alexandre Flament,Paul van Rijen,Esteban Gaillac,Patrick Ruch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BITEM 团队用单一智能体流水线参加 NTCIR-19 R2C2 两个子任务：检索与多轮汇总后取得 22 队中第 4、5 名，回答准确率 0.9219（第 6/25），HMR 0.4915（第 13）；通过基于已记录信号的手工规则将准确率提至 0.9375、HMR 提至 0.6985，并提出 accHMR 作为更合理的评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测中，单看 HMR 可能奖励“答错但置信度低”的系统，无法反映置信度的实际价值；因此需要一个兼顾准确率与置信度质量的指标，并探索在不增加模型调用与检索的前提下提升系统表现。

Method: 采用单一智能体流水线：模型在电影语料上搜索、阅读并记录证据，由调度器持有记录并决定可提交内容；主张须经蕴含级联对照所引段落核验后才被接受，答案须有足够已核验证据支持才发布。每题运行 3–4 次，每次从剔除前几次已见内容的语料中检索。置信度由调度器根据运行残留信号计算，模型不参与自评。最后在同样记录信号上手写少量规则，不增加模型调用与检索。

Result: 两次检索在 22 队中列第 4、5 名，多轮结果汇总带来 0.0709 nDCG@20 增益，在多跳与重后处理问题上增益最大（组织者将其汇总运行列为该领域第一）。25 个回答运行中 16 个基于该两次检索提供的段落，其中 12 个由其他队伍提交。答案运行准确率 0.9219（第 6/25），HMR 0.4915（第 13）；手工规则提升至准确率 0.9375（第 5）与 HMR 0.6985（第 9）。

Conclusion: 单一智能体流水线在检索与回答上均具竞争力，且汇总多轮检索显著增益。基于已有信号的手工规则可低成本提升准确率与置信度质量。由于 HMR 单独排名可能奖励低置信错误回答，提出 accHMR（准确率×HMR）作为更合理指标，修订规则在 accHMR 上得 0.6549（第 5）。未来方向是在流水线已产出的数值上拟合模型，替代手工规则。

Abstract: The BITEM team entered both subtasks of the NTCIR-19 R2C2 task with a single agentic pipeline, in which a model searches, reads and records evidence over a movie corpus while an orchestrator holds the record and rules on what may be submitted. A claim is admitted only once an entailment cascade has checked it against the passage it cites, and an answer is released only once enough checked evidence stands behind it. Each question is run three or four times, every pass retrieving from a corpus stripped of what the earlier passes have already seen. The confidence filed with each answer is computed by the orchestrator from what the run leaves behind and is never asked of the model, which is offered no way to rate itself. The two retrieval runs placed 4th and 5th of 22, pooling the passes was worth 0.0709 nDCG@20, and the gain was largest on the multi-hop and post-processing-heavy questions, where the organisers rank the pooled run top of the field. Sixteen of the 25 answer runs were built on passages these two runs supplied, 12 of them filed by other teams. HMR rewards a system whose confidence is high where it answers right and low where it answers wrong. The pipeline reached an accuracy of 0.9219, 6th of 25, while the confidence filed with those answers gave an HMR of 0.4915, 13th. A few rules crafted over those same recorded signals, with no further model call and no further retrieval, raise that to an accuracy of 0.9375, 5th, and an HMR of 0.6985, 9th. Ranking on HMR alone can reward a system for answering wrongly with low confidence, so we propose accHMR, the accuracy multiplied by HMR, which reports the reward in proportion to the accuracy, and on which the revised rules would have scored 0.6549, 5th. For future work, fitting a model on the numbers the pipeline already produces, rather than writing such rules by hand, would be a real step forward.

</details>


### [370] [Auditable Long-Term Memory: A Deterministic Retrieval Chain Measured at 479/475 of 500 on LongMemEval-S](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38021)
*Christopher J. Chanhnourack*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文评估一个可审计的长期记忆系统在LongMemEval-S上的表现，检索链能高覆盖率放置金标准会话并生成完整包，但头条成绩落在已发表基线附近，因读者版本、评分提示、数据版本与系统内方差等因素，既不能证明优越也不能证明等价；同时缺少留出评估与独立人工裁决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期记忆评测中，需要可审计、可复核的系统与结果。作者希望检验其检索链与推理脚手架能否在LongMemEval-S上取得有竞争力的成绩，并明确区分"系统能力"与"读者/评分/数据差异"带来的影响，因此对评测过程中的可比性与结论边界进行了严格披露。

Method: 检索链采用混合候选检索、交叉编码器重排、覆盖率优先的包编译以及确定性推理脚手架，LLM仅作为可替换的最终阅读器。使用Claude Opus通过未固定版本的CLI别名作为阅读器进行两次500题评测，另用grok-4.6-high及最大推理努力度的智能体变体作对照；第二评判者与官方评判者并行评分，并加入负对照验证器测试。

Result: 候选池在468/470可答题中覆盖全部金标准会话，生成金完整包462/470。Claude Opus阅读器两轮得分为479/500与475/500（GPT-4o评分）；grok-4.6-high为476/474，最大推理努力度智能体变体下降至461/465。第二轮评判者与头条评判者一致率为493/500（98.6%），两轮均得472/500；官方评判者对字节相同的首轮答案重评时也有三处判决翻转。负对照拒绝了一个修正3个错误草稿却破坏11个正确草稿的验证器。

Conclusion: 所有组件均在相同的500题上开发，无留出评估或独立人工裁决；成绩与Chronos High的478/500相当但受读者版本、评分提示与数据版本差异及系统内方差影响，无法判定优劣或等价。作者公开物化包、脚手架、阅读器输出、评判判决与对照，便于检验与重评分，同时如实指出头条阅读器获得额外操作者上下文、完整请求未保留、MCP工具可用性未明等限制。

Abstract: We evaluate an auditable long-term memory system on LongMemEval-S. Its retrieval chain uses hybrid candidate retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, coverage-first packet compilation, and deterministic reasoning scaffolds; an LLM is used only as a replaceable final reader. The chain places all gold sessions in the candidate pool for 468/470 answerable questions and produces gold-complete packets for 462/470. With a Claude Opus reader called through an unpinned CLI alias, two 500-question passes score 479/500 and 475/500 under GPT-4o. The 72 answerable knowledge-update rows used a substantively modified scoring prompt whose effect under the official text has not been measured. The pair straddles Chronos High's published 478/500; differences in reader generation, scoring prompt, and possibly data version, plus within-system variance, establish neither superiority nor equivalence. A grok-4.6-high reader on the same packets scores 476/474, while a maximum-reasoning-effort agentic variant regresses to 461/465. The headline passes differ on eight verdict-flip rows. A second judge agrees with the headline judge on 493/500 rows (98.6%) in each pass and scores both passes 472/500; the official judge also flips three verdicts when re-scoring byte-identical pass-1 answers. Negative controls rejected a verifier that repaired three wrong drafts but broke eleven correct drafts. All components were developed on the same 500 questions, with no held-out evaluation or independent human adjudication; retrieval and scaffold method sources and transcript-derived audits are held; and the headline reader received extra operator context, its complete requests were not retained, and MCP tool availability is unresolved. We release materialized packets, scaffolds, reader outputs, judge verdicts, and controls for inspection and re-scoring.

</details>


### [371] [Layer-Informed Fine-Tuning via Three-Stage Functional Segmentation of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38027)
*Junning Shao,Siwei Wang,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出大语言模型层间存在"概念化-推理-文本化"的结构化分工假设，并据此设计瓶颈识别机制与分层微调方法LIFT，仅更新关键功能层即可实现高效且高性能的微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在推理任务上表现优异甚至超越人类，但学术界对其结构和内部参数如何逐步求解复杂推理问题缺乏清晰理解。作者希望揭示LLM推理过程的层级分工规律，并据此提升微调效率与效果。

Method: 研究LLM在跨语言材料上的推理过程，提出LLM各层在概念化、推理、文本化三阶段存在结构化分工的假设；基于该假设，利用敏感性分析引入瓶颈识别机制，定位特定任务最关键的功能阶段；进而提出Layer-Informed Fine-Tuning（LIFT），仅选择性地更新这些功能关键层进行微调。

Result: 大量实验表明，LIFT方法不仅加速了训练过程，还显著提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: LLM的层级结构存在功能分工，通过识别并针对性更新关键功能层，可在提升微调效率的同时改善模型性能，为理解与优化LLM推理提供了新视角和方法。

Abstract: In recent years, the performance of large language models (LLMs) on reasoning tasks has been remarkable, even surpassing human capabilities on various benchmarks. However, there remains a lack of clear understanding in the academic community regarding how the structure and internal parameters of LLMs progressively solve complex reasoning problems. In this study, we investigate the inference process of LLMs on cross-linguistic materials and propose the hypothesis that LLM layers exhibit a structured division of labor across conceptualization, reasoning, and textualization. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce a bottleneck identification mechanism using sensitivity analysis to pinpoint the most critical functional stage for a specific task. Leveraging this insight, we propose a novel approach, Layer-Informed Fine-Tuning (LIFT), which achieves efficient and effective fine-tuning by selectively updating only these functionally critical layers. We then conduct extensive experiments to show that the LIFT method not only accelerates the training process but also significantly improves model performance.

</details>


### [372] [Gender bias across LLMs is common and highly heterogenous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38036)
*Edoardo Bolzoni,Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究横向评估10个大语言模型（来自9家厂商，2025年4月至2026年6月发布）的性别偏见，发现偏见普遍存在但方向与强度高度异质，甚至模型间表现完全相反，主张偏见审计应作为持续的多厂商流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益嵌入具有现实后果的决策支持工具，理解其性别偏见愈发重要；但既有研究仅覆盖少量模型，尚不清楚性别偏见在模型间的普遍性与异质性程度。

Method: 覆盖9家厂商、2025年4月至2026年6月发布的10个模型，采用两种范式：研究1为对刻板印象短语的性别归属任务；研究2为在"防止灾难性后果"情境下对伤害女性或男性（虐待或酷刑）的道德判断任务。

Result: 研究1中，10个模型里有2个更常把男性刻板印象短语归属于女性作者（反之亦然），另3个模型呈现相反模式；研究2中，若干模型表现出不利于男性的不对称性，方向与人类保护女性免受伤害的倾向一致，但出现该不对称的具体条件因模型而异，另有3个模型在各条件间无差异。

Conclusion: 性别相关偏见在大语言模型中普遍存在，但其方向与幅度高度异质，部分模型表现甚至截然相反；因此偏见审计应被视为持续进行、跨多厂商的过程，而非一次性评估。

Abstract: Understanding gender biases in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly important as these systems become embedded in decision-support tools with real consequences. Prior research has focused only on a small set of models, leaving open the extent to which gender biases are common and heterogeneous across LLMs. We address this gap across ten models released between April 2025 and June 2026, spanning nine vendors, using two paradigms: gender attribution to stereotyped phrases (Study 1) and moral judgment of abuse or torture against a woman or a man to prevent a catastrophic outcome (Study 2). In Study 1, two of ten models attributed masculine-stereotyped phrases to female writers more often than the reverse, while three models showed the opposite pattern. In Study 2, several models converged on a male-disadvantaging asymmetry that was directionally consistent with a documented human tendency to protect female targets from harm, though the specific conditions under which this asymmetry emerged varied by model; three other models, by contrast, showed no variation across conditions. These results indicate that gender-related biases are common in LLMs. Their direction and magnitude, however, are highly heterogeneous, to the point that some models behave in diametrically opposite ways to others. Bias auditing should therefore be treated as an ongoing, multi-vendor process, rather than a one-time assessment.

</details>


### [373] [Correct Answers, Invalid Traces: What Verifiable Grade-School Math Reveals About Chain-of-Thought Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38107)
*Ratish Puduppully,Pranabendu Misra,Paarth Iyer,Durgesh Kalwar,Vardhan Palod,Subbarao Kambhampati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在iGSM合成数学基准上，通过程序化逐步验证思维链，发现答案正确与推理有效仅在分布内几乎一致，分布外会解耦；训练监督干预（非最小轨迹、乱序、替换）显示轨迹有效性与答案正确性可相互分离，削弱了思维链作为选择性规划证据的解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链被广泛解读为模型得出答案过程的真实记录，用于调试、智能体审计与推理主张。但由于自然语言思维轨迹难以机械验证，这一解读缺乏严格检验。iGSM合成基准暴露了正确解法所需的确切量与依赖关系，为程序化逐步校验轨迹提供了可能。

Method: 在iGSM基准上对生成的思维链做逐步程序化验证，检验答案正确是否伴随有效轨迹。先评估仅在有效、最小轨迹上训练的模型；再对轨迹监督进行干预：使用非最小训练轨迹、以不同查询重问同一问题、对10%训练轨迹句子做token乱序、以及替换训练轨迹。

Result: 仅在有效最小轨迹上训练时，答案正确率与轨迹有效性分布内近乎重合，但分布外解耦：最难实例上31.6%的正确答案轨迹无效，其中过半通过全部语法与算术检查却未通过语义依赖检查。非最小训练轨迹导致非最小输出；以不同查询重问会暴露继承自原查询的计算，削弱最小性作为选择性规划证据。10%训练轨迹句子token乱序后，分布外仍保持接近纯净的准确率，尽管无轨迹通过验证；替换训练轨迹在分布内同样保持高准确率。

Conclusion: 思维链的有效性并非答案正确性的可靠标志：二者可在分布外解耦，且答案正确性可在轨迹完全无效的情况下维持。这对思维链监控与解释及AI安全语境下的应用具有重要含义。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought traces are widely read as records of how models reach their answers, informing debugging, agent auditing, and claims about reasoning. Testing this interpretation is difficult because natural-language thinking traces are rarely mechanically verifiable. We revisit it in iGSM, a synthetic grade-school mathematics benchmark designed to study thinking traces and used to support claims of learned reasoning and planning. Crucially, iGSM exposes the exact quantities and dependencies that a correct solution should use, allowing generated traces to be checked programmatically step by step and enabling us to test whether correct answers are reliably accompanied by valid traces. We first evaluate models trained exclusively on valid, minimal traces. Answer correctness and trace validity nearly coincide in distribution but decouple out of distribution: on the hardest instances, 31.6% of correct answers have invalid traces, over half of which pass all syntactic and arithmetic checks but fail semantic dependency checks. We then intervene on trace supervision. Non-minimal training traces induce non-minimal outputs, while re-asking the same problem with a different query reveals computations inherited from the original query, weakening minimality as evidence of selective planning. Shuffling tokens in 10% of training trace sentences preserves near-clean accuracy even out of distribution despite no trace passing verification. Swapped training traces likewise retain high in-distribution accuracy. We discuss the implications of these findings for chain-of-thought monitoring and interpretation in the context of AI safety.

</details>


### [374] [How Local Mixing Encodes Relative Position in Global NoPE Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38109)
*Cutter Dawes,Nick Alonso,Tom Figliolia,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文解释了混合模型为何能在全局无位置编码（NoPE）注意力层中隐式编码位置：SWA与门控线性注意力在残差流中引入近因偏差，该偏差传播并被全局注意力logits选择，且相比仅靠因果掩码的纯全局NoPE模型，这种近因偏差能在长序列中保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力操作本身是位置不变的，而位置信息对自然语言至关重要，因此传统上认为必须显式加入位置编码（如RoPE）。但近期一些混合模型（交错使用SWA、门控线性注意力等局部混合层，并在全局注意力层不编码位置）在大规模下表现成功。然而，这种方式为何有效、如何工作尚缺乏清晰理解。

Method: 作者提出一种理论解释，并结合理论与实证证据来论证：局部混合层（SWA与门控线性注意力）会在残差流中诱导出近因偏差（recency bias），该偏差传播到全局注意力层并被其logits所选择，从而实现隐式的位置编码。同时对比了纯全局NoPE注意力的情形。

Result: 理论与实证均支持核心论点：混合模型中的近因偏差可由全局注意力logits选择；与仅依赖因果掩码的纯全局NoPE注意力不同，混合模型中的近因偏差能够在长序列上得以维持。

Conclusion: 研究加深了对混合模型如何编码位置的理解，并可能为设计可无限外推到更长序列长度的位置编码方式提供启示。

Abstract: The attention operation is naively position invariant. However, positional information is fundamental to natural language, and therefore a variety of explicit position encodings have been developed in transformer-based models, such as rotary position encoding (RoPE). Although explicit position encodings have long been assumed to be required, recent methods that interleave local mixing layers, such as sliding window attention (SWA) and gated linear attention, while not encoding position (NoPE) in global attention layers has recently been shown to be successful at scale. How and why this approach works is not well-understood. In this paper, we develop an explanation of how hybrid models of this sort can implicitly encode position at global NoPE layers. Supported by both theoretical and empirical evidence, our central argument is that SWA and gated linear attention induce a recency bias in the residual stream that propagates to, and is selected by, the global attention logits. Moreover, in contrast to the implicit position encodings found in models with only global NoPE attention, in which positional information arises solely from the causal mask, the recency bias in hybrid models can be maintained across long sequences. In addition to deepening our understanding of how hybrid models encode position, these findings may provide insights for how to encode position in a way that can extrapolate to longer sequence lengths indefinitely.

</details>


### [375] [From Routing Signals to Selective Review: Visual regrounding in MoE VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38111)
*Hongzhu Guo,Mohsen Fayyaz,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个利用混合专家（MoE）视觉语言模型内部路由决策来检测目标缺失的框架，在生成前即可识别假视觉前提，并通过选择性纠错提示提升端到端准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型面对目标缺失的假视觉前提时，仍会回答关于颜色、数量、位置或状态的问题，这一目标缺失接地失败严重影响可靠性。现有接地检测器主要依赖生成响应、隐藏状态或不确定性度量，缺乏利用模型内部机制进行早期检测的手段。

Method: 从 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct 与 Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it 中提取目标 token 的路由概率，为每个模型训练独立的 L2 正则化线性检测器，并基于其预测选择性调用目标感知的复查提示，全程不修改模型权重。

Result: 仅用路由信息，Qwen 与 Gemma 检测器在 GQA-Inpaint 上 ROC-AUC 分别达 0.9988 与 0.9956，在 OBER 上保持 0.8095 与 0.7781。路由门控策略使 Qwen 端到端准确率提升 +22.25% 与 +12.17%，Gemma 提升 +13.42% 与 +1.39%。

Conclusion: 该信号局限于目标物体 token、在早期 MoE 层出现并分布于部分可替代专家中。跨数据集阈值需重新校准，但假阳性复查危害有限，可通过联合选择阈值与提示控制干预风险。路由概率本身即保留了视觉感知的可操作信息，支持低成本检测与选择性视觉重接地。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) may accept false visual premises, answering questions about a target object's color, count, location, or state even when it is absent. We call this reliability-critical behavior a target-absence grounding failure. Existing visual-grounding detectors primarily rely on generated responses, hidden states, or uncertainty measures. We present the first framework to leverage internal routing decisions in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) VLMs to detect target absence before generation and guide selective correction. We extract target-token routing probabilities from Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct and Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it, train a separate L2-regularized linear detector for each model, and use its predictions to selectively invoke a target-aware review prompt. Using routing alone, the Qwen and Gemma detectors achieve ROC-AUCs of 0.9988 and 0.9956 on GQA-Inpaint and retain 0.8095 and 0.7781 on the external OBER dataset, respectively. The resulting routing-gated policy improves end-to-end accuracy on GQA-Inpaint and OBER by +22.25% and +12.17% for Qwen, and by +13.42% and +1.39% for Gemma, without modifying model weights. Further analysis shows that the signal is localized to the target-object token, emerges in early MoE layers, and is distributed across partially substitutable experts. Although cross-dataset threshold shifts require recalibration, false-positive review causes limited harm overall, suggesting that intervention risk can be controlled through joint selection of the detector threshold and review prompt. Overall, we show that routing probabilities alone preserve actionable information about visual perception, allowing computation already produced by an MoE VLM to support low-cost detection and selective visual regrounding.

</details>


### [376] [LongHarness Bench: Stress-Testing Language Model Harnesses for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38137)
*Quang Hieu Pham,Thuy Duong Nguyen,Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个同时评估长上下文harness有效性与效率的新基准，任务需要多样检索策略与自适应推理，结果显示最佳组合仅达68%宏平均准确率，且同一模型在不同harness下效率差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文评估无法区分现代harness：不同harness准确率趋于饱和且评估成本相似，缺少对有效性和效率的双重衡量。

Method: 构建包含四个评估套件的基准，任务要求词汇检索与语义匹配等多样检索策略，以及全局与局部上下文上的策略性自适应推理；大部分上下文语义相关但每步仅少量有用，形成困难搜索问题与不同准确率-成本权衡。以多个前沿语言模型家族配合四种最先进harness进行评测。

Result: 基准对强模型-harness组合仍具挑战性，最佳仅达68%宏平均准确率；同一底层模型在不同harness下效率差异显著。

Conclusion: 效率应成为长上下文评估的重要维度，该基准为开发策略性而非穷尽式处理上下文的harness提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Language-model (LM) harnesses enable LMs to operate effectively over long contexts using additional compute. However, existing long-context evaluations are insufficient for distinguishing modern harnesses, reflected by saturated accuracy across harnesses and largely similar evaluation costs. In this paper, we introduce a benchmark for evaluating both the effectiveness and efficiency of long-context harnesses. Our tasks require diverse retrieval strategies, including lexical search and semantic matching, together with strategic and adaptive reasoning over global and local context. Much of the context is semantically relevant but only a small subset is useful at each step, creating both a challenging search problem and different accuracy--cost tradeoffs across processing strategies. For example, one task requires identifying every person satisfying several conditions using evidence scattered across documents; strategically checking the most selective condition first can narrow the search before verifying the remaining conditions. We evaluate multiple families of frontier language models with four state-of-the-art harnesses. Our benchmarks remain challenging even for strong model--harness combinations: the best reaches 68\% macro-average accuracy across four evaluation suites. More importantly, we find that the same underlying model can exhibit markedly different efficiency under different harnesses. Our results establish efficiency as an important axis for long-context evaluation and provide a testbed for developing harnesses that process context strategically rather than exhaustively.

</details>


### [377] [Pretraining Latent Information Feedback Transformers with Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38149)
*Dor Tirosh,Ido Amos,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIFT 架构与训练方法，在预训练阶段引入深层到浅层的信息反馈，让语言模型跨生成步传播隐状态；实验表明其在语言建模、推理与过程性任务上优于标准 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 语言模型是前馈结构：深层表示无法回流到浅层，跨生成步的信息只能通过解码出的 token 传递。这一狭窄通道迫使模型重复计算中间结果，并丢弃其他可能的续写路径。

Method: 将循环状态学习转化为 teacher-forced 预测问题：每个输入 token 配对一个信息密集的状态，该状态来自现成预训练 LM 的下一 token 分布。模型增加少量参数，同时训练预测下一 token 和下一状态。由于输入状态可预计算，预训练仍可全并行。推理时将模型自身预测的状态反馈回去，计算开销较小且随模型规模增大而降低。

Result: 在 135M 到 1B 参数的预训练模型上，LIFT 在 token 匹配预算下于语言建模、下游推理任务和过程性任务上持续优于标准 Transformer 及基线，与计算匹配的 Transformer 相当或更优。状态追踪任务的对照实验显示，小型 LIFT 优于用 8 倍数据训练的同等规模 Transformer，即使其监督状态来自一个无法完成该任务的 Transformer。

Conclusion: 语言模型可以通过可扩展的教师监督，在预训练中学会利用深层到浅层的反馈信息。

Abstract: Transformer language models (LMs) are feed-forward: deep-layer representations are never fed back to shallower layers, and the only pathway for information to flow downward across generation steps is the decoded token. This narrow channel forces models to recompute intermediate results and to discard alternative continuations. In this work, we remove this bottleneck during pretraining, introducing the LIFT (Latent Information Feedback Transformer) architecture and training method which enable LMs to propagate state across generation. We achieve this by turning recurrent-state learning into a teacher-forced prediction problem: each input token is paired with an information-dense state, derived from the next-token distribution of an off-the-shelf pretrained LM. The model, extended with a small number of additional parameters, is then trained to predict both the next token and the next state. As the input states are precomputed, pretraining remains fully parallel across positions. At inference, the model's own predicted states are fed back, with a minor computational overhead that decreases with model size. Experiments with pretrained models ranging from 135M to 1B parameters show that LIFT consistently outperforms standard Transformers and baselines on language modeling, downstream reasoning tasks, and procedural tasks under token-matched budget, while being on par with or ahead of compute-matched Transformers. Moreover, a controlled study on a state-tracking task shows that a tiny LIFT outperforms same-size Transformers trained on 8x more data, even when trained with the states of a Transformer that fails the task. Overall, we show that LMs can learn to exploit deep-to-shallow feedback during pretraining via scalable teacher supervision.

</details>


### [378] [STEPQuant: When and Where Errors Matter in Delta-Rule Recurrent State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38169)
*Bingchen Yao,Haobo Xu,Haokun Lin,Yichen Wu,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Zhichao Lu,Zhenan Sun,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 STEPQuant，一种面向 Delta-rule 循环状态的空间-时间训练后量化框架，按误差幅度与记忆寿命分配精度，6-bit 下精度接近 FP32 且服务内存最多降低 68.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力用固定大小的循环状态替代不断增长的 KV 缓存，但在并发服务场景下这些持久状态会成为显著的内存瓶颈。直接将循环状态量化到低精度会导致严重精度下降，因为量化误差会沿连续的状态更新传播。

Method: 作者发现误差影响存在于两个互补维度：时间上，长寿命记忆中的误差会持续影响许多解码步；空间上，不同 key 行对输出影响不同，且状态幅度沿行列均有显著差异。基于此，STEPQuant 按误差幅度和记忆寿命分配精度，并依据状态分布与 key 行对输出误差的影响，联合拟合 key 行尺度与 value 列尺度。

Result: 在 Qwen3.8-27B 和 Kimi-Linear-48B-A3B-Instruct 上，跨长、短生成基准测试显示：名义 6-bit 预算下 STEPQuant 精度接近 FP32 状态，其 4-bit 配置优于均匀 INT8。集成到 SGLang 并配合优化 GPU kernel 后，6-bit STEPQuant 实现超过 5 倍循环状态压缩，服务总内存最多减少 68.7%。

Conclusion: STEPQuant 通过空间-时间感知的训练后量化有效缓解了循环状态量化的精度退化问题，在保持精度的同时大幅压缩状态与服务内存，具备实际部署价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces growing KV caches with fixed-size recurrent states, yet these persistent states can become a substantial memory bottleneck under concurrent serving. Directly quantizing recurrent states to low precision often leads to severe accuracy degradation, as quantization errors propagate through successive state updates. We discover that the impact of these errors depends on two complementary dimensions: temporally, errors in long-lived memory can persist across many decoding steps; spatially, errors in different key rows affect model outputs differently, while state magnitudes vary substantially along both rows and columns. Motivated by these observations, we propose STEPQuant, a spatial-temporal post-training quantization framework for Delta-rule recurrent states. STEPQuant allocates precision according to error magnitude and memory lifetime, and jointly fits key-row and value-column scales based on state distributions and key-row impact on output error. Experiments on Qwen3.8-27B and Kimi-Linear-48B-A3B-Instruct across both long- and short-generation benchmarks show that STEPQuant closely matches FP32-state accuracy under a nominal 6-bit budget and outperforms uniform INT8 in its 4-bit configuration. Integrated into SGLang with optimized GPU kernels, 6-bit STEPQuant achieves over 5x recurrent-state compression and reduces total serving memory by up to 68.7%. Our code is available at https://github.com/Dreamer-Toby/STEPQuant.

</details>
